## gov_storm_chasers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agov_storm_chasers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

统治议会中那些最为杰出的议员们擅长那些惊心动魄的体能考验。他们攀登最高的山峰，踩上最大的海浪，将一生都投入到追求这些原始的考验之中，并藉此获取大家的信任，以及引领众人前进的权利。\n\n无论青壮老幼，议会成员统一被称作长老，因为他们的事迹已然成为传奇，他们的名字也将流芳百世。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

The members of the most excellent ruling council excel at death-defying acts of physical prowess. By scaling the highest peak or riding the largest wave, they dedicate their lives to the unwavering pursuit of raw experience. This has earned them the trust of their community, and the right to chart a course forward.\n\nCouncil members are called Elders regardless of age, because their deeds have been deemed legendary. Long will their names be remembered.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:21`

## ap_weather_control_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_weather_control_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在星际气象学方面的最新进展使我们有能力根据命令呈现宇宙风暴。一旦有了合适的技术，我们甚至可以选择要制造的风暴类型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

Our latest advances in astrometeorology grant us the ability to manifest Cosmic Storms on command. With the right technology, we can even choose the type of storm to create.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:27`

## celestial_storm_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acelestial_storm_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

—$celestial_storm$会降低星系中的§Y$intel$§!，使得舰队可以通过被关闭的边境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:32`

### English

## electric_storm_fleet_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_storm_fleet_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

—$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_REGEN_ADD_PERC$对舰船和空间站§R禁用§!。\n—舰船和空间站每月遭受§R$@storm_monthly_dmg_low|0$§!$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_ADD$伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

- $MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_REGEN_ADD_PERC$ is §Rdisabled§! for ships and stations.\n- Inflicts §R$@storm_monthly_dmg_low|0$§! $MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_ADD$ Damage per month to ships and stations.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:40`

## particle_storm_fleet_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparticle_storm_fleet_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

—$MOD_SHIP_HULL_REGEN_ADD_PERC$、$MOD_SHIP_ARMOR_REGEN_ADD_PERC$和$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_REGEN_ADD_PERC$对舰船和空间站§R禁用§!。\n—拥有$@storm_hp_perc_limit|0Y%$$HITPOINTS$的舰船和空间站每月遭受§R$@storm_monthly_dmg_low|0$§!伤害。\n—$@storm_hp_perc_limit|0Y%$$HITPOINTS$以下的舰船产出§G+$@particle_storm_unity_per_ship|0$§!£unity£$unity$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:35`

### English

- $MOD_SHIP_HULL_REGEN_ADD_PERC$, $MOD_SHIP_ARMOR_REGEN_ADD_PERC$ and $MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_REGEN_ADD_PERC$ are §Rdisabled§! for ships and stations.\n- Inflicts §R$@storm_monthly_dmg_low|0$§! Damage per month to ships and stations with more than $@storm_hp_perc_limit|0Y%$ $HITPOINTS$.\n- Gain §G+$@particle_storm_unity_per_ship|0$§! £unity£ $unity$ from Ships at $@storm_hp_perc_limit|0Y%$ $HITPOINTS$ or less.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:41`

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:42`

## tech_storm_manipulation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_storm_manipulation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用风暴否定技术背后的原理，我们可以开始操纵星系的星际气象性质来满足我们自己的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

By harnessing the principles behind storm negation technologies, we can begin to manipulate the astrometeorological nature of the galaxy to serve our own needs.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:73`

## tech_advanced_storm_manipulation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_advanced_storm_manipulation&lang=en)

### 中文

先进风暴操控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

Advanced Storm Manipulation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:74`

## tech_advanced_storm_manipulation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_advanced_storm_manipulation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对星际气象学的持续研究使我们有能力更精确地扭转和操纵§Y宇宙风暴§!以满足我们自己的需要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

Continued study into astrometeorology grants us the power to more precisely bend and manipulate §YCosmic Storms§! to our own needs.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:75`

## tech_industrial_storm_protection

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_industrial_storm_protection&lang=en)

### 中文

行星风暴抵御

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

Planetary Storm Negation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:76`

## tech_industrial_storm_protection_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_industrial_storm_protection_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种天气通讯枢纽的可以减轻宇宙天气灾难造成的危害。过境的星系风暴对生产的影响将会降低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

This nexus of weather communication mitigates the harm done by cosmic weather disasters. Production will suffer less from a passing galactic tempest.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:77`

## tech_advanced_industrial_storm_protection

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_advanced_industrial_storm_protection&lang=en)

### 中文

行星风暴消除

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

Planetary Storm Nullification

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:78`

## tech_advanced_industrial_storm_protection_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_advanced_industrial_storm_protection_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过强化区域天气控制系统，调控行星天气模式，可以显著降低宇宙风暴对生产的大部分负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

By consolidating local weather control systems, planetary weather patterns can be manipulated to significantly reduce most of the cosmic storms' harmful effects on production.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:79`

通过使用科学的天气模型，我们可以追踪即将到来的§Y宇宙风暴§!的运动和形成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

## cstorms.505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[this.GetAffectedStormType]§!带来破坏的余波中，我们取得了许多对这些宇宙自然现象的洞见。\n\n通过研究星际气象学，我们可以在未来更好地保护自己。这一全新的研究领域可能将会得出完全驾驭这些风暴的手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

In the aftermath of the devastation from the §H[this.GetAffectedStormType]§!, we have gained some unique insight on these galactic phenomena.\n\nBy studying astrometeorology, we can better protect ourselves in the future. This new field of research might eventually yield the means to master these storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:176`

## building_storm_attraction_center

### English

Storm Attraction Center

## building_storm_attraction_center_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_storm_attraction_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些设施可以通过小心地调控吸引力来吸引附近的宇宙风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Through the careful manipulation of gravitational forces, these facilities attract nearby cosmic storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:180`

## building_advanced_storm_attraction_center

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_attraction_center&lang=en)

先进风暴吸引中心

### English

Advanced Storm Attraction Center

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:182`

## building_advanced_storm_attraction_center_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_attraction_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在深入理解暗能量之后，我们得以将每座$building_storm_attraction_center$进行超载，以更高的效率吸引宇宙风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Advanced insights into dark energy allow us to supercharge each $building_storm_attraction_center$ and draw in cosmic storms even more efficiently.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:183`

## building_storm_repellent_center

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_storm_repellent_center&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴驱斥中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Storm Repulsion Center

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:185`

## building_storm_repellent_center_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_storm_repellent_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴越远，我们的人民越安全，而且这样他们就不用一直提心吊胆。这些复杂的星际气象核心仪器能够驱离逼近的风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

The further the storm, the safer our population - and the easier their minds. The complex astrometeorological instrumentation of these centers repels approaching storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:186`

## building_advanced_storm_repellent_center

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_repellent_center&lang=en)

### 中文

先进风暴驱斥中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Advanced Storm Repulsion Center

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:187`

## building_advanced_storm_repellent_center_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_repellent_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要能够保护我们的星球免遭宇宙风暴的侵袭，哪怕操控时空都是合理的。该核心装置的功能就在于此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

Any manipulation of spacetime is warranted if it keeps our world clear from cosmic tempests. This center does exactly that.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:188`

## building_storm_resistant_production

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_storm_resistant_production&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴救援中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Storm Relief Center

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:190`

## building_storm_resistant_production_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_storm_resistant_production_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个救援中心帮助当地居民消除宇宙风暴对殖民地生产的影响。通过高效沟通协作，$building_storm_resistant_production$快速部署救援小队，改善行星贸易环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

This aid center assists local efforts to negate the impact of a cosmic storm on colony production. Through efficient coordination and communication, the $building_storm_resistant_production$ allows for quick deployment of rescue teams, as well as improving the conditions for planet-wide trade.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:191`

## building_storm_resistant_production_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_storm_resistant_production_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$default_storm$§!对殖民地生产的负面惩罚减少§G30%§!。\n\n当§Y$default_storm$§!出现时：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_miners_minerals_produces_add$：§G+0.5§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_farmers_food_produces_add$：§G+0.5§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_technician_energy_produces_add$：§G+0.5§!\n\n解锁殖民地决议§Y$decision_hunker_down$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

Negative effects from §Y$default_storm$§! that affect colony production are reduced by §G30%§!.\n\nWhile a §Y$default_storm$§! is present:$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_miners_minerals_produces_add$: §G+0.5§! $TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_farmers_food_produces_add$: §G+0.5§! $TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_technician_energy_produces_add$: §G+0.5§! \n\nAllows Colony Decision §Y$decision_hunker_down$§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:192`

## building_advanced_storm_resistant_production

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_resistant_production&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴抑制器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

Storm Nullifier

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:194`

## building_advanced_storm_resistant_production_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_resistant_production_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在$building_storm_resistant_production$部署天气控制系统后，宇宙风暴的负面影响大为减轻。此外，救援队伍能更高效地部署，行星贸易也几乎不再受当地天气条件的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

By adding weather control systems to each $building_storm_resistant_production$, the negative effects of cosmic storms are significantly lessened. Additionally, rescue teams can be deployed even more efficiently and planetary trade is almost impervious to local weather conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:195`

## building_advanced_storm_resistant_production_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_advanced_storm_resistant_production_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$default_storm$§!对殖民地生产的负面惩罚减少§G45%§!。\n\n当§Y$default_storm$§!出现时：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_miners_minerals_produces_add$：§G+1§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_farmers_food_produces_add$：§G+1§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_technician_energy_produces_add$：§G+1§!\n\n解锁殖民地决议§Y$decision_hunker_down$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Negative effects from §Y$default_storm$§! that affect colony production are reduced by §G45%§!.\n\nWhile a §Y$default_storm$§! is present:$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_miners_minerals_produces_add$: §G+1§! $TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_farmers_food_produces_add$: §G+1§! $TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_planet_technician_energy_produces_add$: §G+1§! \n\nAllows Colony Decision §Y$decision_hunker_down$§!

## building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_adakkaria_patriotic_institute_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$是对情感状况有着先进见解的宝库。在这里，我们让我们的人民在抵御公约残酷的诱惑之歌时能够在内心培育风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

The $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$ is a trove of advanced insights into the sentient condition. Here we empower people to cultivate their inner tempest while avoiding the luring song of the Convention's cruelty.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:199`

## building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_adakkaria_patriotic_institute_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每当宇宙风暴经过该恒星系，就有10%的概率让£pop£§H人口§!获得$Name_Storm_Fused$特质

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

If a cosmic storm passes this system, there is a 10% chance that £pop£ §HPops§! gain the $Name_Storm_Fused$ trait.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:200`

## building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute_effect_upgrade_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_adakkaria_patriotic_institute_effect_upgrade_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每当宇宙风暴经过该恒星系，就有40%的概率让£pop£§H人口§!获得$Name_Storm_Fused$特质。此外，每过10年，就有20%的可能有§Y100§!£pop£§H人口§!化为宇宙风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

If a cosmic storm passes this system, there is a 40% chance that £pop£ §HPops§! gain the $Name_Storm_Fused$ trait. Additionally, there is a 20% chance every 10 years that §Y100§! £pop£ §HPops§! are transformed into a cosmic storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:201`

## cultivated_worldscaping_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acultivated_worldscaping_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了营造庄重典雅的几何美感，这里的地形经过精心塑造，而生活功能则退居其次。园中的植物品类经过筛选，仅保留单一种类栽培，以此消除任何可能破坏整体和谐的元素。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

The landscape has been altered to create a sublime geometric experience, prioritizing form over life. Any remaining flora has been reduced to a monoculture to eliminate discordant elements.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:301`

## cultivated_worldscaping_modifier_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acultivated_worldscaping_modifier_tt&lang=en)

-最大区划数§Y+3§!\n-最大资源区划数：§G+75%§!\n-§Y$default_storm$§!防护§G+10%§!\n-宜居性§R-20%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

- Max Districts §Y+3§!\n- Max Resource Districts §G+75%§!\n- §Y$default_storm$§! Protection §G+10%§!\n- Habitability §R-20%§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:302`

## decision_hunker_down_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_hunker_down_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的居民担忧最坏的情况出现，于是采取一切措施预防宇宙风暴带来的损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Fearing the worst, the inhabitants of this planet will take any and all precautions against the effects of cosmic storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:306`

## decision_stop_hunker_down

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_stop_hunker_down&lang=en)

## decision_stop_hunker_down_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_stop_hunker_down_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

既然宇宙风暴已经离去，我们也不用一直提心吊胆了。不过一切真能恢复“正常”吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Once the danger of the cosmic storm has passed, we can relax our precautions. But will things ever truly return to 'normal'?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:308`

## decision_cultivated_worldscaping

## decision_cultivated_worldscaping_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_cultivated_worldscaping_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们将这颗星球塑造成一场完美对称的盛典吧。如此纯粹的几何地景能带来内心的宁静。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Let us sculpt this world into a celebration of perfect symmetry. To observe the pure geometry of the landscape will bring serenity.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:310`

## civic_astrometeorology

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_astrometeorology&lang=en)

### 中文

星际气象研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

Astrometeorology

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:314`

## civic_astrometeorology_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_astrometeorology_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

怀揣着对自然力量的尊重，这个种群不遗余力地研究自然气候内在的运作机制。在踏足星空之后，他们对于星际气象展现了极大的兴趣。而他们雄心壮志的核心就是探索宇宙风暴这一迷人又蕴含极大破坏力的现象。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

With deep respect for the forces of nature, this species goes to great lengths to uncover the inner workings of environmental phenomena. As they set out across the stars, they take with them a particular interest in astrometeorology. Central to their ambitions are the most fantastic and potentially destructive of all astronomical events, the cosmic storms themselves.\n

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:315`

## civic_astrometeorology_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_astrometeorology_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着这个种群踏足星空，他们的集体意识就被星际气象现象所吸引。蜂群为了一个崇高的目标而拓展疆域：研究宇宙风暴对外界环境产生的影响，并探索种种奇观。为此他们不惜亲身踏入风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

As the collective reaches for the stars, their consciousness is entranced by the sight of astrometeorological phenomena. These drones expand their home for a noble purpose: to study environmental effects and find wonder in even the cosmic storms themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:319`

## civic_astrometeorology_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_astrometeorology_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对自然力量的理解给这个机械帝国带来了深刻的影响，帝国的集体意识不遗余力地对自然现象开展研究，以神圣庄严的态度勤勤恳恳地记录着所有独特的星际气象现象。而他们研究的重点则是宇宙风暴这一最奇异且蕴含极大破坏力的现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Understanding how the forces of nature can have profound effects on a machine empire, this collective goes to great lengths to study them. Their models painstakingly detail every every curious astronomical event to an almost sacred level. Central to their focus are the most fantastic and potentially destructive of all astronomical events, the cosmic storms themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:323`

### English

## civic_astrometeorology_megacorp_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_astrometeorology_megacorp_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环境效应不是可以开发的免费资源，那还能是什么呢？这家巨型企业专门从星际气象现象中获取尽可能多的知识，并将其出售给出价最高的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

What are environmental effects if not another free resource to exploit? This megacorp specializes in acquiring as much knowledge from astrometeorological phenomena as they can and sell it to the highest bidder.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:327`

### 中文

风暴热忱

### English

Storm Devotion

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:330`

## civic_storm_callers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_storm_callers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自古以来，这个社会能够用仪式来召唤风暴。年复一年，他们不停地运用科技创新提高仪式的效率。而随着太空时代到来，他们得以将这一知识运用与于群星之间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Ever since ancient times, this society has been able to attract tempests through their rituals. Over the years, they combined technical innovations to enhance their efficiency. With the dawn of the space age, they now seek to take this knowledge to the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:331`

## civic_storm_callers_effect_tooltip

### English

## civic_storm_callers_megacorp_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_storm_callers_megacorp_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这家庞大的媒体集团起初只是一个对神圣仪式的进行简单广播的组织，后来先知和祭司的位置被魅力非凡的艺人所取代，他们都在吸引宇宙风暴这一赛道上争奇斗艳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

What started as a simple broadcast of sacred rituals, has since blossomed into a massive media conglomerate. Seers and priests were replaced by charismatic entertainers, all competing to gain a modicum of attention as they appeal to the cosmic storms themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:335`

## civic_environmental_architects_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_environmental_architects_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过亿万年精心培养其母星上的完美平衡之后，这个社会已经准备好面对宇宙中那些最不宜居的环境。人们内心坚信，自然世界可以被塑造，控制，并臻于完美。这不是驯化环境，而是为了追求完美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

After eons of carefully cultivating a perfect balance on their homeworld, this society is ready to tend to even the most inhospitable environments among the stars. At their core, these people believe nature can be shaped, controlled, and perfected. Not to tame, but in service of perfect beauty.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:343`

## civic_environmental_architects_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_environmental_architects_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为一个巨大生物环境的一份子，这些蜂群能够识别出大自然环境中潜藏的机会。为了实现集体的目标，他们试着掌控并重塑自然系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

Being a part of a great organism allows these drones to recognize opportunities inside the greatest organism of all: nature. They seek to control and shape natural systems to the collective's desires.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:351`

## civic_environmental_architects_machine_desc

### 中文

在有机先驱逝去之后，他们的机械造物仍然保持着对于审美的偏爱。自然世界就是实现编程完美主义的沃土。

## resolution_cosmic_storms_galactic_emergency_relief_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_cosmic_storms_galactic_emergency_relief_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

解锁§Y应急经费§!，每位[global_event_country.GetCommunityOrImperium]成员需要贡献§R5%§!的$r_energy$产出。\n\n[global_event_country.GetCommunityOrImperium]成员一旦被§Y$default_storm$§!影响，每有一个恒星系受灾，就能每月获得§G+3§! $r_energy$。\n\n$storm_tech_cost$§R+30%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

Unlocks the §YEmergency Fund§! which requires each member of the [global_event_country.GetCommunityOrImperium] to contribute §R5%§! of their $r_energy$ production.\n\n[global_event_country.GetCommunityOrImperium] Members affected by a §Y$default_storm$§! receives §G+3§! $r_energy$ per affected system per month.\n\n$storm_tech_cost$ §R+30%§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:491`

## resolution_cosmic_storms_storm_encouragement_mandate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_cosmic_storms_storm_encouragement_mandate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过释放重要的粒子和能量，宇宙风暴为星系注入生命，焕新它所触及的行星和系统。它们的自然流动不应受到阻碍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

By dispersing vital particles and energy, cosmic storms breathe life into the galaxy, renewing the planets and systems they touch. Their natural passage must not be hindered.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:498`

### 中文

星系是一个巨大且互相联系的系统，宇宙风暴是其中一个重要的组成部分。干涉风暴的行为将违反星际法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

The galaxy is a vast, interconnected ecosystem in which cosmic storms play a vital part. Influencing their behavior must be outlawed.

## r_weather_manipulator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_weather_manipulator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个因内特贸易体用来操纵宇宙风暴的机械的更先进版本。默认情况下该设备会增加我们所有风暴相关科技的效能，但如果使用得当的话，它甚至能将所有的风暴从我们的边境驱离出去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

A more advanced version of the machine the Inetian Traders used to manipulate cosmic storms; by default this device will increase the effectiveness of all our storm-related technology, but when properly utilized it can even drive out all storms from our borders.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:537`

## storm_relic_repelling_field_modifier_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astorm_relic_repelling_field_modifier_tt&lang=en)

## r_the_tempest_invocator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_the_tempest_invocator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

即使该装置处于关闭状态，其中蕴含的能量也是显而易见的。我们真的敢在最杰出的人才身上引发一场剧烈的风暴吗？为了帝国的荣耀，牺牲势在必行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

The waiting potential of this device is tangible even in a powered-down state. Dare we invoke tempestuous fury inside one of our best and brightest? For the glory of our nation, sacrifices must be made.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:547`

## STORMS_ANOM_ANTS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_ANTS&lang=en)

### 中文

有机彗星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

The Organic Comet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:569`

## STORMS_ANOM_ANTS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_ANTS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴经过后，出现了一颗组成成分极不寻常的彗星。它似乎是由有机材料组成的。我们的[solar_system.Owner.GetScientistPlural]迫切想要进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

A cosmic storm has left behind a comet of unusual composition. It appears to be made of organic material. Our [solar_system.Owner.GetScientistPlural] are keen to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:570`

## cstorms.5020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蚁行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

Antsteroid

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:571`

## cstorms.5020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现这颗彗星实际上是一个巨大的巢穴，里面充斥着微小的昆虫。彗尾也不是冰雾，而是巢穴内部化学反应所排放的废气。\n\n巢穴的外壳由死亡的昆虫组成，这使得内部生的物免受太空恶劣环境的影响。我们还发现了几种依附于巢穴生活的真菌。巢穴中存在一种复杂的共生关系，真菌依赖排放的废气维生，而昆虫则以真菌为食。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

The comet was revealed to be a giant nest filled with tiny insects. The tail of the comet is not outgassed ice, but rather exhaust from chemical processes occurring deep inside the hive.\n\nThe outer layer of the nest is composed of dead individuals, protecting the living inside from the harsh conditions of space. We've also picked up several types of fungi living in and around the nest. There is a complex symbiosis at work here, with the fungi living off of the exhaust gases and the insects feeding on the fungi.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:572`

## cstorms.5020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

捕获这个巢穴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Harvest the nest.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:573`

## cstorms.5020.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5020.b&lang=en)

### 中文

远远观测不要去碰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

Look but don't touch.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:574`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_STAT&lang=en)

### 中文

令人费解的组成

### English

Confusing Composition

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:576`

## STORMS_ANOM_STAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_STAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道上的这个物体是途经的宇宙风暴带来的。它看上去像是一座空间站，但是对其结构组成的扫描结果却有点令人费解。我们建议对此开展调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

This orbiting object has been left behind by a passing cosmic storm. It looks like a space station but scans of its structural composition is returning most confusing results. We recommend investigating.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:577`

## cstorms.5030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

静滞启示录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

Stationary Apocalypse

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:578`

## cstorms.5030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetScientistPluralCapital]报告称这个空间站的大部分是由常见的固定材料建成的，这些物质勉强维持着空间站不至于散架。随着我们深入这座结构，构成空间站的物质从金属合金过渡成了塑料等其他材料。\n\n在空间站的核心处，我们发现了一台机器正在高效运转，它不断地拆解有机原料并将其转化为我们之前见到的东西。\n\n在运行DNA模拟模型后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]只能得出结论，这些原料可能是空间站的前住民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

[Root.Owner.GetScientistPluralCapital] report that much of this space station is constructed from everyday stationary objects, barely holding it together. Deeper into the structure, the materials transition from metal alloys to plastics and other substances.\n\nInside the heart of the construct we found a machine, still in the process of efficiently deconstructing organic material and shaping it into the very items encountered so far.\n\nAfter running a DNA simulation model, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] have been forced to conclude that the material likely came from the former inhabitants of this outpost.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:579`

## cstorms.5030.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5030.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

看看我们能发现些什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

See if we can't gain something here.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:580`

## STORMS_ANOM_BRAIN

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_BRAIN&lang=en)

### 中文

存在主义耗散

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Existentialist Dissipation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:582`

## STORMS_ANOM_BRAIN_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_BRAIN_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

自从一场$shroud_storm$肆虐该恒星系后，我们就开始不断收到一些奇怪的信号。然而，当我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]想要回应这些呼叫时，信号又中断了。科学家们建议派出一艘科研船进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

Ever since a $shroud_storm$ raged through this system, we've been receiving strange signals. However, as soon as our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] try to answer this cacophony of hails, it dissipates again. They recommend sending a science vessel.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:583`

## cstorms.5025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

头脑风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

Brainstorm

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:584`

## cstorms.5025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们始料未及的发现：大脑集群。来自各种物种的湿糊糊的有机处理器官，成百上千个地连接在一起，悬浮在一个气球状的大型胶囊之中。它似乎已经被废弃，在近期的$shroud_storm$消散之后飘浮在深空之中。\n\n胶囊之中有一个惊人的传感器阵列连接在大脑和胶囊之上，此外还有一个通信模块，能够呼叫舰船并发送信息，之前它对我们就是这么做的。\n\n现在看来似乎是这些大脑自己联系了我们，解释了那一连串的信息。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]认为这个装置的初衷是测试有机体对$shroud_storm$的反应。考虑到这些悬浮大脑内不稳定的残留灵能，尝试与其连接可能会带来危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

We could never have anticipated our discovery: brains. Organic, squishy central processing organs from all sorts of species. Hundreds and hundreds of them, all linked together and suspended inside a large balloon-like capsule. It appears to be derelict, left floating in the depths of space after a recent $shroud_storm$ dissipated.\n\nThe capsule contains an incredible array of sensors attached to the brains and the balloon, and there is also a communication module, capable of hailing ships and transmitting information, just as it did to us.\n\nIt seems likely that the brains themselves contacted us, explaining the cacophony of messages. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] think the construction was designed to measure organic responses to a $shroud_storm$. Given the unstable psionic residue within these floating minds, attempting to interface them would only lead to disaster.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:585`

## terrestial_telescope_category

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterrestial_telescope_category&lang=en)

### 中文

天文望远镜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

Terrestrial Telescopes

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:588`

## terrestial_telescope_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterrestial_telescope_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fleet.GetName]的扫描仪显示[From.GetStarName]恒星系中漂浮着一座被废弃的空间站。\n\n看起来它是被途径的宇宙风暴卷到这里来的。里面没有探测到生命，也没有任何形式的能量信号。空间站两侧则伸出来一对巨大的望远镜。这对光学设备看起来很先进，不过其尺寸相对于空间站而言还是显得太大了。整个空间站的所展现的科技与结构对于我们而言还是比较陌生。\n\n我们可以试着重启这组望远镜，这或许能给我们带来一些有趣的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

Scans performed by the [fleet.GetName] indicate the presence of a derelict space station in the [From.GetStarName] System.\n\nIt looks like it was deposited there by a passing cosmic storm. Life signs return negative, and there are no power signals of any kind. Two massive telescopes protrude from opposite sides of the station. These optical instruments appear advanced, and far too large relative to the station's size. The technology and architecture are not yet familiar to us.\n\nWe could attempt to reactivate the telescopes, as this could yield some interesting insights.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:589`

## cstorms.1605.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1605.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天文望远镜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

Terrestrial Telescopes

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:590`

## cstorms.1605.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1605.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]着实费了一番巧劲才登上空间站并重启望远镜，终究还是没有什么能难倒他们。然而，望远镜瞄向的地方并没有什么值得特别注意的东西。\n\n我们发现空间站周围散布着一些有趣的文物，那是望远镜所拍下的清晰照片，它们被放在精美的相框中。这些照片都是关于宇宙风暴的，它们详细记录了经过该恒星系的所有风暴并且每一次都拍摄了多张照片。望远镜操作员更换变焦，从不同角度进行拍摄，甚至还艺术地倾斜镜头来展示风暴最美的景象。\n\n深入调查后，我们在这座废弃站点上的唯一发现就是这些有趣的艺术作品。我们将对它们进行归档，以便让后来人能够欣赏到这些罕见的、充满力量和美感的图像。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Boarding the station and activating the telescopes took some finessing from our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural], but it was nothing they could not handle. However, the corner of the galaxy the instruments were pointed at showed nothing of note.\n\nSome interesting artifacts were found scattered around the station: detailed images recorded from the telescopes, neatly presented in decorative frames. Every single capture is focussed on cosmic storms, with multiple prints of each storm that has passed through this system. The telescope operator used different angles, zoom levels, and even artistic tilts to show the storms in their best light.\n\nUpon deeper investigation, the artwork is the only interesting find aboard the abandoned station. We will archive them so many future generations can enjoy these images of rare power and beauty.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:591`

## STORMS_ANOM_POLYCHROMATIC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_POLYCHROMATIC&lang=en)

### 中文

多彩之星

### English

Polychromatic Star

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:593`

## STORMS_ANOM_POLYCHROMATIC_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_POLYCHROMATIC_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴过境[from.solar_system.GetName]星系时改变了该恒星的外观。在风暴的余波过后，这颗恒星的日冕层呈现出异彩纷呈的漩涡，向四面八方喷射出五光十色的耀斑。这一现象值得进一步分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

The passing of a cosmic storm through the [from.solar_system.GetName] system has transformed the visual appearance of the system's star. In the aftermath of the storm, the star's coronal layer presents as a swirl of ever-changing colors, ejecting polychromatic flares in all directions. The phenomenon warrants further analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:594`

## cstorms.5035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$STORMS_ANOM_POLYCHROMATIC$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

$STORMS_ANOM_POLYCHROMATIC$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:595`

## cstorms.5035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.leader.GetName]汇报了自[from.GetAffectedStormType]过境以来，[From.GetStarName]的聚变反应所发生的奇妙变化。\n\n显然，这个风暴引发了恒星内核某种意想不到的核反应，导致其对流区稳定地释放出[from.GetEarnedResources]。\n\n这些复杂而特殊的反应产生了我们所观察到的这些不寻常的多色闪光。无论如何，在一段时间内这的都会一直是一个值得仔细研究的来源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

[root.leader.GetName] reports fascinating changes in [From.GetStarName]'s fusion reactions since the passage of the [from.GetAffectedStormType].\n\nIt appears that the storm induced unexpected nuclear reactions within the star's core, resulting in a steady release of [from.GetEarnedResources] from its convective zones.\n\nThese complex and idiosyncratic reactions are producing the unusual polychromatic flaring we have observed. In any case, it will continue to be a source worthy of close study for some time.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:596`

## cstorms.5035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究这个新过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Study these new processes.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:597`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5035.b&lang=en)

### English

Harvest these new resources.

## STORMS_ANOM_TRIPLETS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_TRIPLETS&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星三姊妹

### English

Asteroid Triplets

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:600`

## STORMS_ANOM_TRIPLETS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_TRIPLETS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一场路过的宇宙风暴致使[from.solar_system.GetName]星系中产生了三颗新的小行星。这些天体容易发生难以解释的方向变化，因此无法预测它们的轨迹。此事需要进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

A passing cosmic storm deposited three new asteroids in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system. These celestial bodies are prone to inexplicable changes of direction, making it impossible to predict their trajectories. Further research is needed.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:601`

## cstorms.5040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$STORMS_ANOM_TRIPLETS$

### English

$STORMS_ANOM_TRIPLETS$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:602`

## cstorms.5040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.leader.GetName]发现，沉积于[from.solar_system.GetName]星系的小行星具有高度不稳定的磁性质，导致它们在恒星系中现有的磁层上发生随机的弹跳。\n\n[root.leader.GetName]通过给每个小行星施加磁场补偿器进行了稳定，使其轨道规范化并使其便于进行资源开采。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

[root.leader.GetName] discovered that the asteroids deposited in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system possess highly unstable magnetic fields, causing them to ricochet haphazardly off the system's existing magnetospheres.\n\n[root.leader.GetName] stabilized them by adding magnetic compensators to each asteroid, regulating their trajectories and thus making them amenable to resource extraction efforts.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:603`

## STORMS_ANOM_TRAVELLERS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_TRAVELLERS&lang=en)

### 中文

余波求助信号

### English

Aftermath Distress Signal

## STORMS_ANOM_TRAVELLERS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_ANOM_TRAVELLERS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!的余波中，从§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系上侦测到了一个未识别的求助信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

In the aftermath of the §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§! an unidentified distress signal has been detected in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:606`

## cstorms.5050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

偏航旅者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Displaced Travelers

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:607`

## cstorms.5050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经确定了§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中遇险信号的来源，那一艘严重受损但仍在运作的商船。\n\n乘员声称他们被§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!撞离了航线很远。风暴也烧毁了他们的引擎，让飞船和里面的东西滞留在我们的空间里。\n\n如果我们能帮忙的话，他们愿意交出一部分货物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

We have identified the source of the distress signal in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system as a heavily damaged but still functioning mercantile vessel.\n\nThe occupants claim they have been knocked a great distance off course by the §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!. The storm has also burnt out their engines, leaving the ship and its contents stranded in our space.\n\nThey are willing to part with a portion of their cargo if we can help.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:608`

## cstorms.5050.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5050.a&lang=en)

同意帮助他们。

### English

### English

Cosmic Sludge

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:615`

## STORMS_COSMIC_SLUDGE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_COSMIC_SLUDGE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!的余波中，监测§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!的质量探测器得到了异常读数。\n\n系统性诊断没有发现设备存在技术故障，所以这些读数需要进行进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

Mass sensors monitoring §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! are showing erratic readings in the aftermath of the §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!.\n\nSystem diagnostics show no sign of a technical error with the equipment. These readings require further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:616`

## cstorms.5060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$STORMS_COSMIC_SLUDGE$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

$STORMS_COSMIC_SLUDGE$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:617`

## cstorms.5060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系内的异常读数源自一片由§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!的余波所残留下的不寻常的碎片阵列。\n\n这个漂浮的实体是由一团无定形的宇宙污泥在风暴的极端压力下锻造而成。它的无数组成元素形成了一种独特的活性化合物。\n\n对其化学成分的深度研究可以对产生它的条件提供有价值的星际气象学洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

The erratic sensor readings in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system are derived from an exceptional debris formation left in the wake of the §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!.\n\nThis drifting entity is an amorphous blob of cosmic sludge, forged in the extreme pressures of the storm. Its innumerable constituent elements have formed a uniquely reactive compound.\n\nFocused study of its chemistry could yield valuable astrometeorological insights into the conditions that produced it.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:618`

## cstorms.5060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究它的成分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Study its composition.

### English

Break it down.

### English

## STORMS_SHATTERED_SATTELITE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_SHATTERED_SATTELITE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!的余波中，§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系内探测到了一个不明物体。对该碎片的进一步研究可以提供宝贵的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

In the aftermath of the §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!, an unidentified object has been detected in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system. Further analysis of this debris could provide valuable insights.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:623`

## cstorms.5070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$STORMS_SHATTERED_SATTELITE$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

$STORMS_SHATTERED_SATTELITE$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:624`

## cstorms.5070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中的物体是一颗古老人造卫星的残骸，上面装有复杂的星际气象探测阵列。我们认为它是伴随着§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!横跨星系漂星过海而来的。\n\n它已严重损坏无法维修，但中央数据库包含了大量的信息贮藏可以为我们所用。\n\n卫星的其余部分就只能作为废品进行打捞了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

The object in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system is the wreckage of an ancient satellite equipped with complex astrometeorological sensor arrays. We believe that it was swept a great distance across the galaxy in the wake of the §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!.\n\nNow damaged beyond repair, its central databases contain a vast store of information that we can repurpose for our own needs.\n\nThe rest of the satellite is only salvageable as scrap.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:625`

## STORMS_EVISCERATED_FAUNA

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_EVISCERATED_FAUNA&lang=en)

### 中文

异物

### English

## STORMS_EVISCERATED_FAUNA_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_EVISCERATED_FAUNA_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中的探测器发现一个近期被§H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!带进这个恒星系的不明物体。\n\n需要近距离调查以验证该异常物体的本质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Sensors in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system have detected an unknown object brought into the system by the recent §H[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!.\n\nCloser inspection is needed to verify the nature of the anomaly.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:628`

## cstorms.5080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有机残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Organic Debris

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:629`

## cstorms.5080.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5080.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现，§H[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系的怪异物体其实是一大团来自某种太空生物的有机残留物。由于所有可供识别的特征都被宇宙风暴撕碎了，所以无法对这些生物碎片进行准确分类。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

The foreign object in the §H[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system was discovered to be a large mass of organic remains originating from some type of spaceborne fauna. Having had any identifiable features shredded apart by the cosmic storm, it is impossible to correctly catalog the biological debris.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:630`

## cstorms.5080.desc.swarm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5080.desc.swarm&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，大部分仍可食用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Fortunately, it remains mostly edible.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:631`

## cstorms.5080.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5080.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

[leader.GetName]确信，对该生物遗骸进行更详细的法医分析将揭示更多有关其起源性质的信息，以及§Y[from.GetAffectedStormType]§!对类似太空生物的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

[leader.GetName] is confident that a more detailed forensic analysis of the creature's remains would reveal more information about the nature of its origins and the effects of a §Y[from.GetAffectedStormType]§! on similar space faring organisms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:632`

我们饿了。

### English

## FORENSIC_DISSECTION_PROJECT

## FORENSIC_DISSECTION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFORENSIC_DISSECTION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[solar_system.GetName]§!星系发现了被宇宙风暴夹带着穿越星系的某种未知生物的遗骸。对待这具遗骸进行法医鉴定可以揭示更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

We have encountered the remains of an unknown creature in the §H[solar_system.GetName]§! system dragged across the galaxy by a cosmic storm. Forensic dissection of the corpse should reveal more information.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:638`

## cstorms.5085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5085.name&lang=en)

$FORENSIC_DISSECTION_PROJECT$

### English

$FORENSIC_DISSECTION_PROJECT$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:639`

## cstorms.5085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过了谨慎的解剖，这团肉块究竟属于什么物种仍是个谜。在风暴中心遭受的巨大破坏使它失去了一切可供识别的特征和遗传标记。\n\n然而，在这项研究的过程中，我们获得了对§Y宇宙风暴§!组成的洞见，了解了其对外星生物质的破坏性影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

After careful dissection, the original species of this visceral mass remains a mystery. The extraordinary damage taken in the epicenter of the storm has stripped it of any recognizable features or genetic markers.\n\nHowever, in the process of this study we have gained insight into the composition of §YCosmic Storms§! and their devastating effects on xenobiological matter.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:640`

### English

## STORMS_DISPLACED_TIYANKI_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_DISPLACED_TIYANKI_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我的探测器从$default_storm$的余波中在§H[solar_system.GetName]§!星系发现了三个缇扬奇信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

In the aftermath of the $default_storm$ our sensors have detected three Tiyanki signatures in the §H[solar_system.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:644`

### English

## cstorms.5090.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5090.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[solar_system.GetName]§!星系内的三只缇扬奇信号经过识别后发现是一伙流离失所、营养不良的幼崽。\n\n§Y$default_storm$§!将这些幼崽与它们的大群分开，迫使他们自力更生。\n\n它们看来不太可能在幽深的太空中存活太久。我们的传感器显示它们整在试图呼叫鲸群，但没有成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

The three Tiyanki signatures in the §H[solar_system.GetName]§! system have been identified as a set of displaced and malnourished calves.\n\nThe §Y$default_storm$§! has separated these calves from their larger pod, leaving them to fend for themselves.\n\nThey are unlikely to survive for long in open space. Our sensors show they are attempting to call for their pod but are having no success.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:646`

## cstorms.5090.desc.swarm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5090.desc.swarm&lang=en)

我们可以放大它们呼救的声音，把它们当作诱饵，引来一顿更大的大餐。

### English

We could boost their cries for help and use them as bait to draw in a larger feast.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:647`

### English

### English

## REUNITE_POD_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREUNITE_POD_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

三只被宇宙风暴从鲸群中带丢的缇扬奇幼崽在[this.solar_system.GetName]星系游荡。我们可以通过放大它们的叫声来帮助它们把鲸群吸引到这个星系中来帮助它们重聚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

Three Tiyanki calves are lost and wandering the [this.solar_system.GetName] system, separated from their pod by a cosmic storm. We can help reunite them with their pod by boosting their cries to attract the rest of their pod to the system.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:658`

## cstorms.5095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇重聚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Tiyanki Reunited

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:660`

## cstorms.5095.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5095.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇的成兽在听到了三只幼崽被放大的叫声后，再度现身在§H[solar_system.GetName]§!星系中。\n\n年轻的缇扬奇重新回到了鲸群，现在已经恢复了快乐和健康。\n\n母鲸在我们的科研船附近留下了一大堆物质碎片，似乎是在感谢我们的帮助。令我们惊讶的是，这件奇怪的礼物中包含了好几种可用的材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

A Tiyanki cow and bull have both appeared in the §H[solar_system.GetName]§! system having heard the amplified cries of the three displaced calves.\n\nThe juvenile Tiyanki have been reintegrated back into their pod, and already appear much happier and healthier.\n\nThe cow has deposited a large mass of debris near our science vessel, seemingly as some kind of appreciation for our assistance. To our surprise this curious offering contains several usable materials.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:661`

## cstorms.5095.desc.swarm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.5095.desc.swarm&lang=en)

### 中文

当然，这些大东西才是真正的猎物；我们已经准备好猎杀了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Of course, these larger beasts are the true prize; primed and ready for the slaughter.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:662`

## collided_planets_category

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollided_planets_category&lang=en)

### 中文

相撞行星

### English

The Collided Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:669`

## collided_planets_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollided_planets_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亿万年前，两颗行星在此相撞。在毁灭的余波中，诞生了一颗独一无二的星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

Eons ago two planets collided. In the wake of their demise a unique world was born.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:670`

## cs_collided_planet_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_collided_planet_chain_title&lang=en)

相撞行星

### English

The Collided Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:672`

## cs_collided_planet_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_collided_planet_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难的阴影时刻威胁着这颗独特行星之上的脆弱平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

Disaster threatens to shatter the fragile balance of this unique planet.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:673`

## cstorms.1200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

双星往事

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

A Tale of Two Planets

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:675`

## cstorms.1200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从探测到[From.Planet.GetName]的那一刻起，它就一直吸引着我们的目光。\n\n在这样一个广阔而混乱的星系中，两个天体相撞并不是什么罕见的事情。不过，在此前类似的碰撞中，至少相撞的其中一个或者全部两个都会被摧毁。\n\n让[From.Planet.GetName]变成现在样子的碰撞发生在数百万年前，但这两颗星球却保持着一种奇怪的平衡，甚至在这一过程中还形成了维护生命体的大气层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

From the moment we detected [From.Planet.GetName], its mere existence has been a source of fascination.\n\nIt is not the collision of two celestial objects that is unusual, as the galaxy is a vast and chaotic place. However, in all known instances of such an impact, one or both of the bodies in question was destroyed.\n\nThe collision that formed [From.Planet.GetName] took place millions of years ago, and yet these two worlds maintain a strange equilibrium, and have even formed a life-sustaining atmosphere in the process.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:676`

## cstorms.1205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

脆弱的平衡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Precarious Balance

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:677`

## cstorms.1205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“相撞行星”上的情况远比预期的更加危险。最近一连串的地震又揭开了曾经相撞地区的伤疤，大量岩浆从地表喷涌而出。全球变暖和遮天蔽日的火山灰已经开始影响行星地表的各种生物。\n\n更糟糕的是，根据预测结果，如果我们不能在接下来的50年中稳定这对行星，那么行星板块活动将愈演愈烈，并最终使[From.Planet.GetName]解体！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

The situation on the so-called "collided planet" has turned out to be far more precarious than anticipated. A recent series of earthquakes opened up the old scars around the impact zone, causing a significant amount of magma to erupt from the ground. Various planetary biomes are already beginning to suffer from rising global temperatures and the volcanic ash that clouds the skies.\n\nTo make matters worse, if the joint planets are not stabilized within the next 50 years, our readings predict that tectonic activity will continue to escalate, ultimately causing the complete disintegration of [From.Planet.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:678`

## cstorms.1210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蜕变新生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Metamorphosis Unchained

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:679`

## cstorms.1210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们顶尖的地质学家齐聚于此，提出了两条可行的方案来拯救处于恐怖地壳活动之中的[From.Planet.GetName]。\n\n第一个计划是稳定当前状态，尽管填补撞击区域周围的火山需要数个世纪之久，但是我们可以先小心地在地表进行钻孔，减小地壳压力，避免板块失稳。残余的火山产物将用于改造行星，塑造一个稳定的生态环境。\n\n第二个计划就比较宏大了，与其减轻地壳压力，我们不如利用火山喷发产生的能量和物质来开展定向的环境改造。\n\n这种方法使我们能够塑造出多种生态环境，让不同的生命在此生存，将这个星球变为天堂。然而，该计划失败的可能性很大，甚至可能导致这颗独特的星球完全毁灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Our top geologists came together and formed two possible plans to save the [From.Planet.GetName] from succumbing to tectonic terror.\n\nThe first plan is to stabilize the collision. While it would take many centuries to heal the volcanic wound around the impact zone, through careful terrestrial trepanning we can alleviate the pressure on the planet's crust, preventing further tectonic instability. The remaining volcanic byproducts will be used to terraform the planet into a stable biome.\n\nThe second plan is far more ambitious. Instead of alleviating the pressure on the planet's crust, the energy and materials generated by the volcanic eruption could be adapted to a tailored terraforming process.\n\nThis approach allows us to sculpt a multitude of biomes capable of sustaining every type of lifeform, turning this world into a paradise. However, there is a significant chance that this procedure will fail - and could even lead to the complete and utter destruction of this unique stellar body.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:680`

## collided_planet_collapsing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollided_planet_collapsing&lang=en)

### 中文

行星解体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Planetary Disintegration

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:685`

Devastating tectonic activity will break the Collided Planet apart - destroying both worlds in the process.

## project_cosmic_storms_collidedplanet_harmonize_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aproject_cosmic_storms_collidedplanet_harmonize_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

执行这个大胆的计划，改造被撞行星的生态环境吧。我们要么创造一个适合所有生命的星球，要么将这颗行星推向毁灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

Carry out our ambitious plan to harmonize the biomes of the collided planet. We will either create a world for all, or doom it in the process.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:692`

## cstorms.1220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

稳定成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

Collision Maintained

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:694`

## cstorms.1220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们稳定被撞星球的努力得到了回报！先前威胁[From.Planet.GetName]的地震已经被平息，火山带来的产物也如同我们预想中一样迅速减少，这颗星球又回复到了最初处于脆弱平衡的状态。\n\n眼下环境改造的问题在于：[From.Planet.GetName]改造的方向应该是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

Our efforts to stabilize the collided world paid off! The tremors that previously threatened [From.Planet.GetName]'s existence have been silenced and the volcanic byproducts are rapidly decreasing as we speak, returning the world to its fragile balance.\n\nOne question remains for our final terraforming push: which class of planet shall [From.Planet.GetName] become?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:695`

## cstorms.1220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

保持当前的陆地星球状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

Maintain the continental class.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:696`

## cstorms.1220.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1220.b&lang=en)

改造成草原星球。

### English

Terraform a savanna world.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:697`

## cstorms.1220.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1220.c&lang=en)

改造成高山星球。

### English

Create an alpine world.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:698`

## cstorms.1225.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1225.name&lang=en)

### 中文

适合所有生物的星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

A World For Everyone

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:699`

## cstorms.1225.desc.stabalize

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1225.desc.stabalize&lang=en)

### 中文

令人震惊的是，我们原以为这些工作只能堪堪稳定[collided_planets.GetName]，没想到我们的成果远超预期。\n\n$cstorms.1225.desc.def$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

Extraordinary! We thought our efforts would only go as far as to stabilize [collided_planets.GetName], but it turned into something so much more!\n\n$cstorms.1225.desc.def$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:700`

## cstorms.1225.desc.def

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1225.desc.def&lang=en)

### 中文

看，这是一个天堂，一个能够让你想象中所有生命形式生活的天堂。\n\n拯救了一个走向毁灭的世界，并将其改造成了现在这副振奋人心的模样——还有什么比这更伟大的成就呢？命运女神无疑是眷顾我们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

Behold, a paradise, a planet capable of sustaining nearly every form of life imaginable.\n\nTo be able to save a doomed world and to transform it into something this inspiring - has there ever been a greater accomplishment? Fortune surely favors us.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:701`

## cstorms.1230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

全面大修

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Fixer Upper

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:702`

## cstorms.1230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管星球本身幸存下来了，但将其改造为天堂的努力却付诸东流了。\n\n行星严峻的地壳活动阻碍了我们对生态环境的改造，不过不稳定的状况似乎已经结束了。火山活动则回到了之前的水平，尽管在撞击区域火山依旧活跃，但短时间内不太可能再次升级。\n\n最坏的时期似乎已经过去了。现在我们所面临的挑战是重建这颗星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

While the world itself was thankfully saved, our endeavor to terraform the collided planet into a paradise has failed.\n\nOur attempts to tailor the biome were frustrated by the planet's severe tectonic activity, although the worst of the destabilization appears to be over. Volcanic activity has returned to the levels first witnessed before the recent upheaval, and while the impact site remains active, it is unlikely to escalate any time soon.\n\nThe worst appears to be over. Our challenge now is to rebuild this world.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:703`

## cstorms.1235.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

家园四分五裂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Home Asunder

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:704`

## cstorms.1235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图拯救濒临毁灭的被撞行星的努力付诸东流了。尽管科学家们采取了一切可能的预防措施，但他们的模型从未能考虑到所有情况。\n\n随着星球的残骸逐渐漂向虚空，整个星系都为这一奇景的消失而哀悼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

Our attempt to save the collided planet from impending doom has failed miserably. While our scientists took every possible precaution, their models were never able to take every contingency into account.\n\nAs the remnants of these worlds drift ever further into the void, the galaxy mourns the loss of this unique phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:705`

## cstorms.1240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

撞击，毁灭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

Collided and Crashed

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:706`

## cstorms.1240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被撞行星的毁灭进程完全按照我们的所预测的时间线进行，准确度精确到十分位。这个曾经一度点缀了星海的奇景，如今除了空留下一堆岩石，什么也没有剩下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

The collapse of the collided planet followed our timeline predictions down to the decimal. Where once the galaxy was enriched by a unique phenomenon, now nothing but rocks remain.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:707`

## collided_planets_destroyed_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollided_planets_destroyed_tt&lang=en)

[collided_planets.GetName]行星已被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Planet [collided_planets.GetName] is destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:708`

## cs_crimson_crawlers_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_crimson_crawlers_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

猩红爬虫

### English

Crimson Crawlers
