## cs_crimson_crawlers_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_crimson_crawlers_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现一颗行星表面覆盖着密密麻麻数以兆计的红色昆虫。这对于我们而言究竟是灾疫还是机遇呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

We found a planet carpeted in trillions of red insects. A plague, or an opportunity?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:712`

## crimson_crawlers_category

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_category&lang=en)

### 中文

猩红地表

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

Crimson Coverage

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:713`

## crimson_crawlers_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetStarName]恒星系中发现了一颗红色的行星，颜色很深。我们应该对此开展调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

A scarlet planet was surveyed in the [From.GetStarName] system. The intensity of the color is remarkable. We should investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:714`

## cstorms.1255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

猩红爬虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

Crimson Crawlers

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:715`

## cstorms.1255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从远处看，[From.GetName]就像那些红色珠宝一样散发着光芒。对于这样一种地质异常现象，通常可能的原因是因为有黏土或者是其他种类的土壤中夹杂了硬金属。但这次的事实要更为离奇。\n\n那是虫子，上万亿条虫子。它们聚集在一起，密密麻麻地铺满了[From.GetName]表面。\n\n我们快速将其命名为“猩红爬虫”，这群无脊椎生物的繁殖周期十分短。尽管没有破坏自然环境，但它们四处蠕动，搞得[From.GetName]不适合开展殖民。\n\n有人提出了一条不怎么正经的方法来处理这些昆虫。初步扫描显示它们是可食用的，或许我们可以把它们当作新的食物来源？不管怎样，至少我们建议先调查一下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

From a distance, [From.GetName]'s shone like some precious crimson jewel. Clay or other soil types infused with hard metals would be the usual explanation for such a geographical anomaly, but the truth was far more bizarre.\n\nBugs. Trillions, upon trillions, of bugs. They swarm over the surface of [From.GetName] in an almost unbroken carpet of multi-legged life.\n\nQuickly dubbed the 'crimson crawlers', these invertebrates possess a remarkably hyperactive reproduction cycle. While not harmful to their native environment, [From.GetName] is not well suited for colonization with these creatures scurrying about.\n\nOne rather unorthodox method of handling the insects has been proposed. Initial scans show they are edible, so perhaps we have found a new food source? We advise at least investigating the opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:716`

## project_cosmic_storms_crimson_crawlers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aproject_cosmic_storms_crimson_crawlers&lang=en)

### 中文

“精神”食粮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

Food for Thought

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:717`

## project_cosmic_storms_crimson_crawlers_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aproject_cosmic_storms_crimson_crawlers_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]地表完全被这些温和的红色昆虫所覆盖。想要行星变得宜居，就要先处理掉这些虫子。与此同时，它们似乎是一种不错的食物来源，或许我们可以从此入手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

[From.Planet.GetName] is entirely covered by placid, red insects. The planet could be habitable if the bugs are dealt with. At the same time, they appear to be a good source of food. There may be a path forward here.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:718`

## cstorms.1260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不同寻常的料理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

Uncommon Cuisine

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:719`

## cstorms.1260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一开始，大家都觉得把这种猩红爬虫当作食物有点太荒谬了。但在把一份虫子标本带回船上后，人们觉得吃这些虫子也不是不能接受。\n\n这些虫子简直是奇迹的造物，神经系统极其先进，但是却没有大脑这种中枢处理器官。更重要的是，它们浅色的肉出人意料地嫩滑多汁，哪怕是生吃，猩红的外骨骼尝起来也既酥脆又美味。\n\n现在的问题是，这些虫子是更适合作为研究材料，还是更适合作为一种美味的食物。把它们做成菜肴会带来更多风味和乐趣，但特别是在搭配适当调味品的情况下直接生吃会更快更简单。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

The idea of consuming the crimson crawlers may have seemed preposterous at first, but when a specimen was brought on board, it became far more palatable.\n\nThe insects are a creature of wonder, possessing an extremely advanced nervous system without the benefit of a central processing organ. More importantly, their pale flesh is surprisingly tender and moist, and even when consumed raw, the vermillion-hued exoskeleton was deemed both crispy and delightful.\n\nThe question remains as to whether the bugs would better serve as research material or as a delectable source of food. Cooking them might provide more flavor and fun, but eating them as they come will be quicker and easier, especially if paired with the appropriate condiment.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:720`

## cstorms.1260.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1260.a&lang=en)

涮锅起灶！

困于风暴的舰船

### English

Stormbound

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:726`

## cs_stormbound_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_stormbound_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘不祥的飞船出现在我们的传感器上——但它正处于宇宙风暴之中。那里发生了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

An ominous vessel has appeared on sensors - but only within the cosmic storms. What could be at play here?

被困舰船再现

### English

Stormbound Rises

## cstorms.1310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从[This.GetName]星系接收到了一些奇怪的报告。显然，上一场在该星域肆虐的宇宙风暴把一艘不知来处的舰船卷了出来。这艘船没有呼号，它不知怎么地穿过了暴风雨，仿佛完全没受周围风暴的影响。\n\n这艘船没有回应我们的呼叫，风暴消散后，它也消失得无影无踪了。这可能是由于风暴对仪器的干扰，但是肉眼观测却毋庸置疑地表明了真相。\n\n这其中可能潜藏着极为先进的科技，还是说发生了某些不祥的事情？我们会时刻盯着这艘神秘舰船，直到它再次出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

We are receiving strange reports from the [This.GetName] system. Apparently, the last cosmic storm raging in that sector revealed a ship of unknown origin. Bearing no call sign, it somehow cruised through the tempest as if entirely unaffected by the surrounding violence.\n\nIt did not respond to hails, and as soon as the storm dissipated, so did the vessel itself. This might have been dismissed as instrument artifacting, but the visual evidence spoke for itself.\n\nCould this be a display of incredibly advanced technology, or is there something more ominous at play? We will be sure to report back any occurrences of this mysterious vessel.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:731`

## cstorms.1313.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1313.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被困舰船回归

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Stormbound Returns

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:732`

## cstorms.1313.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1313.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.GetName]中的一座研究站带给了我们一份惊人的报告：在上一次宇宙风暴肆虐期间，一艘奇怪的舰船差点就撞上了哨站。肉眼观测表明，这艘船就是之前报告的那艘不详的舰船。\n\n据空间站汇报，这艘舰船的船体由奇异的有机物、钙矿以及金属合金的融合体构成。\n\n在船上没有探测到任何有机生命的迹象，这让我们推测这艘船是不是自动化或者是由于某些意外而失去了控制。\n\n然而，最令人困惑的事情是，太空碎片已经将这艘船的大部分推进系统击碎，它本应难逃崩坏的命运。\n\n如果我们能派遣科研船监测下一次宇宙风暴的话，说不定可以获得有关这艘飞船的更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

One of the research stations in [This.GetName] came to us with a startling report: During the last cosmic storm, a strange ship had almost collided with the outpost. Visual reports show it was the same ominous vessel first reported some time ago.\n\nAccording to the space station, the hulls on the vessel were constructed from a strange amalgamation of organic compounds, calcium minerals and metallic alloys.\n\nNo organic life was detected, leading us to speculate that either this vessel was automated, or had somehow gone rogue.\n\nHowever, the most baffling thing was yet to come: large sections of its propulsion system were clearly shattered by space debris. This vessel should have been dead in space.\n\nIf we assign a science vessel to monitor the next cosmic storm, they might be able to find out more about this mysterious craft.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:733`

## cstorms.1313.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1313.a&lang=en)

我们没时间理会这无稽之谈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

We have no time for such nonsense.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:734`

## cstorms.1316.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1316.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴中的低语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

Whispers in the Tempest

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:735`

## cstorms.1316.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1316.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[stormbound_science.GetName]远远地发现那艘不祥的舰船出现在风暴之中。当它飘过时，我们从宇宙空间中探测到了一股强烈的信号。\n\n我们无法探测到这艘船的原因终于真相大白：它的物理形态已经伸展得超越了多维空间，看上去就好像已经进入了虫洞又好像没有。可能是由于宇宙风暴的多维起源，这艘船才得以回到我们的空间。\n\n知道这一点后，当这艘船下次再出现时，我们应该能够探测到，说不定还能将它拉回到我们的现实中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

In the distance [stormbound_science.GetName] witnessed the ominous ship appearing between the layers of the storm. As it floated by, we detected wild readings emanating from the cosmic tapestry.\n\nThis finally provided an answer to why this ship could not be observed by our sensors: its physical manifestation had been stretched beyond the manifold, as if it had both entered and not entered a wormhole. It was likely due to the hyperdimensional origins of cosmic storms that the ship managed to return to our space.\n\nKnowing this, we should be able to detect the next occurrence, and perhaps pull this craft back to our reality.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:736`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1320.name&lang=en)

归乡之路

### English

Homebound

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:739`

## cstorms.1320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就在那里！那艘不祥的船只正位于[Root.Fleet.GetName]前方，并联络了我们。屏幕上出现了一个面带华丽面具的机械实体，她自称为碧廷，并向我们讲述了一则引人入胜的故事。\n\n她曾是一位著名的指挥官，也是恩典公约这一组织的杰出代表。在一次巡逻途中，她和她的船员被卷入了宇宙风暴之中，为求得一线生机，她大胆将舰船的‘突破驱动装置’过载。这种实验性技术从未有人测试过，结果舰船连带船员们一起被困在了风暴的高维场中。\n\n故事到此为止，她从严重损毁的船上抛出一个小匣子，里面放着一摞文件。“把这些拿回去吧，”碧廷请求道。之后通讯结束，舰船也随之消失在多维结空间结构中。\n\n这些文件上都是外星文字，我们需要花点时间进行破译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

There it is! Positioning itself in front of [Root.Fleet.GetName], the ominous ship hailed us. A mechanical entity in an ornate mask appeared on screen. Identifying herself as B'Tiin, her tale was a fascinating one.\n\nShe was once a renowned officer, a true paragon of an organization known as the Convention of Benefactors. While on patrol, she and her crew were caught inside a cosmic storm, and in a daring bid for survival, she made the decision to overcharge the ship's 'Breach Drive.' This experimental technology had never been tested, and the vessel and its crew was trapped within the storm's hyperdimensional field.\n\nHer story finished, a small container was released from the decimated ship. Inside was a collection of documents. "Take these home," B'Tiin requested, after which the transmission ended, and the ship disappeared into the spatial manifold.\n\nThe documents are written in a foreign script, and will take some time to decipher.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:740`

## cstorms.1320.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1320.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开始翻译。

### English

Begin the translation.

## cstorms.1321.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1321.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来自高维的讯息

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

Letters From The Manifold

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:742`

## cstorms.1321.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1321.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

古怪匣子中的文件已经被翻译完毕。其中一份文件记录了那艘船所谓“突破驱动装置”的设计图。尽管该装置仍超出我们的科技水平，但对其构造原理的仔细研究之后，我们对于超空间航行的理解更加深入了。\n\n其余的则是一些私人信件，没有什么用处，但是却更能触动人心。这些信都是写给他们心爱的人，笔间填满了思念、希望和遗憾。\n\n似乎碧廷的目的是想要让她的船员们有最后一次机会与外界联系。但不幸的是，我们不知道这些信要寄往何方，所以不如把这些信留下来，至少还可以保存他们这份奇异的遗产。或者我们也可以销毁这些信件，毕竟这艘古怪的舰船和幽灵般的信件有点让人内心发毛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

The contents of the strange container have been deciphered. One document contained a schematic of the ship's so-called Breach Drive. While still beyond our own technology, careful study of the principles behind its construction have already improved our understanding of hyperspatial travel.\n\nLess beneficial perhaps, but more affecting, were the translations of personal letters. Each is addressed to a loved one, speaking of fondness, hope, and regret.\n\nIt seems B'Tiin's intention was to give her crew one last chance to reach out. Unfortunately, we have no way of knowing where to deliver these letters. Making them available to our own people could at least preserve their strange legacy. Alternatively, it might be wise to destroy them. Knowledge of strange ships and ghostly letters may be unsettling.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:743`

## but_they_are_cute_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abut_they_are_cute_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在调查完[From.Planet.GetName]后，我们发现了一种奇特的生物。尽管我们只和它们相处了一会，但这已经激发了我们的保护欲。建议开展进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

After a survey of [From.Planet.GetName], a strange creature was discovered. Despite having only been among us for a short time, it has already instilled a deep  desire to nurture and protect it. Further study is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:754`

## cstorms.1500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

但它们实在太可爱了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

But They're Cute!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:755`

## cstorms.1500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这只生物自被发现以来就展现出极大的影响力，激发了我们的爱意和投喂欲。四只鼓起的泡泡般的眼睛展现着它的纯真，吸引所有人前来撸它那柔顺如云的毛发。它似乎很亲近[Root.GetLeaderName]，大部分时间都呆在[Leader.GetHisHer]的膝盖上，享受着[Leader.GetHisHer]的宠爱，没有展现出任何攻击或是危险动作，就好像是被特意设计成这副无害的模样。\n\n尽管如此，一些人质疑，在未经事先批准的情况下，将一种以前未发现的生物留在船上是不负责任的。[Root.GetLeaderName]则保证它大概率是安全的，但也认可需要进行正式调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

From the moment the creature was discovered, its impact was undeniable, inspiring love and devotion among us almost instantaneously. Its four bulging eyes displayed innocence and invited anyone to stroke its soft, cloud-like fur. It seems particularly attached to [Root.GetLeaderName], spending most of its time on [Leader.GetHisHer] lap enjoying [Leader.GetHisHer] affections, not showing any signs of aggression or danger. It is as if it was perfectly engineered to be as inoffensive as possible.\n\nStill, some skeptics have pointed out how irresponsible it would be to keep a previously undiscovered life form aboard the ship without prior approval. [Root.GetLeaderName] vouched that it is definitely probably safe, but has agreed to request a formal investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:756`

## cstorms.1505.name

生物奇迹

### English

Biological Marvel

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:762`

## cstorms.1505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不需要进行麻醉，也不需要强迫就能触摸并扫描这只动物，它表现得十分顺从。船员们根据它被抚摸时发出的声音将其命名为咕咕。\n\n我们确认咕咕是杂食动物，它的毛发由常见且非常柔软的角蛋白丝组成。但真正神奇的事情是它的内在，它可以在一个月内更新包括自己神经系统在内的全部细胞。这意味着它可以在几天内再生出正常功能的躯体。\n\n如此复杂的生物结构似乎不太可能是自然进化的产物。我们以前从未见过类似的生物，因此无法确定它的性情。尽管看起来温顺，但它随时可能对我们发起攻击。尽管如此，自从它出现以来，[Root.GetLeaderName]似乎比以往任何时候都更有工作动力。\n\n我们应该怎么处理咕咕？我们能把它留下吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

The docile hexapod did not need to be sedated or even coerced into being prodded and scanned. It seemed strangely agreeable. The crew nicknamed it Gurr, after the soft noise it makes when petted.\n\nWe have concluded that Gurr is an omnivore, and that its fur consists of familiar and very soft keratin-based filaments. But the true marvel and mystery takes place inside the adorable creature: it achieves total cell renewal within a month. This includes its nervous system, meaning that it could regrow fully functional body parts within a matter of days.\n\nIt seems unlikely something with such a sophisticated biological makeup is a product of natural evolution. We have never before seen anything like it, and cannot be certain of its temperament. Despite its apparent docility, it could turn on us at any moment. That said, since its appearance, [Root.GetLeaderName] seems more motivated to work than ever.\n\nWhat shall we do with Gurr? Can we keep it?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:763`

## cstorms.1505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1505.a&lang=en)

只要你搞好卫生就行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

As long as you clean up after it.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:764`

## cstorms.1505.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1505.b&lang=en)

把它处理掉。

### English

## cstorms.1510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

情感支持生物咕咕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

Emotional Support Gurr

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:766`

## cstorms.1510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这只可爱的生物被允许留在科研船上，目前已经被安置妥当。它安抚了[Root.GetLeaderName]的情绪，使其不论走到哪都神清气爽，船员们的士气也前所未有的高。\n\n希望这个非凡的“自然”奇迹能继续成为我们所有人的可爱且温顺的朋友。它，咕咕，将属于我们，直到永远。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

The adorable creature was allowed to stay aboard the science vessel and has since settled in nicely. It calms [Root.GetLeaderName], lightens the mood wherever it trots, and crew morale is at an all time high!\n\nHopefully this remarkable wonder of "nature" will continue to be a cute, docile, friend to all of us. From now until the end, it shall be ours, and it shall be named Gurr.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:767`

## cstorms.1510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

太棒了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

Splendid!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:768`

## cstorms.1515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

咕咕的结局

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

The end of Gurr

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:769`

## cstorms.1515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

鉴于这只动物的身体不同寻常并且明显经过基因改造，我们不能把它留在科学船上。它很有可能给船员带来负面影响，而且说不定它的主人什么时候就突然愤怒现身要求我们归还。决定执行得迅速且仁慈，在一瞬间，它那柔和的“咕咕”声就此永远消失了。\n\n这个决定尽管情有可原，但还是引发了[Root.GetLeaderName]和船员的抗拒。他们还需要一些时间来平复失去这只可爱奇异动物的悲痛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Given its unusual and clearly genetically engineered body, the creature could not stay aboard the science vessel. The risk of it adversely affecting the crew, or an aggrieved owner appearing to demand its return was simply too great. The deed was done swiftly and mercifully. In an instant, its soft "gurr" was silenced forever.\n\nWhile understandable, this decision has prompted an emotional backlash from [Root.GetLeaderName] and the crew. It will take some time to get over the loss of this adorable freak of nature.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:770`

## cstorms.1515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1515.a&lang=en)

### 中文

别无选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

There was no other choice.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:771`

## cstorms.1515.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1515.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

保存一些基因样品吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Let's save a DNA sample.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:772`

## cstorms.1520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

咕咕的结局？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

The end of Gurr?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:773`

## cstorms.1520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

处理掉这只可爱动物之后，事情没有就此结束，它出色的再生能力似乎超出了我们的预期。\n\n在它死亡后不久，从那显然毫无生机的组织中诞生了两个完全相同的生物。即使杀死它们母体的人就是我们，这些新生的生命体也没有表现出任何攻击性。\n\n我们决定就此打住，而不是冒险创造出一支咕咕大军。[Root.GetLeaderName]和船员们对能够留下这对生物感到非常高兴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

Neutralizing the adorable creature was meant to be the end of it, but its remarkable regenerative characteristics seemed to have gone further than we could have guessed.\n\nNot too long after its demise, two identical copies of the creature were spawned from its apparently lifeless tissue. Even after our decision to terminate its progenitor's life, the resulting lifeforms are not showing any signs of aggression.\n\nWe shall leave it at this, rather than risk creating an army of Gurrs. [Root.GetLeaderName] and the crew have expressed their delight at being allowed to keep the pair.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:774`

## cstorms.1520.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1520.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一次教训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

Lesson learned.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:775`

## cstorms.1550.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1550.name&lang=en)

### 中文

模仿者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

The Imitator

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:778`

## cstorms.1550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]发现一个随风暴逐流的逃生舱。尽管数据签名看起来像是我们的，但可以肯定它不属于我们。我们谨慎地回应了通讯请求，而舱内人员显得尤为高兴。\n\n[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeSheCap]解释称[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe]已经随着风暴拉扯了一段时间，正寻求救援，[event_target:the_imitator.GetHerHis]飞船上的生命维持系统已经濒临崩溃。\n\n当我们要求其表明身份时，这个陌生人却奇怪地声称自己就是我们的船员。这显然是在撒谎，因为我们的船员一个不漏，都在船上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

The pod discovered in [From.Planet.GetName] has been left to the mercy of the storm. Even though the data signature it uses looks to be one of ours, the pod is definitely alien. We answered the request for communications cautiously, but we were met with elation by the capsule's inhabitant.\n\n[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeSheCap] explained [event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe] has been dragged along by the tempest for some time and is seeking rescue, [event_target:the_imitator.GetHerHis] vessel's life support systems on the brink of failure.\n\nWhen asked to identify themselves, the stranger bizarrely claimed to be a member of our own crew. The lie is painfully obvious, if for no other reason than the fact that everyone on board our ship is accounted for.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:779`

## cstorms.1550.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1550.a&lang=en)

### 中文

别跟他兜圈子了，摧毁逃生舱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

Enough games. Destroy the pod.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:780`

## cstorms.1550.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1550.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把逃生舱带上船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

Bring the pod aboard.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:781`

## cstorms.1555.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1555.name&lang=en)

### 中文

模仿者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

The Imitator

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:782`

## cstorms.1555.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1555.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们把逃生舱带上船了。我们打开太空舱的封条，真相就揭晓了，里面的外星人立即发出了绝望的请求。\n\n原来这名外星人在对自己母舰的逃生舱进行例行检查时被困在的仓内，并且逃生舱被发射到了太空中。非常不幸的是，船上没有人发现[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe]消失了。\n\n在被困几个月之后，[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe]终于通过一些在逃生舱内能搞到的小东西，向路过的飞船发送一种诱饵信号。然而这也没能吸引到任何援手。更糟的是，一股宇宙风暴席卷了逃生舱，并把它带到了母星系好多光年之外的地方。在孤独和恐惧的驱使下，[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe]想出了一个计划，伪装成发生在附近的任何船只的失踪船员。这位逃生舱的幸存者为他欺骗了我们道歉，但[event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe]很庆幸自己活了下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

We brought the escape pod aboard our ship. The ruse ended as soon as the capsule's seal opened, after which the alien within immediately launched into a desperate plea.\n\nDuring a routine inspection of the escape pods on [event_target:the_imitator.GetHerHis] mother ship, [event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe] was trapped inside and ejected into space. Sadly, no one aboard their vessel noticed [event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe] was missing.\n\nStranded for months, [event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe] used what little tech [event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe] could find aboard [event_target:the_imitator.GetHerHis] pod to mimic the energy signals of passing ships as a type of lure. However, even this failed to attract help. Worst of all, a cosmic storm took hold of the pod, dragging it lightyears from its home system. Driven to extremes by loneliness and fear, [event_target:the_imitator.GetHeShe] concocted a plan to pose as a missing crew member of any ship that happened by. The pod-dweller apologized for the deception, but is glad to be alive.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:783`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1555.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们或许能够用得上这样灵活的头脑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Such resourcefulness could be useful.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:784`

提供交通工具送到附近的有人居住的星球。

### English

Provide transport to the nearest inhabited planet.

## the_imitator_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_imitator_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在最近的一次调查中发现了一个单独的逃生舱。奇怪的是，这个逃生舱用与我们相同的数据签名向我们打招呼。这个逃生舱似乎是被最近一场穿过这个星系的宇宙风暴所带过来的。建议进行更深入的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

A lone escape pod was spotted during a recent survey. Strangely, the pod hailed us using a data signature matching that of our own. The pod seems to have been displaced by a recent cosmic storm that passed through the system. Investigation is recommended.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:787`

## the_imitator_category

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_imitator_category&lang=en)

### 中文

模仿者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

The Imitator

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:790`

## the_imitator_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_imitator_catch_phrase&lang=en)

### 中文

我很高兴来到这里！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

I'm so glad to be here!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:791`

## the_imitator_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_imitator_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在随着宇宙风暴漂流之后，这个狡猾但古怪的天才却知道如何扭转局面，而且通常是向好的方向。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

Brought aboard after being dragged around by a Cosmic Storm, this crafty but eccentric genius knows how to turn any situation around, usually for the better.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:792`

## site_green_skies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_green_skies&lang=en)

### 中文

绿色天空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

Green Skies

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:799`

## site_green_skies_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_green_skies_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的扫描仪探测到该星球的一个区域不断发出一些有趣的信号。经分析，那里很有可能存在古代文明的遗迹正等着我们去发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

Our scanners picked up a series of intriguing signals coming from this area of the planet. Based on their composition, it's highly likely there is a remnant of an ancient civilization waiting to be uncovered there.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:800`

## cstorms.1400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇特的混合风格

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

A Curious Collage

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:801`

## cstorms.1400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的基地附近出现了一片奇特的城市遗址。\n\n这些建筑包含了我们迄今为止所遇到的每一个文明的建筑特点——也包括我们自己的！\n\n然而，这些建筑所用的材料组成与我们在行星殖民过程中建立基地时使用的材料相似。这很奇怪，因为我们的这类材料不可能坚持数十年之久，更别提我们扫描确定的这些遗迹所经过的时间了。\n\n这些遗址不太可能属于哪位神秘的先驱者，但扫描结果却与之相悖。这其中到底有什么玄机？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

Near our basecamp we were greeted by a set of peculiar city ruins.\n\nThe architecture showed characteristics of every civilization we've encountered so far - including our own!\n\nHowever, the material composition is similar to what we use to establish base camps during planetary colonization. This is strange because these materials could never hold out for multiple decades, let alone the age our scanners determined these ruins to be.\n\nIt's unlikely for these ruins to have belonged to a mysterious precursor, yet the scanners persist in their conclusion. What could be at play here?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:802`

## cstorms.1405.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1405.name&lang=en)

### 中文

山雨欲来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

Weathered Situation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:803`

## cstorms.1405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们追踪信号进入了废墟城市的地下层。一般情况下，这类考古遗址都会存在一个交通相连的地下网络，或者有一批避难所。不过，我们发现了一个布满布料碎片、聚合物容器，甚至有机废物的小洞穴。看来我们并不是第一批探索这里的人。\n\n令人担忧的是，[Root.GetLeaderName] 告诉我们外面的天空正在变绿，一场宇宙风暴即将在 [Fromfrom.getName] 形成。尽管如此，我们仍然向着信号的来源前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

The trail of the signals led to a pathway into the lower levels of the ruined city. Usually archeological sites of this type have an underground transportation network or a collection of safety bunkers. Instead we found a small cave system littered with scraps of fabric, polymer containers and even organic waste. It appears we are not the first to explore this archeological site.\n\nWorryingly, [Root.GetLeaderName] informs us the skies outside are turning green; a cosmic storm is about to develop inside [Fromfrom.getName]. Still, we press on towards the origin point of the signals.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:804`

## cstorms.1410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失算之举

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

Folly

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:805`

## cstorms.1410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一时被一扇严重损毁的金属大门挡住去路，但还是不费吹灰之力就打开了通道，门后是一个简陋的仓库。\n\n仓库的架子上堆满了各种废弃的高科技设备，墙边则小心地摆放着属于几家知名企业的箱子。最后，我们终于找到了追踪已久的信号源：一个与我们自己的设备相似的应答机。\n\n在我们登录设备并查看其数据库后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]得出一个大胆的结论：该信号被故意设计得尽可能神秘和引人入胜——它模仿结合了其他文明的特征。\n\n此外，在箱子里我们还发现了地面上废墟所使用的建造模件。\n\n于是我们联系基地，开始汇报当前地发现，但宇宙风暴已经开始干扰我们的通讯。我们最后接收到来自[Root.GetLeaderName]的讯息令人担忧，地上营地似乎正遭受攻击！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Our progress was briefly halted by a set of badly damaged metal doors. With little effort we worked our way past this obstacle and a rudimentary storage facility revealed itself.\n\nIts shelves were filled to the brim with an assortment of derelict technologies. Boxes of well known corporations had been carefully stacked against the walls. And finally, the source of the signals we had been chasing for so long: a transponder computer not unlike our own.\n\nAs we logged into the device and searched its databases, our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] started forming a wild conclusion: the signal was deliberately constructed to appear as mysterious and intriguing as possible - mimicking and combining characteristics from other civilizations.\n\nWhat's more, inside the boxes we found construction kits of the ruins we encountered above ground.\n\nWe contacted our basecamp to report our findings, but the cosmic storm has already started interrupting our com lines. The last thing we heard from [Root.GetLeaderName] is a worrying set of commands; it appears our basecamp is under attack!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:806`

### English

## cstorms.1415.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1415.name&lang=en)

### 中文

海盗来袭！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

Pirates!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:808`

## cstorms.1415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一支海盗舰队隐藏在[Fromfrom.getName]的宇宙风暴之中！正是它们攻击了我们的基地。这些海盗向我们发送了一条讯息：\n\n§L亲爱的蠢货们：\n\n谢谢你们来到我们这处隐蔽的驻扎地。\n\n看到你们这些看似聪明的生物们闻到一丝古代文物和肮脏遗珍的气息就立刻抛弃一切理智，不禁让人感叹不已。也许你们希望找到一种摧毁强大敌人的手段？建造无数的舰队直抵事件视界？或者只是想让自己的人民过上美好生活？多么俗套的想法啊。\n\n请不要难过——在你们之前还有许多人来过，而我们的商业模式则依赖于那些重蹈你们覆辙的后来人。\n\n现在，只需2000能量币，我们就会把抢来的东西还给你们。\n\n要是拒绝我们的提议，谁知道会发生什么可怕的事情呢，说不定会给[Leader.getHerHim]和[Leader.getHisHer]的这支装备欠佳的船员们带来什么噩运，他们可还被困在这个星球上。\n\n请做出明智之选。§!\n\n躲在洞穴里暂时是安全的，但我们不知道能安全多久。我们要满足这些土匪的要求吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

The cosmic storm inside [Fromfrom.getName] turned out to have been concealing a pirate fleet! It was their crew that attacked our basecamp. The pirates asked us to relay this message to you:\n\n§LDear absolute fools,\n\nThank you for joining us at our most elusive retreat.\n\nIsn't it amazing just how quickly seemingly intelligent beings abandon all reason whenever they detect the merest whiff of ancient artifacts and devious relics? Perhaps you had hoped to find a means to destroy a great enemy? To build fleets as far as the event horizon reaches? Or were you just trying to be nice to your own people? How quaint.\n\nPlease don't feel bad - many others came before you, and our business model depends on that many more will follow your footsteps.\n\nNow, for a modest sum of 2000 energy credits we will return something you have lost.\n\nReject our offer and who knows what terrible things could happen to [Leader.getHerHim] and [Leader.getHisHer] poorly armed crew that is still trapped on the planet.\n\nPlease choose wisely.§!\n\nWe are safe in these caverns for now, but we don't know for how long. Are we going to entertain these bandits?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:809`

## cstorms.1416.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1416.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到一条新讯息：\n\n§L可惜，我们本可以用这笔钱做很多有趣的事情。\n\n我们会转告这位领袖和[Leader.getHisHer]的团队，就说他们的命不值分文。也许附近的奴隶商或一位同化斗士会有兴趣。我们听说精神阉割是现在的新潮流。\n\n就此别过。§!\n\n我们把手头上一切能找到的东西用来修筑壁垒，附近的物资也足够我们撑上一段时间。但海盗们最终肯定会突破我们的防御！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

We have received a new message:\n\n§LShame. There are a lot of fun things we could've done with those resources.\n\nWe will inform the leader and [Leader.getHisHer] crew that their lives weren't worth the effort or credits. Maybe a nearby slaver or a very driven assimilator might be interested. We hear nerve stapling is the hot new trend.\n\nEntrepreneurs out.§!\n\nWe have used everything we could find to barricade ourselves in, and there are enough supplies to keep us safe for a while. But the pirate fleet is bound to break through our defenses eventually!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:814`

## cstorms.1420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

错不二犯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Won't Get Fooled Again

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:815`

## cstorms.1420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些海盗的技术完全支持不了它们狂妄的言论。不久之后，他们的舰队成为了星系生态系统的一部分。\n\n我们很快重新与地表的船员们取得联系。他们被吓坏了，但没有受到伤害。\n\n虽然在这里没有发现古代文明的洞见，但这段经历告诉我们以后要提高警惕。探险队领袖未能察觉到眼前的危险——我们以后不能再犯同样的错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

These pirates could not back up their cocky speeches with skill. Before long, whatever was left of their fleet became a part of the galactic ecosystem.\n\nWe reestablished contact with the crew on the surface soon after. They were rather shaken by the experience, but suffered no harm.\n\nWhile we discovered no insights into ancient civilizations here, our experience instilled a need for vigilance. The expedition leader failed to see incoming danger - we will not make the same mistake.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:816`

## cstorms.1420.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1420.a&lang=en)

### 中文

吸取教训。

### English

Let that be a lesson to us all.

### English

## cstorms.1425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了回复的讯息：\n\n§L如果可以的话，我愿意称之为明智之选！\n\n请知悉，您对于“急需度假星球的弱势企业家”的拨款已经收到。\n\n很快你们就能再见到你们的领袖和随人附赠的船员。\n\n此外，您可以向所有贸易伙伴推荐我们。§!\n\n不久之后，我们重新与地表船员建立了联系。他们称自己被吓坏了，但没有受到伤害。\n\n虽然在这里没有发现古代文明的洞见，但这段经历告诉我们以后要提高警惕。探险队领袖未能察觉到眼前的危险——我们以后不能再犯同样的错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

We've received a message in return:\n\n§LMarvelous choice, if we may say so!\n\nLet it be known that your contribution to our 'Fund for Disadvantaged Entrepreneurs in Need of a Resort Planet' has been received and processed.\n\nYou will find your leader and the complimentary crew in your possession soon enough.\n\nAnd do recommend us to all your trade partners.§!\n\nSoon after we were able to reestablish contact with the crew on the surface. They reported to be rather shaken by the experience, but suffered no harm.\n\nWhile we discovered no insights into ancient civilizations here, our experience instilled a need for vigilance. The expedition leader failed to see incoming danger - we will not make the same mistake.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:820`

## site_hold_the_line_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_hold_the_line_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

席卷而过的宇宙风暴留下了一份奇怪的礼物：一座属于老旧居住站系统的轨道复合体。登陆之后有可能加以激活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

The passing cosmic storm left behind a curious present: an orbital complex from an old habitat system. Boarding may allow us to reactivate it.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:833`

## cstorms.1275.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1275.name&lang=en)

### 中文

全新困惑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

Brand New Bemusement

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:834`

## cstorms.1275.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1275.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管这座轨道复合体被宇宙风暴拖拽着漂星过海，但它似乎没怎么受到损伤。我们发现居住站中的居住和商业空间几乎状态完好。这些区域一尘不染，仿佛从未有人使用过一般。\n\n在中控室中迎接我们的是一个令人眼花缭乱的输入系统：各种没有任何说明的刻度盘，设置不一的拉杆，大量的按钮以看似随机的间隔五颜六色地闪烁着。幸运的是，我们最终找到了一本手册。相信只要成功翻译出来，我们就可以重新激活这座居住站了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

Despite having been dragged across the galaxy by the cosmic storm, the orbital complex suffered very little harm. We found the living and commercial spaces of the habitat in almost perfect condition. Not a single bit of dust laid atop the surface that would betray a previous occupancy, as if it had never before been used.\n\nInside the central control hub we were greeted by a bewildering input system: dials with no explanation, levers with inconsistent settings and a great amount of buttons flashing a whole spectrum of colors at seemingly random intervals. Fortunately, we eventually came across a manual. Once this is successfully translated, we are confident we can reactivate the habitat.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:835`

## cstorms.1280.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1280.name&lang=en)

### 中文

手动超控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Manual Override

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:836`

## cstorms.1280.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1280.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，我们之前的估计过于乐观了：翻译出来的手册和这个控制系统一样令人无法理解。不仅缺失了一半的页面，图片说明上有着大量的符号学设定，而且另一半手册则是用一种难以破译的象形文字书写的。\n\n然而，最后一页展示了一个通讯频率并附有一组可翻译成“帮助台”的字符。我们现在正尝试联络这个频率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Unfortunately, our previous estimation appears to have been optimistic: the manual turned out to be just as incomprehensible as the control system. Half of the pages appeared to be missing and the pictorial instructions make significant semiotic assumptions. The other half of the manual is written in difficult to interpret hieroglyphs.\n\nHowever, the last page displayed a com frequency accompanied by a set of characters translated to 'help desk'. We're attempting this frequency now.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:837`

## cstorms.1285.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1285.name&lang=en)

### 中文

您的来电对我们很重要

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Your Call is Important To Us

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:838`

## cstorms.1285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

手册中的通讯频道连接到了一个名为“天空花园”的商业实体的帮助台主机。\n\n助手人工智能用一种友好而中立的语气告诉我们，它感谢我们的联系，并询问是否有什么可以帮得上忙的。列出的选项包括“回答有关订阅的问题”、“了解新的智能防御系统”，以及我们是否希望更多地了解该公司的“社会责任倡议”。\n\n然而，随后的选项把我们引向越来越不相关的话题。更糟的是，它还经常会让我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]等待，然后切断通讯。显然，这个人工智能服务并没有像居住站的其他部分一样经受住时间的考验。\n\n无论如何，我们将继续按自己的方法来试着搞定这个神秘的系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

The manual's com frequency connected us to the help desk mainframe of a commercial entity known as Skygarden.\n\nWith a friendly though neutral tone, the help desk AI informed us that it appreciated our reaching out, and asked if there was anything it could aid us with. Among the listed options were 'answering questions about subscriptions', 'learning about the new line of smart defense systems' and if we wished to know more about the corporation's 'social responsibility initiatives'.\n\nHowever, the subsequent options continued to direct us to entirely unrelated topics. Worst of all, it would often put our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] on hold only to drop the call. Obviously, the service AI has not survived the passing of time as well as the rest of the habitat.\n\nNevertheless, we will continue trying to find our way through this arcane system.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:839`

## cstorms.1290.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1290.name&lang=en)

### 中文

选项迷宫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

Maze of Options

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:840`

## cstorms.1290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]汇报，无论他们和人工智能说什么，在几分钟的等待过去后，这个系统都只会复读之前的内容。\n\n我们尝试运行多个模拟来绘制精确的菜单树，但是却发生了递归错误，并且无法同时解决和检查解决方案。即使运行量子模型也没有得到理想的结果。我们怀疑在这个过程中我们不小心破坏了一些波函数。\n\n我们不知所措了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] report that regardless of what they said to the AI, after passing through several menus, the system would simply revert back to a previous statement.\n\nWe attempted to run several simulations to map out the exact menu tree, but our efforts returned recursive errors and an inability to both solve and check the solution at the same time. Even running a quantum model achieved no desirable results. We suspect we accidentally collapsed a few wave functions in the process.\n\nWe're at a loss.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:841`

## cstorms.1290.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1290.a&lang=en)

告诉这个人工智能它的§R违禁词§!被§R违禁词§!了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

Tell the AI it's §RCENSORED§! and §RCENSORED§!.

## cstorms.1295.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1295.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

终于有进展了！这个人工智能警告我们用语不当，并告知我们应该等待客户服务代表的联系。\n\n$cstorms.1295.desc.followup$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

Finally a breakthrough! The AI admonished us for using inappropriate language and stated we should wait until we are contacted by a customer service representative.\n\n$cstorms.1295.desc.followup$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:846`

## cstorms.1295.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1295.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

终于有进展了！这个人工智能显然对于我们长时间的沉默感到困惑不解，并将会把我们转给客服代表。\n\n$cstorms.1295.desc.followup$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

Finally a breakthrough! The AI was apparently confused at our prolonged silence and will refer us to a customer service representative.\n\n$cstorms.1295.desc.followup$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:847`

## cstorms.1295.desc.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1295.desc.c&lang=en)

### 中文

终于有进展了！这个人工智能警告我们正在虐待操作面板，并告知我们应该等待客户服务代表的联系。\n\n$cstorms.1295.desc.followup$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

Finally a breakthrough! The AI admonished us for abusing the control panel and stated we should wait until we are contacted by a customer service representative.\n\n$cstorms.1295.desc.followup$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:848`

## cstorms.1295.desc.followup

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1295.desc.followup&lang=en)

### 中文

我们等了一阵子之后，通讯频道又打开了。另一端的实体介绍她自己为天空花园公司的记录管理员。服务台本身是她从上一位记录管理员那里继承来的人工智能程序。据她所知，自从几十年前天空花园破产以来，这个通讯站就再没有使用过了。当我们问她为什么继续为一个破产的机构工作时，她只说这就是她的合同内容。\n\n向记录管理员阐明了我们的情况后，她翻出了一本古老的居住站快速入门指南。她很快就会把书页的数字印痕传送给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

For a while we were left waiting, and then the com line was reopened. The entity on the other end introduced herself as the record keeper of the Skygarden Corporation. The help desk itself was an AI program she had inherited from the last record keeper. As far as she knows, the com station had not been used since the bankruptcy of Skygarden many decades ago. When we questioned why she remained working for a bankrupt organization, she simply said it was in her contract.\n\nExplaining our situation, the record keeper dug up an old quick start guide to the habitat. She will transfer digital impressions of the pages to us soon.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:849`

## cstorms.1300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

功亏一篑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

Years to Earn, Seconds to Lose

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:850`

## cstorms.1300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有了新的指南后，重新启动居住站毫不费力。\n\n在机器启动的声音慢慢平息后，我们决定再一次向记录员表示感谢。\n\n她说在这个通讯台找到了一些信息，显然，这是五个多世纪前建造的原型，但在一场特别恶劣的宇宙风暴之后，迷失于混沌太空之中。财产损失和开发成本的打水漂就够让天空花园破产的了。\n\n帮助我们是这位记录员多年来第一件有意义的服务。\n\n“再说了，”她说。“我也很高兴能再次听到我曾祖母的声音，即使只是一个人工智能而已。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

With the new guide in our possession, restarting the habitat required little effort.\n\nAfter the noise of activating machinery slowly died down, we decided to reach out to the record keeper once more to thank her.\n\nShe told us she had recovered some information on this station. Apparently it had been built over five centuries ago as a prototype, but was lost in the astrological chaos following a particularly nasty cosmic storm. The loss of property and development costs was enough to bankrupt Skygarden.\n\nHelping us was the record keeper's first useful task in years.\n\n"Besides," she said. "It was nice to hear my great-grandmother's voice on the line again, even it was just an AI."

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:851`

## site_repowered_complex

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_repowered_complex&lang=en)

### 中文

设施复工

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

Repowered Complex

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:854`

## site_repowered_complex_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_repowered_complex_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

紧随着最近过境的§Y$electric_storm$§!，在§H[From.Planet.GetName]§!的地表之下探测到了一场难以解释的能量骤增。进一步扫描发现了一座古老的地下复合体，其中的发电机被风暴重新激活，让这座设施又开始了运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

Following the passing of a recent §Y$electric_storm$§!, an unexplained power surge was detected beneath the surface of §H[From.Planet.GetName]§!. Further scans revealed an aging subterranean complex whose power generators were reactivated by the storm, bringing the facility back online.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:855`

## cstorms.1845.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1845.name&lang=en)

### 中文

严阵把守

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

Fortified Entrance

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:856`

## cstorms.1845.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1845.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[From.Planet.GetName]§!上遗弃设施的入口建设得格外地坚固。显然非常地耐用，外表铺着有厚重的装甲、辐射护盾和一座气象传感阵列。§H[Leader.GetName]§!相信它是用来抵御极端条件的。\n\n扫描表明，通过数道密封的安全门之后可以抵达一座巨大的地下舱室。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

The entrance of the isolated complex on §H[From.Planet.GetName]§! is of exceptionally robust construction. Clearly built to last, its exterior features heavy armor plating, radiation shields, and an array of atmospheric sensors. §H[Leader.GetName]§! believes it was built to withstand extreme conditions.\n\nScans suggest that a large underground chamber can be accessed via a series of sealed security doors.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:857`

## cstorms.1850.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1850.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生存庇护所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

Survival Shelter

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:858`

## cstorms.1850.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1850.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在进入主要舱室后，§H[Leader.GetName]§!得出结论，我们发现了一座很久以前为某些未知有机物种建造的尚未启用的生存避难所。\n\n它配备了储备充足的居住区，包括睡眠舱、休闲区和医疗舱。每层楼的储物柜里都有大量未开封的营养包、电池和工具。这座隐藏的洞穴里的所有东西都处于未使用的状态。\n\n最后一扇安全门仍然密封这着，我们相信打开它便会到达基地的指挥室。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

Having gained access to the main interior, §H[Leader.GetName]§! concludes that we have discovered an unused survival shelter, built long ago for some unknown organic species.\n\nIt is outfitted with well stocked habitation quarters comprising sleep pods, leisure zones, and a medical bay. Storage lockers on every floor hold deep reserves of unopened nutrient packs, batteries, and tools. Everything contained in this hidden vault is in pristine condition.\n\nOne final set of security doors remains sealed. We believe they will lead us to the facility's command deck.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:859`

## cstorms.1850.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1850.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续下去。

### English

Continue.

## cstorms.1855.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1855.name&lang=en)

### 中文

豪华避难所套餐

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

The Deluxe Shelter Package

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:861`

## cstorms.1855.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1855.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座舱室的指挥面板上有一组令人印象深刻的§Y$default_storm$§!显示器，全部连接着外面的气象探测阵列。这些控制台在待机状态运行后，储存了了几个世纪以来的历史风暴数据，这将极大地促进我们对星际气象学的理解。\n\n§H[Leader.GetName]§!还找到了一台精致小巧的全息投影仪。当被激活时，它会投射出一个3D的软体类模型，从带着公司标志的庇护所地标边跑过，一边进行着推销。\n\n这个幸存的产品是否找到了买家不得而知，但它显然已经在这里闲置了很多年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

The shelter's command deck is equipped with an impressive array of §Y$default_storm$§! monitors, all fed by the atmospheric sensors on the surface. After running on standby, these consoles contain centuries' worth of historical storm data, which will greatly advance our understanding of astrometeorology.\n\n§H[Leader.GetName]§! also found a small and well-used holo-projector. When activated, it beams a 3D molluscoid figure delivering a sales pitch, running through the shelter's key features under a corporate logo.\n\nWhether this survival product ever found a buyer is impossible to say, but it has clearly remained unused here for many years.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:862`

## cstorms.1855.desc.megacorp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1855.desc.megacorp&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的营销部门已经在分析哪里出了问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

Our marketing departments are already analyzing where they went wrong.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:863`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_PREC_WM_ONE&lang=en)

### 中文

远古庇护所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

Ancient Shelter

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:869`

## STORMS_PREC_WM_ONE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_PREC_WM_ONE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们辨别出了一些隐藏于§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!地下的结构，似乎有着很悠久的历史。应该借此机会进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

We have identified several obscured subterranean structures on §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! that appear to be of considerable age. We should take this opportunity to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:870`

## cstorms.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古庇护所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

The Ancient Shelters

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:872`

## cstorms.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.GetStarName]§!恒星系内的[From.Planet.GetName]行星是一颗残破的星球，显然是某个消失在历史长河中的帝国的遗迹。这些引起我们兴趣的地下结构都差不多古老，且相对完好无损。\n\n回声共振成像显示出一座复杂的地下结构，包含储藏室和庇护所，设计用于对抗自然之力。行星表面的破坏毫无疑问是宇宙风暴的杰作，这也与这座庇护所的存在遥相呼应。\n\n然而，大量的碎片让我们难以进一步调查。进行考古学发掘才能让我们得到进一步的收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

The planet §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! in the [From.GetStarName] system is a ruined world, apparently a remnant of an empire lost to antiquity. The subterranean structures that were of interest to us are equally ancient, and relatively intact.\n\nSonar resonance imaging revealed a complex underground structure of storage rooms as well as shelters designed to withstand the forces of nature. The destruction on the planet`s surface is no doubt a result of cosmic storms, which would align with the existence of the sanctuary.\n\nHowever, the amount of debris is making it hard to investigate any further. An archaeological dig site could tell us more.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:873`

## site_weathermanipulators_the_ancient_storm_shelters

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_the_ancient_storm_shelters&lang=en)

### 中文

远古庇护所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

The Ancient Shelters

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:876`

## site_weathermanipulators_the_ancient_storm_shelters_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_the_ancient_storm_shelters_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在一颗被风暴毁灭的星球中埋葬于废墟之下的庇护所：外部的残破使其难以调查，但内部似乎还是完好的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

A shelter buried under the debris of a storm-ravaged world; the devastation is making it hard to investigate, but the interior appears intact.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:877`

## cstorms.110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

于风暴中迎接毁灭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

From Dust till Devastation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:878`

## cstorms.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从地面接近避难所时，发现两座倒塌塔楼的下面有一个小开口，里面发出点点微光，透过这个开口可以看到一些机器正在运转。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]将这个开口作为基点，进而测定整个复杂设施的大小，并尽可能在不破坏其脆弱结构的前提下找到打开它的方法。\n\n这一被掩埋的废墟上布满了宇宙风暴肆虐的典型痕迹。奇怪的是，没有迹象表明这座城市的居民进行自保避难。\n\n似乎他们尽可能长时间地忍受着风暴的肆虐，然后匆忙撤离。或许真正的答案就藏在§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!的地下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Approaching the shelter from ground-level revealed a small opening beneath two collapsed towers, through which the faint glow of operational machinery could be seen.\n\nThe opening provides an anchor point for our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] to map out the size of the complex, and possibly find a way to open it up without damaging the delicate structural integrity.\n\nThe ruins that entomb this site have all the hallmarks of cosmic storm damage. Curiously, there are no signs that the city's inhabitants attempted to protect themselves.\n\nIt is as if they endured the violence for as long as possible, only to evacuate in a great hurry. Perhaps the answers lie beneath the ruined ground of §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:879`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

明白了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

Understood.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:880`

## cstorms.115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

双塔之下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

Beneath the Towers

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:881`

## cstorms.115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在清理了多层碎片和灰尘之后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]终于把塔楼下的开口挖得足够大，可以让他们进入避难所。\n\n这间避难所简单且实用，有许多附属房间，其中大多数都是空的。事实上，它虽然看上去简陋，但看起来确实是某种避难所，然而却无法抵御宇宙风暴。墙壁太薄，几乎没有支撑，天花板和地板也没有加固。这个避难所之所以能够保存下来，纯粹是因为运气好。如果不是因为上面的两座塔楼和山体滑坡让整个建筑群整体产生了位移，这个“风暴”避难所早就被毁灭了。\n\n从外面看到的微光来自一些故障的报警系统，在避难所深处还可以看到几个仍在运行的终端。这可能是了解该设施从前的居民以及相关异常现象的绝佳机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

After clearing many layers of debris and dust, the opening underneath the towers grew large enough for our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] to enter the shelter.\n\nThe sanctuary is simple and utilitarian, with many offshoot rooms, most of them empty. In fact, it seems overly simple. It definitely appears to be a shelter of some kind, but it would not hold up against cosmic storms. The walls are too thin, providing very little support, and the ceilings and floors are not reinforced. It has been saved by nothing less than pure luck. Had it not been for the two towers above and the landslide moving the complex in one fell swoop, this 'storm' shelter would have been eradicated long ago.\n\nThe glow seen from the outside stems from a few malfunctioning alarm systems, and several operational terminals can be spotted deeper into the shelter. This could be a prime opportunity to learn more about the previous owners of this facility, as well as the curious circumstances at play.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:882`

## cstorms.120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

老旧抄本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

Timeworn Transcriptions

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:883`

## cstorms.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些终端比我们想象中更难摸到，部分被塌方阻挡。一些碎石是新掉落的，这表明避难所在被扰动后出现了严重的结构问题。建议小心行事。\n\n不幸的是，这些古老的数据很难解码。所有的记录都是一种现已失传的语言写成的，不过界面中留下的星际通讯日志可能会对我们有所帮助。通过将这些日志与散落在避难所周围的废弃实体介质结合起来，我们应该能够完成基础翻译。此外，只要避难所不倒塌，还有更多的房间可以调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

The terminals were harder to reach than anticipated, partially obstructed by cave-ins. Some of the rubble was more recent, indicating a critical failure with the shelter now that it has been disturbed. Caution is advised.\n\nUnfortunately, the ancient data processors do not give up their secrets easily. Everything was recorded in a lost language, though the intergalactic communication logs left behind in the interface might be able to help us. By combining this with the abandoned physical media scattered around the shelter, we should be able to complete a basic translation. Additionally, there are still more rooms to investigate, so long the shelter does not collapse.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:884`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.120.a&lang=en)

## cstorms.125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

资产保险

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

Insurance of Assets

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:888`

## cstorms.125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

位于更下面的房间不像入口通道那么荒芜。一箱箱的商品包装整齐，从上到下堆得满满当当。初步翻译说明这些是原材料和未完成的电子元件，而这两者都不是生存所必需的。这份清单还详细列出了这里储存的物品数量，以及它们在星系平等贸易网络（简称为因内特）中预计会产生的利润。\n\n他们对这个庞大的交易所不吝褒美之辞，还有一丝不苟的交易记录和逐项的利润指数。事实上，这个消失的文明似乎是就以这个交易所命名的，称自己为因内特贸易体。\n\n这个避难所并不是为了保护普通民众而建的，而是一坐美化的商品仓库。这就提出了一个问题，为什么他们的避难所设备如此糟糕，无法应对风暴?\n\n进一步的翻译应该能带来更多见解，但我们的时间有限，基础设施正在令人不安地隐约发出崩塌的声响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

The rooms and chambers located further down are not nearly so abandoned as the entryway. Boxes of merchandise fill them from top to bottom, all neatly packaged and stacked. Initial translations describe unprocessed materials and unfinished electrical components, neither of which are imperative to survival. The list also details quantities of items at this location, and the projected profits they would have generated on something called the Intergalactic Network of Equal Trade, INET for short.\n\nThe language surrounding this vast trading exchange is almost ecstatic, with meticulous notes on transactions and itemized indexes of profit. In fact, it appears this vanished civilization based their name on the exchange, calling themselves the Inetian Traders.\n\nThis shelter was not built to protect the general population, but rather as a glorified commodities warehouse. This raises the question as to why their shelters were so woefully under-equipped to handle the storms?\n\nFurther translation should provide more insights, but we are on borrowed time, as evidenced by the unsettling sound of creaking infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:889`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

市场策划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

Marketplace Machinations

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:893`

## cstorms.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

撕裂和崩塌的声音愈发强烈了。尽管如此，在§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!的庇护所内，翻译工作仍在进行着。虽然对库存商品的调查没有得出什么结果，但功能正常的终端揭示了很多信息。\n\n一组混乱的坐标指向了位于附近行星上的一座“强大”设备的位置。据我们所知，这个装置拥有“操纵”宇宙风暴的能力，如果这种说法是真的，那真是令人难以置信。\n\n然而，由于避难所的稳定性越来越差，而且坐标的副本已经传送回了在轨飞船，所以我们就决定撤离现场。仍有许多问题尚未得到解答，搜寻工作仍会继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

The sound of tearing and cracking has intensified. Nevertheless, translation efforts continue within the shelter on §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!. While the stockpiled merchandise proved fruitless as a source of inquiry, the functioning terminals revealed much.\n\nA set of scrambled coordinates point to the location of a device of 'great power' located on a nearby planet. From what we can understand, this device possessed the ability to "manipulate" a cosmic storm, a claim which would be incredible if true.\n\nHowever, with the waning stability of the shelter and a copy of the coordinates already transmitted to our vessel in orbit, we evacuated the site. Many questions remain unanswered, but the hunt is on.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:894`

这是个不容错过的好机会。

### English

## INETIAN_PROJECTS_01_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINETIAN_PROJECTS_01_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!的庇护所崩塌之前发现了一组受干扰的坐标。我们有理由相信其中的一个坐标是指向一台因内特贸易体用于操纵宇宙风暴的机器。这可是一项值得去找到的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

A set of scrambled coordinates were recovered from §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§! before the shelter collapsed. We have reason to believe one of these might lead to a machine the Inetian Traders used to manipulate cosmic storms. This could be tech worth the cost to find.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:899`

## cstorms.135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

富饶星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

Bountiful System

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:902`

## cstorms.135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.GetScientistPlural]很幸运地从§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上崩塌的避难所中找到了一组受干扰的坐标。这些数据已经得到了修复，并且得出了三个可供我们探索的位置：\n\n§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!、§Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!还有§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!。进一步的分析表明，每颗行星都在因内特贸易网络中扮演了重要的角色，重要到值得记叙在§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上的庇护所里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

Luckily our [Root.GetScientistPlural] made it out from the collapsing shelter on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§! with a scrambled set of coordinates. This data has since been restored, and has resulted in three locations for us to explore:\n\n§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!, §Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!, and §Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!. Further analysis indicates that each planet served a critical role in the Inetian Trader network, one important enough to be chronicled in the shelter on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:903`
