## wm_home_system_poi_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awm_home_system_poi_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

回收的坐标指向同一个恒星系内的三座发掘基址，每一处都曾经对因内特贸易网络至关重要。派遣科研船进行发掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

The recovered coordinates lead to three dig sites within a single system, each once vital to the Inetian Trader network. Send science ships to excavate them.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:906`

## cs_inetian_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_inetian_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

先驱者——因内特贸易体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Precursors - The Inetian Traders

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:909`

## cs_inetian_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_inetian_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一座远古庇护所的残骸，它属于一个兴盛于数百万年前的不存在于我们任何记录中的文明，仿佛就像被精心地从历史中抹除了一般。如果进行调查，也许就能搞明白个中缘由。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

We have found the remnants of an ancient shelter, belonging to an civilization that thrived millions of years ago - a civilization that appears nowhere else in our records, as if it was meticulously scrubbed from history. If we investigate, we might be able to find out why.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:910`

## site_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesA_Residential_Planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesA_Residential_Planet&lang=en)

### 中文

坐标A：殖民星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

Coordinates A: Colonized Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:913`

## site_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesA_Residential_Planet_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesA_Residential_Planet_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在远古风暴庇护所中发现的三个坐标之一将我带到了§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!。地表的废墟绵延无尽，显然这颗行星曾经拥有许多庞大的城市中心。这很可能是数百万年前因内特贸易体的公民居住的地方。为什么这些坐标要被安全地存储在一个风暴庇护所里仍需调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

One of the three coordinate sets found in the ancient storm shelter has led us to §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!. Given the extent of the ruins, it's clear that this planet once boasted many sprawling urban centers. This is likely where the citizens of the Inetian Traders would have lived, eons ago. Why these coordinates were securely stored in a storm shelter will need to be investigated.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:914`

## cstorms.140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宗教意图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

Religious Intent

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:915`

## cstorms.140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!上的城市十不存一。大部分堪称地标性的大型建筑不仅受到了岁月的侵蚀，还有风暴的摧残。残存的建筑在特点上与在§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上看到的一致，也就是说，尽管有充分的证据表明存在着风暴的肆虐，但明显缺乏灾害应对准备。彻底的破坏让我们很难找到有用的东西，但希望不是完全不可能。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]在着陆点附近确定了三个兴趣点：一处是相对完整的公共服务结构，一座是类似寺庙的建筑，还有一个是巨大的中央广场。这应该是继续挖掘因内特贸易体秘密的好地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

There is not much left of the cities on §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!. Besides the passage of time, most of the impressive structures that dot the landscape have been ravaged by storms. What architectural features remain are consistent with those seen on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!, namely a distinct lack of disaster response preparedness despite ample evidence for storm violence. The thorough destruction will make it difficult to find anything useful, but hopefully not impossible.\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] have determined three points of interest near the landing site: a relatively intact public service structure, a temple of some kind, and a massive central square. These should provide a good place to continue digging into the Inetian Traders' secrets. 

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:916`

## cstorms.145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

经济核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

Economic Epicenter

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:917`

## cstorms.145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多个小组分头去调查这三处最有希望的地点。虽然公共服务设施和广场作为文化和建筑的典例很有意思，但只有关于信仰的地方特别值得注意。\n\n它摇摇欲坠的外墙里隐藏着一座雄伟的大厅，里面有521个计算终端被排列成一个同心圆，在这个同心圆的中心放置着一个令人印象深刻、功能齐全的恒星系统模型。模型中灵敏的纳米组件与行星的电磁场协调一致。尽管这个模型的历史令人难以置信地悠久，但它仍然一丝不苟地反映着整个恒星系的天体运动。\n\n此外，沿着它的圆形底座周围可以看到每一个终端位上都运行着一个交互界面。该界面的图片元素中包含了公司实体和股票价值的列表。看来这不是一个宗教崇拜的地方，而是一个因内特交易中心和证券交易所。\n\n想必这座建筑中一定储存着重要的信息，我们应该能找到些什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

### English

Multiple teams fanned out to investigate the three most promising locations. While the service structure and public square were interesting as cultural and architectural examples, only the place of reverence was particularly noteworthy.\n\nIts crumbling facade conceals a cavernous hall. Within, 521 computation terminals are arranged in concentric circles, at the exact center of which rests an impressive - and fully functional - model of this star system. Sensitive nano components within the model are attuned to the planet's electromagnetic fields. Despite its incredible age, the model remains an accurate representation of this system's celestial objects.\n\nFurthermore, along its circular base runs an interface visible from every terminal position. Burned into the picture elements of this interface is a list of corporate entities and stock values. This is not a place of religious reverence, but rather an INET trading hub and stock exchange.\n\nIt is logical to conclude that this building once housed important data. It ought to be found.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:918`

参考手册

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

Book of Reference

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:920`

## cstorms.150.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.150.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在更全面的调查后发现，§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!上的这个交易中心被洗劫一空了。终端全部无法操作，除了因长年显示而已经被烙印在显示屏上的图像以外没有残留下任何贸易信息。\n\n然而，藏在大厅一角的一个存储单元里似乎藏有用户手册。利用我们在§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!挖掘过程中开发的基本翻译秘钥，我们对这些手册进行了粗略的解释。\n\n手册里有操作终端的说明、调整系统模型的说明，以及一台被称为局部扰动抑制器的机器的帮助说明。关于如何操作、瞄准和激活这台机器的细节倒是描写得很详细。\n\n这一定是远古庇护所里提到的那座装置，也或许就是我们要找的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

After more thorough investigation it has become evident that the trading hub on §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! has been stripped bare. The terminals are inoperable, and besides the image burned into the model's interface, no trading information is left to be found.\n\nHowever, a storage unit tucked away in one corner of the hall contained what appear to be user manuals. With the rudimentary translation keys developed during our excavation on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!, a rough interpretation of these manuals has been made.\n\nThere are instructions for operating the terminals, for tuning the system model, and for calling in assistance from a machine dubbed the Sectoral Disturbance Depressor. The details on how to operate, aim, and activate this machine are explicit.\n\nThis must be the device mentioned in the ancient storm shelter, and may be exactly what we are looking for.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:921`

### English

we are one step closer to understanding the intentions of the Inetian Traders, and perhaps harnessing the power of this machine for ourselves.

## site_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesB_Industrial_Planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesB_Industrial_Planet&lang=en)

### 中文

坐标B：工业行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

Coordinates B: Industrial Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:930`

## site_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesB_Industrial_Planet_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesB_Industrial_Planet_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在远古风暴庇护所中发现的三个坐标之一将我带到了§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!，这是因内特贸易体的一颗工业星球。从其上现存的建筑来看，这颗行星显然不是用来居住的。然而，我们仍不能确定其上的工厂究竟是用于生产什么的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

One of the three coordinates found in the ancient storm shelter has led us to §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!, an industrial world from the Inetian Traders' empire. Based on the existing architecture, this was clearly not a residential planet. However, it remains uncertain what exactly its industrial might was meant to produce.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:931`

## cstorms.155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工业效率

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

Industrial Efficiency

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:932`

## cstorms.155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

座座工厂的上空依旧萦绕着遮天蔽日的浓厚云雾，让§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!的地表也变得难以看清。在遭受宇宙风暴的摧残之后，这颗星球的大部分地表满目疮痍，鳞次栉比的工业地景被巨大的裂隙赫然割开。\n\n这些产出设施没有任何要被尝试拆除的迹象，只是简单地被遗弃于此，其中甚至依旧还保有生产用的原材料。对此进行分析可以让我们理解这颗行星在这个远古文明的工商业基础设施中到底扮演着什么样的角色。它也许还能告诉我们关于§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上所谓著名机械的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

It was difficult to see the surface of §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! through the thick smog still circling above its factories. Much of the planet's surface has been ravaged by cosmic storm damage, leaving large crevices in the homogenous industrial landscape. One of these presented a perfect landing opportunity for our ship.\n\nThere is no sign that any attempt was made to dismantle these production facilities. They were simply left behind, and many still contain production material. Analyzing this may allow us to understand what purpose the planet served within the ancient civilization's mercantile infrastructure. It might also tell us more about that celebrated machine referenced on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:933`

## cstorms.160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

关键运输

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Conveying the Critical

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:934`

## cstorms.160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!上所使用的工业原料的初步分析发现其中含有高度爆炸性的组分。这些产品是某种更大流程的一部分，并不能单独使用。这些物质最终通过一个传送带系统运送到一个中央仓库。\n\n从周围的烧灼痕迹来看，这个仓库很明显经常用于行星际货运。现在问题是这些产品要交付到哪里去？鉴于这些货物的爆炸性，收货地址应该不会很远。\n\n我们在附近还发现了几个集装箱。他们显然为了能安全地打包里面的内容物花费了很多功夫，警告标签的残余物依旧还印在集装箱的侧面。标签上只能辨识出一个单词：“关键”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

Early analysis of the industrial materials at use on §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! indicate a highly volatile makeup. These products were part of a larger process, and not to be used on their own. The substances were eventually transported through a system of conveyors towards a centralized collection depot.\n\nFrom the surrounding scorch marks, it's clear that this depot was in constant use by interplanetary transport. The question is where was the product being delivered? Given the explosive nature of the cargo it could not have been far.\n\nNearby we discovered several shipping crates. Pains were clearly taken to securely package the contents, as the remnants of warning labels remain printed on the sides. Only one word is still legible: "Critical."

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:935`

## cstorms.165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缺失一环

### English

Missing Link

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:936`

## cstorms.165.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]很快就拆解了这个审慎封装的集装箱。\n\n在打开第一个集装箱时发出了一阵强烈的嘶嘶声，说明其中含有一个带气候控制系统的内室。这顿时引起了一阵关于内容物会不会失稳的恐慌。幸运的是它们完好无损。\n\n仔细检查发现里面是一个精心制作、小心安放的机器部件，里面还含有一些工厂生产的爆炸性物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] quickly dismantled the precariously packaged crates.\n\nUpon opening the first crate, a strong hiss denoted the presence of a climate controlled inner chamber, prompting a momentary panic that the contents might destabilize. Fortunately, they remained undamaged.\n\nCloser inspection revealed a wonderfully crafted and carefully placed machine component, inside of which was a quantity of the volatile material produced by the factory.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:937`

## cstorms.165.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n为此，我们会把这个部件带回船上进行进一步分析。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]可没必要在§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!的严酷地表上待那么长时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:797`

### English

\n\nAs such, we will bring the part aboard for further study. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] should not spend more time on the harsh surface of §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! than necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:938`

## cstorms.165.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\n集装箱内的运单上写着“局部扰动抑制器”。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]并不确定这到底是个什么东西，但根据这个重型集装箱和外面贴的警告来看，一定是件重要的物品。$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\nA shipping label on the inside of the crate mentions something called 'Sectoral Disturbance Depressors.' Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] are unsure as to what this means, but given the heavy duty storage container and the warnings plastered on the outside of the crate, it must have been something important.$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:939`

## cstorms.165.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\n这个部件一眼看上去就相当眼熟，乘员们拿出在§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!上发现的手册进行了对比后发现，集装箱里精心制造的部件就是局部扰动抑制器的一个关键零件。$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\nThe part immediately looked familiar, and the crew retrieved the manual found on §Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§! to compare. The intricately manufactured object within the crate is a vital component to the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors.$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:940`

## cstorms.165.desc.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.desc.c&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\n其独特的形状和充满液体的核心表明这应该就是完成§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!安装的那个部件。其中的物质想必就是用来为局部扰动抑制器充能的。然而，看起来想要安全地装上这个部件需要专门的知识。$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\nIts unique shape and liquid-filled core revealed that this might be the exact part needed to complete the installation on §Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!. The substance within must be used to power the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors. However, it appears it will require specific knowledge to install it safely.$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:941`

## cstorms.165.desc.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.165.desc.d&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\n似乎这就是§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!上的局部扰动抑制器所缺失的部件。依据在§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!发现的手册，我们应该可以正确地进行安装，并且完成激活。$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

$cstorms.165.intro$\n\nThe part seems to be the missing piece from the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors on §Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!. With the manual found on §Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!, we will be able to properly install it and perhaps finally activate it.$cstorms.165.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:942`

## site_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesC_Barren_Planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesC_Barren_Planet&lang=en)

### 中文

坐标C：荒芜行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

Coordinates C: Barren Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:946`

## site_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesC_Barren_Planet_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_CoordinatesC_Barren_Planet_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在远古风暴庇护所中发现的三个坐标之一将我带到了§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!。它的地表除了积雪和灰尘以外几乎什么也没有，初步扫描发现这颗行星曾一度是宜居的。不知这颗已经化为废墟的行星在过去是否对因内特贸易体有重要意义呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

One of the three coordinates found in the ancient storm shelter has led us to §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!. The surface is little more than snow and dust, but initial scans show that the planet was once habitable. Was it important to the Inetian Traders before or after its ruin?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:947`

## cstorms.170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽肆虐

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

Endless and Relentless

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:948`

## cstorms.170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽的狂风肆虐在§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!的表面上。如果这还不够具有挑战性，那么白雪覆盖的地面之下还藏着一个怪异的惊喜。\n\n在地表以下，还有一层又一层厚厚的尘土在等待着我们。在其中又发现了曾生活在这颗星球上的生命形式的痕迹。他们都是在同一时间被埋葬的，说明这是一次单一的大灭绝事件。§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!生态系统的崩溃必然导致了随后的大气变薄，带入了冰河时代，最终使这颗星球变得一片荒芜。\n\n除了沙砾和冰原，这个地方肯定还有更多的东西，但很明显，地表不会告诉我们更多秘密了。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]建议深入研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:807`

### English

Endless blizzards scour the surface of §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!. As if that wasn't challenging enough, the snow-covered landscape concealed a strange surprise.\n\nLayer upon layer of dust awaited us below. Evidence of the life forms that once inhabited this world was found within. All were entombed at the same time, suggesting a singular mass extinction event. The collapse of §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!'s ecosystems must have subsequently thinned the atmosphere, bringing about an ice age that has turned the planet into a wasteland.\n\nThere must be more to this place than grit and ice sheets, but it is clear that the surface is not going to provide more answers. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] advise searching deeper.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:949`

开始筛吧。

### English

Start sifting.

## cstorms.171.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.171.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生命之根

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:809`

### English

Roots of Life

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:951`

## cstorms.171.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.171.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]穿上了保护装备，乘坐重型抗寒机械，不久就完成了清除积雪和细尘这种简单重复的工作。很快，我们就发现了奇怪的特征，一系列如树根一般蜿蜒纵横的沟渠在整片土地上蔓延着。\n\n在这些裂缝中，我们发现了各种生物的干枯遗骸，从植物到动物，以及介于之间的所有生物，所有能从地表上找到的一切应有尽有。不知何故，这些沟渠把有机质夹在岩壁之间磨碎，以提取所有有用的营养物质，并将它们运送到一个交汇点，这一过程类似于消化。它们喷出的灰尘如今覆盖了§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!的大部分地表。\n\n这下问题就来了，这些裂缝究竟是在饲育谁，或者说什么东西呢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

Equipped with protective gear and frost-resistant heavy machinery, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] made short work of the tedious yet simple process of removing the snow and fine dust layers. Soon, strange patterns were discovered, a series of trenches spreading in a root-like pattern covering a vast swath of territory.\n\nWithin those crevices we found the desiccated remains of all sorts of organisms; everything it could pull from the planet's surface, from vegetation to wildlife and all that existed between. Somehow, the trenches ground up the organics by pulverizing them between their rocky walls, extracting any useful nutrients and transporting them to a convergence point in a process akin to digestion. The dust they spat back out now covers the majority of §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!'s landscapes.\n\nThis begs the question as to who, or what the crevices were feeding.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:952`

## cstorms.171.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.171.a&lang=en)

### 中文

找到这个源头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

Find the origin.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:953`

## cstorms.172.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.name&lang=en)

### 中文

往昔机械

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

Machines of yore

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:954`

## cstorms.172.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

地下的沟渠仿佛无边无际。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]越是深入，这些沟槽就越是密集。它们都汇聚到了§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!上仅存的一座山上。在这座山的顶上是一片不自然的高原，有三台大型的机器互相等间距地放置着。这些装置看起来应该是因内特人建造的，上面不仅写着他们的语言，而且与我们所了解的他们社会的设计相一致。相较于它们古老的历史和吹拂着高原的极端强风，这些机器看起来状况极为良好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

The network of underground trenches is vast. The further our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] went, the more concentrated these troughs became. They all converged on one of the last remaining mountains on §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!. Cresting the peak is a distinctly unnatural plateau, with three large machines placed at equal distances from one another. These appear to be Inetian in origin, marked in their language and consistent in design with what we know of their society. Despite their remarkable age and the extreme winds that whip the plateau, they remain in excellent condition.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:955`

## cstorms.172.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n无论如何，我们都找到了想要的东西。我们离重大发现仅有一步之遥了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

\n\nRegardless, we have found what we were looking for. We stand at the precipice of an incredible discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:956`

## cstorms.172.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.172.intro$这些应该就是§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上面所提到的机器。很遗憾的是，我们缺乏可以理解这些装置内部复杂的工作机制所需的知识。不仅如此，在其中一台机器的基部所发现的控制台显然也缺失了关键的部件。在一块小小的牌匾上雕刻着一行小字“局部扰动抑制器”。\n\n如果我们能够激活这些机器，就能够发现它们的用途。为此，我们需要探索剩下的坐标。$cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

$cstorms.172.intro$ They must be the machines mentioned on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!. Unfortunately, we lack the knowledge required to understand the complex inner workings of these devices. Not only that, but the control console discovered at the base of one of the machines is clearly missing vital components. Engraved on a small plaque are the words "Sectoral Disturbance Depressors."\n\nIf we can activate the machines we may finally uncover their purpose. To do so we will need to explore the remaining coordinates. $cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:957`

## cstorms.172.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.172.intro$\n\n我们在§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!上发现的手册的图示看起来与我们面前的这台装置一致，也就是说，这些就是所谓的局部扰动抑制器，而这也就是§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上面发现的风暴庇护所所提到的机械装置。\n\n说明书指出，这些机器需要某个控制面板才能操作。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]在其中一台机器的基部发现了这个控制面板，但似乎缺少了关键部件。但我们还有一组坐标还未探索，也许其中就藏着解决这个困境的方法。$cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:816`

### English

$cstorms.172.intro$ \n\nThe schematics we observed in the manual found on §Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§! seem to match the instruments in front of us, which must mean that these are the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors, and that this is the machine installation mentioned in the storm shelter found on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!.\n\nThe manual indicates that these machines require a control panel to be operated. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] spotted it at the foot of one of the machines, but it seems to be missing a vital part. However, we have one set of coordinates left unexplored, which could hold the means to solve this predicament. $cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:958`

## cstorms.172.desc.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.desc.c&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.172.intro$\n\n这一定就是§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上所提到的那台被称为局部扰动抑制器的机器！不仅如此，我们在§Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!找到的机械部件也可以轻易地装入其中一台机器基部的控制面板。\n\n然而，这个部件非常复杂且历史悠久，盲目尝试进一步修理是很危险的。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]建议在继续推进之前搜寻更多的信息。$cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:817`

### English

$cstorms.172.intro$ \n\nThese must be the machines mentioned on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!, dubbed the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors! What's more, the machine part we found on §Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§! was easily slotted into a control panel at the foot of one of the machines.\n\nHowever, any attempt at further repairs is risky given the complexity and age of the component parts. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] recommend searching for more information before proceeding. $cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:959`

## cstorms.172.desc.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.172.desc.d&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.172.intro$\n\n这些毫无疑问就是§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上所提到的机器了，同时也是§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!上的手册所阐述的对象，如今我们也知道它被称为局部扰动抑制器。我们现在需要做的就是将§Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!上找到的部件装上去，并且希望这台机器仍然能够运转。只有那样我们才能最终发现其用途。$cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:818`

### English

$cstorms.172.intro$ \n\nThese are without a doubt the machines mentioned on §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!, as well as the subjects of the manual from §Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!, now known as the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors. All that is left to do is install the part we found on §Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§! and hope the machines are still operational. Only then will we finally uncover their purpose. $cstorms.172.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:960`

是时候看看这台机器到底是做什么用的了。

### English

Time to see what the machines can do.

## INETIAN_PROJECTS_02_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINETIAN_PROJECTS_02_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§!上的三个坐标拼起了一幅古老的拼图，剩下的事就是激活局部扰动抑制器了。终于到了搞明白它的用途的时候了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:823`

### English

The three coordinates from §Y[wm_storm_shelter_planet.GetName]§! completed an ancient puzzle. All that is left is to activate the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors. The time has come to discover their purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:966`

## cstorms.176.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.176.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机械的用途

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

The Meaning of the Machine

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:967`

## cstorms.176.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.176.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一块拼图终于找到了，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]开始忙碌了起来。§Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!上的部件严丝合缝地插入了一块控制面板。§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!上的手册引导我们进行正确的安装。刚一装好，三台机器霎时间活动了起来。\n\n雷鸣般的震响振落了高原上的尘与雪，又在一声闪电般的爆鸣中一位[Root.Owner.GetScientist]瞬间蒸发了，那具躯体化作的尘埃旋涡迅速被吸进那一道道通向行业干扰抑制器的沟渠中。\n\n这就是§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!上发生的事情的关键所在：通往机器底部的沟槽是燃料管道，会吸收周围的生物质。其结果就是在整颗星球上留下毁灭的痕迹。\n\n局部扰动抑制器现已全面激活，剩下的燃料还够一次性使用。我们需要做的就是按下按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

### English

The last piece of the puzzle found, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] got to work. The component from §Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§! slotted neatly into a control console. The manual from §Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§! was employed to install it correctly. Once this was done, all three machines burst into life.\n\nThunderous vibrations shook the dust and snow from the plateau. With a crackle like that of lightning a [Root.Owner.GetScientist] was instantly vaporized, the swirl of dust that was once a body quickly sucked into the trenches towards the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors.\n\nHerein lies the key to what happened on §Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!: the trenches leading to the bottom of the machines are fuel lines, drawing in the bio-matter of their surroundings. The result is a trail of destruction across the planet.\n\nThe Sectoral Disturbance Depressors are now fully operational. There is enough fuel left for a single use. All that remains is the push of a button.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:968`

### 中文

残骸重发现

### English

Rediscovered Remnants

## cstorms.180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在揭示了局部扰动抑制器的秘密之后，关于因内特贸易体的线索似乎就中断了。然而，对数据的彻底重新评估又提供了一些新的见解。\n\n§Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§!上的贸易记录表明，这些人保有高度的平等主义信仰。这个结论的依据是他们拥有很高的生活标准以及行星上留存的大量奢侈品工厂；每位公民，无论其职业、社会地位或血统如何，都享有平等的舒适和机会。\n\n在§Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!上，我们发现了详细描述每个工厂用例的索引。这些都是更大的生产链的一部分，用于为这个庞大的帝国提供必要的手段来维持其富裕的生活方式。要公平地分配这些利益，就必须以惊人的速度消耗大量的材料和食物。\n\n§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!上修复好的行业干扰抑制器在完全报废之前又提供了更多的洞见。虽然其功能的确切目的仍然是一个谜，但至少我们寻回了一个可追踪的数据信号；这些机器的目标不是一个自由运动的实体。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]建议在附近的区域寻找与这个信号相匹配的反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

After uncovering the secrets of the Sectoral Disturbance Suppressors, the trail about the Inetian Traders' fate went cold. However, a thorough reassessment of the data provided some new insights.\n\nTrading records from §Y[wm_digsite_A.GetName]§! demonstrate that these people held strong egalitarian beliefs. This conclusion is backed by the high standard of living and plentiful luxury facilities still left on the planet; with a small degree of variance, every citizen enjoyed an equal share of comfort and opportunity, no matter their profession, social standing, or lineage.\n\nOn §Y[wm_digsite_B.GetName]§!, we found indexes detailing the use case of each factory. These were all part of much larger production chains, set up to provide the vast empire with the necessary means to support its opulent lifestyle. Equal distribution of these benefits necessitated a truly staggering amount of material and food consumption, produced at breakneck speeds.\n\n§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!'s repaired Sectoral Disturbance Depressors provided some more insights before being fully decommissioned. While the exact purpose of their function is still a mystery, a traceable data signal was recovered; the machines were not aimed at a freely moving entity.\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] recommend searching nearby sectors for a matching response to this signal.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:974`

找找这个东西。

信号来源

The Signal's Source

## wm_storm_formation_poi_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awm_storm_formation_poi_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对扰动的分析结果指向了一颗遥远的星球。派遣一艘科研船去发掘遗址有望最终揭示因内特贸易体瞄准的目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

Our analysis traced the disturbance to a distant world. A science ship sent to excavate the site may finally reveal what the Inetian Traders were targeting.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:978`

## site_weathermanipulators_storm_formation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_storm_formation&lang=en)

### 中文

平凡起源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:835`

### English

Origins of the Pervading

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:981`

## site_weathermanipulators_storm_formation_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_storm_formation_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在局部扰动抑制器的控制台中保存着一个数据信号。虽然有些难度，但我们能复制它的模式，并且在全星系中搜寻匹配。航迹引向了一颗不可宜居的星球。是时候弄明白因内特贸易体瞄准着什么，以及为什么瞄准了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

Saved within the command console of the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors was a data signal. Though difficult, we were able to clone its pattern and search the galaxy for a match. The trail led to an uninhabited planet. It is time to discover what the Inetian Traders were targeting, and why.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:982`

## cstorms.181.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.181.name&lang=en)

### 中文

朴素平原

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

Unadorned Plains

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:983`

## cstorms.181.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.181.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

乍看上去§Y[From.Planet.GetName]§!似乎一切正常。它和星系中随处可见的数百颗其他同类星球别无二致，也没有发现这个信号具体是从什么地方发出来的指示。没有可见的信号发射机，也没有足够强大的技术痕迹，可以从其他系统接收到它的呼叫。\n\n我们的搜寻仍在继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

At first glance, nothing §Y[From.Planet.GetName]§! appears out of place. A planet like hundreds of others, found all over the galaxy. Nor is there any indication of where exactly the signal is coming from. There is no visible transmitter or piece of technology strong enough to have its call registered from systems away.\n\nWe continue our search.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:984`

## cstorms.182.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.182.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浓缩灾难

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

Concentrated Catastrophe

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:985`

## cstorms.182.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.182.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在抵达地表后，我们很快发现，收到的信号强弱取决于我们的位置。根据这个简单的规律，我们终于找到了行星另一侧的一座高耸的山峰。\n\n我们越是接近那些若隐若现的山脉，土地就变得愈发地荒芜、贫瘠而破败，仿佛有一股毁灭性的力量撕裂了大地。根据破坏的特征，我们认为这个地方曾被宇宙尘暴袭击过。然而，如果这是真的，那么应该整颗星球都受到影响才合乎逻辑。我们以前从未见过风暴会造成如此集中的破坏。要了解更多，就必须登上这座高耸的山峰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

After arriving on the surface, we soon discovered that the signal became stronger or weaker depending on our position. Following this simple pattern, we were led at last to a towering mountain range on the far side of the planet.\n\nAs we approached the looming mountains, the landscape grew ever more blasted, barren, ruined, as if a devastating force had ripped across this part of the world. Based on the damage pattern, we believe this place was at some point ravaged by a cosmic dust storm. However, if that were true, it would be logical to conclude the whole planet would be affected. Never before have we seen such concentrated destruction from such a tempest. To learn more, it will be necessary to scale the imposing peaks.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:986`

## cstorms.183.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.183.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逸散能量

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Fleeting Energy

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:987`

## cstorms.183.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.183.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被破坏的山脉由许多的山峰和山谷组成，但一个形状奇怪的陨石坑很快就引起了我们的注意。它的坑壁光滑，围绕着中心有一系列同心圆被刻在大地上。\n\n当我们下到坑里的时候，感觉到有一股强大的力量拉扯着，这股力量毫无疑问地指向了同心圆的圆心。一缕缕闪烁的能量从圆环中升起，其中一些飘得很远，最终逃离了行星的大气层。这就是信号的源头。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]建议分析陨石坑及其能量波动，以便更好地了解这里发生了什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

The ravaged mountain range consisted of many peaks and valleys, but one oddly shaped crater caught our attention very early on. The walls of the pit were smooth, and in its center a number of concentric rings were carved into the ground.\n\nAs we descended into the bowl, we began to feel the pull of a deeply compelling force, inescapable and unmistakably coming from the circle's origin point. Strands of flickering energy arose from the rings, some drifting so far off they eventually escaped the planet's atmosphere. This is the source of the signal.\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] suggest analyzing the crater and its energy fluctuations in order to better understand what happened here.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:988`

## cstorms.184.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.184.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不确定开端

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

Uncertain Beginnings

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:989`

## cstorms.184.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.184.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据环境数据和详细的模拟，我们可以有把握地得出结论，确实有一场天体沙尘暴穿过了这些山峰。事实上，它就是在这个位置上形成的！至于它为什么没有在这个过程中吞噬整颗星球，我们最好的猜测就是它只是太小了——分析表明，这场风暴的规模甚至远小于普通的风暴。\n\n基于这一事实，因内特贸易体将局部扰动抑制器称为“操纵装置”，并且这一特定风暴的能量信号被存储在他们的数据库中，看起来他们很可能试图控制宇宙风暴。通过计算它离开这个地方后的路径，我们可以学到很多对我们有利的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

Based on environmental data and detailed simulations, we can safely conclude that it was indeed a celestial dust storm which moved through these peaks. In fact, it formed in this very spot! As for why it did not swallow the entire planet in the process, our best guess is that it was simply too small - analysis describes a storm that was a fraction of the size of even an average tempest.\n\nBased on the fact that the Inetian Traders referred the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors as 'a manipulation device', and that this specific storm's energy signal was stored within their databanks, it seems likely that they were attempting to control the cosmic storm. By calculating the path it took after leaving this place, we may learn much to our advantage.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:990`

## cstorms.184.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.184.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们继续吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

Let us continue.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:991`

## INETIAN_PROJECTS_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINETIAN_PROJECTS_03&lang=en)

### 中文

追踪贸易体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

Tracing the Traders

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:994`

## INETIAN_PROJECTS_03_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINETIAN_PROJECTS_03_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一处古代星尘沙暴的形成地点，但证据表明，它比典型的风暴要小得多。鉴于它的规模之小，因内特贸易体认为有必要使用他们的局部扰动抑制器来控制风暴就显得很奇怪了。\n\n如果继续追踪风暴的路径，我们也许就能发现他们为什么在这个项目上投入这么多时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

We found the formation point of an ancient celestial dust storm, but evidence suggests it must have been much smaller than a typical disturbance. Given its size, it is curious that the Inetian Traders felt the need to control the storm using their Sectoral Disturbance Depressors.\n\nIf we track the path of the storm, we might be able to discover why they invested so much time in this project.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:995`

## cstorms.186.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.186.name&lang=en)

### 中文

追踪贸易体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

Tracing the Traders

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:996`

## cstorms.186.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.186.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

追踪星尘沙暴的路径没有我们预期的那么简单直接。仅到离起源点只有几个星系的距离，它的强度、大小和路径就发生了剧烈的变化。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]怀疑，正是在这一位置上，风暴受到了局部扰动抑制器的袭扰。它使风暴失去了平衡，从§Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!表面刮起的野生动物残骸碎片污染了风暴的内部漩涡。\n\n随着风暴继续在星系中漂移，它吞没了许多恒星系，带来了毁灭性的后果。\n\n为了干扰这个微小扰动的路径所做的一切，目的都是使其远离一个特定的恒星系……传感器探测到该恒星系中存在一座废弃的空间站。为了保护这个地方，因内特贸易体甚至不惜制造了多次大灭绝。\n\n空间站可能不是我们所希望的首都星系，但它肯定同样重要才值得代价如此高昂的保护措施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

To track the celestial dust storm's path turned out to be less straightforward than anticipated. Only a few systems away from its origin point something drastic happened to its strength, size, and course. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] suspect that it was at this point that the storm was hit with the Sectoral Disturbance Depressors. It knocked the storm off balance, and polluted its inner vortex with the pulverized remains of wildlife swept up from §Y[wm_digsite_C.GetName]§!'s surface.\n\nAs the storm continued to drift across the galaxy, it engulfed multiple systems with devastating repercussions.\n\nAll of this to alter the course of a minor disturbance away from a particular system... Sensors have detected the presence of an abandoned space station in that system. In order to protect this place, the Inetian Traders left multiple mass extinctions in their wake.\n\nThe station may not be the capital world we hoped for, but it must be equally important to warrant such measures.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:997`

设定航线。

### English

## wm_last_shelter_poi_name

废弃空间站

### English

The Abandoned Station

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1000`

## wm_last_shelter_poi_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awm_last_shelter_poi_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

信号的踪迹断在了一座因内特贸易体不惜一切代价想保护的废弃空间站上。派遣一艘科研船去发掘遗址有望揭示个中缘由。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

The signal's trail ends at an abandoned station the Inetian Traders went to ruinous lengths to protect. A science ship sent to excavate the site may reveal why.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1001`

## site_weathermanipulators_last_shelter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_last_shelter&lang=en)

### 中文

末日前夕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

Before the Fall

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1004`

## site_weathermanipulators_last_shelter_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_weathermanipulators_last_shelter_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特贸易体试图保护这个恒星系中的一座小小的空间站不受星尘沙暴的影响。而这个风暴则始料未及地发育增强并且摧毁了许多其他的恒星系。为什么他们要如此费尽心机地保护这座设施呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

The Inetian Traders tried to protect a space station within this system from a small celestial dust storm. As an unforeseen consequence, the storm grew so large several other of their systems were destroyed. Why were they so desperate to protect this facility?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1005`

## cstorms.190.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.190.name&lang=en)

### 中文

稳定轨道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

Stable Orbit

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1006`

## cstorms.190.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.190.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环绕着[From.Planet.GetName]的空间站的规模让我们始料未及。它看来不仅仅是一个“站”了，例如科研设施和复杂的生命维持系统还有极深空探测器等应有尽有。这说明这座设施很可能是用作长期殖民地而不是一个临时定居点的。\n\n然而这座空间站现在状况糟糕透了。它的壳体已经受损，灾难性的减压已经炸毁了整个区段。目前还不清楚损坏是由外部还是内部造成的。幸运的是，轨道控制和轴稳定仍然有效，这也解释了为什么这座结构没有漂移到恒星上，也没有在不可避免的自转中瓦解。\n\n很难在不破坏这种微妙平衡的情况下进入它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

The space station orbiting [From.Planet.GetName] is much larger than originally anticipated. It appears to be more than just a station, boasting research instruments as well as sophisticated life support systems and extensive deep space sensors. This may indicate the facility was used more as a long-term colony, rather than a minor waypoint.\n\nIt is in a dismal state, however. The hull has been compromised, with catastrophic decompression having blown out entire sectors. It is unclear whether the damage was caused from the outside, or from within. Fortunately, orbit control and axis stabilization are still operational, explaining why the structure has not drifted into the star or broken out in an unstoppable spin.\n\nGetting inside without destabilizing this fastidiously maintained balance will be difficult.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1007`

## cstorms.191.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.191.name&lang=en)

### 中文

印象深刻的压力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Impressive Pressure

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1008`

## cstorms.191.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.191.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

要想进到[This.System.GetName]星系的这座空间站里除非我们能先撬开一道未受损的气闸，而且还不引起气爆。尽管历史悠久，这座空间站的内部仍然是充压的，还能成功地模拟典型的行星大气。\n\n我们花了好几天的时间在自己的舰船和空间站之间构建气密系统，精心地平衡了气压，终于才能安全地打开舱门。不幸的是，我们马上就遇到了下一个困难：前方的过道被扭曲的支撑梁和附近减压产生的碎石堵塞了，我们需要一些时间清理才能继续前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

To enter the station in the [This.System.GetName] system, it was necessary to pry open an undamaged airlock without causing further implosions. The internals of the station were still pressurized, successfully mimicking a typical planetary atmosphere despite its impressive age.\n\nIt took days to create a perfect seal between our ship and the station, meticulously equalizing the air pressure. Eventually we were able to open the door safely. Unfortunately, we were immediately met with another obstacle: a blocked corridor, clogged with twisted support beams and rubble from nearby depressurization. It will take some time before we can proceed.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1009`

## cstorms.192.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.192.name&lang=en)

### 中文

内部墓室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Inner Tomb

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1010`

## cstorms.192.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.192.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在清开一条通路后，我们开始探索这座空间站寻找答案。\n\n全面的调查表明，这座空间站并不是建造用于观测太空的，而是用来保护什么东西，它的许多精心设计的扫描设备都是针对内部的。在其中还发现了属于因内特贸易体公民的个人物品，以及描述空间站生活的日志。他们谈到了一个重要的有效载荷，它被保存在基地内一个戒备森严的舱室里。虽然没花多长时间就找到了，但这个舱室宛若坟墓。\n\n近乎木乃伊般的干燥遗骸堆满了舱室，它们都来自因内特贸易体。从平静的姿势来看，他们似乎没有发生什么反抗，只是坐以待毙。这里究竟发生了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

After clearing a way forward, we began to explore the station in search of answers.\n\nA thorough investigation revealed that the station was built not to observe the cosmos, but rather to protect something, with much of its elaborate scanning equipment directed internally. Personal effects belonging to Inetian Trader citizens were found, as well as logs describing station life. They spoke of an important payload, kept in a tightly guarded chamber somewhere within the base. While it did not take long to locate, the room was revealed to be a tomb.\n\nDesiccated, near-mummified remains occupied the space, each of them members of the Inetian Traders' empire. Given their mundane positions, it seems they did not put up a fight, but simply sat down to die. What happened here?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1011`

## cstorms.193.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.193.name&lang=en)

### 中文

狂妄自大

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Hubris

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1012`

## cstorms.193.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.193.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些公民们所说的有效载荷正位于舱室的中央。这台独特设备崭新的外观看起来仿佛尚未启用一般。它绝对属于因内特人，但比我们以前看到的任何东西都先进。\n\n该装置两侧的终端显示几段反复重复的相同文本。翻译很快就出来了:\n\n§L最亲爱的访客，如果你正在读这篇文章，那么你就是我们傲慢的见证者。在我们对平等的崇高追求中，我们忘记了真正的均衡器：创造与毁灭。\n\n为了使我们的贸易路线免受宇宙风暴的破坏，我们篡改了自然。曾经的小骚乱已经超出了控制。宇宙风暴横扫了我们的许多星球。这不是巧合，而是一种报复。如今我们把自己封闭在这座空间站里，这就是最后的一道防线，因为无论做什么都太晚了。尽管我们再也不用抑制器，新的风暴仍然还在形成，而且比之前更强、更野蛮。我们最后的任务很明确。\n\n我们不知疲倦的地制造这台机器，为你们这些访客制造。它会把你从宇宙风暴中拯救出来同时避免激怒这些风暴。对我们而言死亡是一种解脱，而对你们而言还不是。§!\n\n在这个舱室里，因内特贸易体接受了自己的命运。他们的慷慨之伟大恰如他们的冒失之严重。最后，我们带着他们的礼物，离开了这座空间站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

The payload as described by the citizens was positioned squarely in the center of the room. It is a singular device, apparently brand new and unused. It is most definitely Inetian, but more advanced than anything we have seen from them before.\n\nTerminals flanking the object display a few paragraphs of the same text repeated over and over again. Translation came quickly:\n\n§LDearest Visitor, if you are reading this then you are witness to our hubris. In our noble pursuit of equality, we forgot the true equalizer: creation, and destruction.\n\nSeeking to free our trade routes from the ravages of cosmic tempests, we tampered with nature. What were once minor disturbances grew beyond influence. The cosmic storms tore through our worlds. This was not coincidence, but revenge. We sealed ourselves within this station. It was our last defense, as we were too late. Despite no longer using our depressors, new storms were forming, stronger and more savage. Our final mission was clear.\n\nFeverishly we worked to complete this machine, and it was built for you, Visitor. It will save you from the cosmic storms, without further angering them. Our demise is our absolution, it does not have to be yours.§!\n\nWithin this room the Inetian Traders sealed their fate. Their generosity was as grand as their carelessness. Leaving the station, we returned with their parting gift.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1013`

## cstorms.195.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.195.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特贸易体的最后安息地似乎是一座非常高科技的空间站。他们建造这座空间站来抵抗他们自己一手造成的恶劣宇宙风暴。这座独特的居住站是因内特科技的巅峰之作，拥有惊人的宜居控制和保护系统。但即便如此先进的居住站都没能无限期地保护它们。\n\n由于这座空间站经过千万年的衰败已经过于危险不宜居住，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]可以提取它的从系统中提取知识宝库。不仅如此，他们还在一个数据库中发现了因内特贸易体最重要的贸易路线，并在一台工作站上发现了更高效、对星球破坏更小的气候控制机器的精致原型。\n\n看来至少一部分因内特贸易体的坚持没有白费，这将继续帮助我们度过风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:875`

### English

The final resting place of the Inetian Traders turned out to be a very high-tech space station. They had built it as a refuge against the terrible cosmic storms they had brought into existence themselves. This unique habitat is a true zenith of Inetian tech, boasting incredible habitability control and protection systems. But even that could not save them indefinitely.\n\nAs such, while the station is no longer safe to inhabit due to the eons of decay, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] were able to extract a trove of knowledge from its systems. Not only that, they also found the Inetian Traders' most important trade routes stored on a databank, and delicate prototypes of more efficient, less-planet destroying climate control machines, found simply laying upon a workstation.\n\nIt seems at least some of the Inetian Traders' tenacity has prevailed, and it will continue to help us through these storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1026`

## cs_adAkkaria_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acs_adAkkaria_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要跟随一条线索去揭开关于这个怪异古老文明的终极命运的秘密，无论这条线索通向何方。

## STORMS_PREC_ONE

### English

Ancient Signals

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1046`

## STORMS_PREC_ONE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORMS_PREC_ONE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个星系中我们截获了一系列怪异的电磁信号。初步数据分析表明这些发报器也许有数千年的历史。我们的船员渴望找到它的源头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

We've picked up a series of strange electronic signals inside this system. Preliminary data shows these transmissions might be thousands of years old. Our crew is keen to find their origin.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1047`

## cstorms.205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

仁善信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

A Signal of Benevolence

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1048`

## cstorms.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个星系里探测到的奇怪信号源于一座围绕[FromFrom.GetName]的古老通讯站。这些信息应该是某个失落文明的老旧广播。\n\n因此，我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]在破译时遇到了一些困难。他们建议登上这座空间站，直接与通讯主机建立交互。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

The strange signals we've detected within this system are originating from an ancient communication station orbiting [FromFrom.GetName]. The messages appear to be old broadcasts from a forgotten civilization.\n\nAs a result, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] are having some difficulty deciphering them. They suggest boarding the station to interface with the transmission mainframe directly.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1049`

## site_adakkaria_the_propaganda_station

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakkaria_the_propaganda_station&lang=en)

## site_adakkaria_the_propaganda_station_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakkaria_the_propaganda_station_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

这座古老的通讯站仍可工作。登上这座站点并直接与其系统交互后，我们也许就能破译此前收到的广播。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

This ancient communication station is still active. By boarding the site and directly interfacing with its systems we might be able to decipher its previous broadcasts.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1053`

## cstorms.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

登上空间站的过程几乎没有遇到什么问题，但在其结构内有点难找到方向。部分得益于它外部先进的屏蔽设施，它的构造保存得非常完好，但狭窄的走廊和低矮的天花板对我们的团队来说是一个挑战。\n\n除了墙上装饰着大片的纸-塑料混合物外，前哨站的内部几乎没有提供关于其前主人本质的线索。这些海报描绘了一种未知有机物种个体的风格化图像。他们的外表引人注目，站在征服和庆祝的背景前。视觉效果显然是为了灌输某种自豪感和亲切感。\n\n通讯主机本身则简直是个谜。起初[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]们认为它是自然界的模拟物，类似于各种[Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]中常见的东西。然而，在按钮和表盘的下面却是一种用未知合金制成的复杂的电子电路。[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]们怀疑这个文明可能跳过了计算机科学发展的几个步骤，这就解释了这里存在不同时代科技的杂糅现象。\n\n尽管如此，经过一系列的尝试和错误，我们的[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]还是成功地破译了许多广播录音中的第一段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

Boarding the station caused few problems, though navigating the structure was difficult. While it is surprisingly well-preserved, in part due to the advanced composition of the external shielding, the narrow corridors and the low ceilings proved a challenge for our team.\n\nThe interior of the outpost provides few clues as to the nature of its former owners, save for the large sheets of a paper-plastic mixture decorating the walls. These posters depict stylized imagery of individuals of an unknown organic species. Their appearance is notable, standing tall and resolute before a background of conquest and celebration. The visuals are clearly designed to instill a certain sense of pride and kinship.\n\nThe transmission mainframe itself is just as much of a mystery. Initially our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] believed it to be analogue in nature, akin to what we've seen in [Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]. However, beneath the buttons and dials lay a complex arrangement of electronic circuitry, made from alloys yet unknown to us. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] suspect this civilization might have skipped several steps of development in computer science, which explains the anachronistic mix of technology.\n\nNevertheless, after a series of trial and error, our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] managed to decipher the first of many recorded broadcasts.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1055`

## cstorms.220.name

### 中文

来自崇高领袖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

From the Exalted Leadership

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1056`

## cstorms.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在与这台远古通讯主机成功连接后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]提供了第一次广播的转写：\n\n§L阿达卡利亚公约的人民们！\n\n我不是以领导的身份、而是以一位老友和导师的身份与你们说话。\n\n你们一定注意到了，白昼正在变暗，天空愈发地阴沉，甚至恒星本身也都变得充满敌意。\n\n但我是有信心的。正如我们的恩主对我们有信心一样，我对我们的目标、对你们也有信心。\n\n几个世纪以来，我们的人民保护这些星球不受野蛮部落的侵害。从这些邪恶势力手中拯救星系是我们的使命。命运不会因一次挫折而改变。我们不会被阴谋反对我们的邪恶军团所破坏。\n\n我相信我们的使命。阿达卡利亚仁善公约会为所有文明带去真理和自由。是我们的使命保住了自己的性命，是我们的命运让我们取得胜利、繁荣昌盛!\n\n因此，我的朋友们，不要屈服于怀疑之辞。要为损失而哀悼，给予烈士以荣誉。然后让我们像往常一样团结到一起。为了我们的恩主，为了我们的阿达卡利亚公约，为了仁善！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

After successfully interfacing with the ancient transmission mainframe, our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] have provided a transcript of the first broadcast:\n\n§LPeople of the adAkkaria Convention!\n\nI speak to you not as a leader, but as a friend and a mentor.\n\nIt cannot have gone unnoticed that the days are darkening and the skies are turning bitter. Even the stars themselves are hostile.\n\nBut I have faith. As our Benefactors had faith in us, I have faith in our purpose. I have faith in you.\n\nFor centuries our people protected the stars from the worst of the barbarian hordes. It is our destiny to save the galaxy from these malicious forces. And destiny is not undone by a single setback. We will not be marred by the malevolent legions conspiring against us.\n\nI believe in our mission. The adAkkaria Convention of Benevolence brings truth and freedom to all civilisations. It is our mission that preserves us. It is our fate that allows us to triumph and thrive!\n\nAnd so my fellow friends, let us not give in to doubt. Mourn our losses, yes. Honor the fallen. And then let us come together as we've always done. For our Benefactors. For the adAkkaria Convention. For Benevolence!§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1057`

### English

Unicom's Ultimatum

## cstorms.225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对我们的[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]来说，整理远古空间站上发掘出的数据是一项艰巨的任务。不过，有几档广播节目特别有信息量，其中一些包含有录制对话的片段：\n\n§S伊尔萨皮涅·图尔希 — 主播§!\n§L——说到最近来自宇宙联合体的干涉，我们请来了最高教授阿克玛尼·桑多和全感存在博士埃米克·英纳。尊敬的教授，您如何解读宇联的要求？§!\n\n§S阿克玛尼·桑多 — 最高教授§!\n§L他们的要求简直荒谬绝伦！宇联还在以极其轻蔑的态度对待欠文明星球。但我倒要问问这些自称是星系道德守护者的家伙们：扣留可以用来拯救生命的技术、扣留那些构成我们成功社会基石的理念不是更不道德吗？如果我们任由一颗星球加速走向灭亡，而不伸出援手拯救它，那我们会变得多么卑鄙？§!\n\n§S伊尔萨皮涅·图尔希 — 主播§!\n§L真是绝妙的观点！全感存在博士，我想您一定也对宇联都对我们命运的干涉而感到愤怒吧。§!\n\n§S埃米克·英纳 — 全感存在博士§!\n§L我现在要说的话可能会让你震惊。尽管这个组织非常顽固，但我认为应该考虑研究一下宇联的关——§!\n\n§T看来广播在这个时候被切断了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:902`

### English

Sorting through the data uncovered on the ancient station has proven a daunting task for our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists]. However, several broadcasts have been flagged as particularly revealing, with some providing a glimpse into recorded conversations:\n\n§SilSapne Tuerthi - Anchor§!\n§L - - and to discuss the recent interference from the Universal Community, here is Most Exalted Professor akMani Sando and Doctor of All Perceived Existence emEk Inna. Most Exalted Professor, what is your interpretation of UniCom's demands?§!\n\n§SakMani Sando - Most Exalted Professor§!\n§LThe demands are outrageous! The Universal Community continues to treat under civilized worlds with the utmost disdain. But let me ask these self-proclaimed guardians of galactic morality: is it not more unethical to withhold life-saving technologies? To withhold those ideals that form the cornerstone of our triumphant society? How vile would we become if we allowed a world to speed towards its own demise and not reach out to save it?§!\n\n§SilSapne Tuerthi - Anchor§!\n§LAll excellent points! Doctor of All Perceived Existence, I imagine you must be equally outraged at UniCom's tampering with our destiny.§!\n\n§SemEkku Inna - Doctor of All Perceived Existence§!\n§LWhat I have to say now may shock you. Despite the stubbornness of the organization, I think an effort should be made to study UniCom's concer-§!\n\n§TIt appears the broadcast was cut off at this point.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1059`

## cstorms.230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

另一条广播也标记上了极有价值。尽管这是一部戏剧性的作品，但也为我们的调查提供了一些相关的背景信息：\n\n§S阿克塔缅·瑞万§!\n§L我注意到你昨晚又拜访了恩图拉·萨曼。我还以为你为了我们的爱而抛弃了他们。§!\n\n§S因内尔卡·乌泰§!\n§L我向您保证，这只是一次礼节性的拜访。要知道我只忠于您一人！§!\n\n§S阿克塔缅·瑞万§!\n§L这不过是你的花言巧语，但你的思想却告诉我了截然不同的事情。§!\n\n§S因内尔卡·乌泰§!\n§L您也是如此！§!\n\n§S阿克塔缅·瑞万§!\n§L没错，正是如此！我恰在昨晚发现了自己的灵能天赋！我本以为这是对我们最深刻感情的愉快探索，结果却变成了绝望！你的思想中潜藏着背叛！§!\n\n§S因内尔卡·乌泰§!\n§L您不理解！我的心，我无法控制它。我必须认为这条路是正确的。哦，瑞万，要是您新获得的灵能天赋能让您读懂我的心而不是我的思想就好了！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

Another broadcast has been flagged as noteworthy. Although clearly a dramatic work, it does provide us with some useful context for our investigations:\n\n§SakTamian Riwan§!\n§LIt has come to my attention that you visited enTulla Saman again last night. I thought you had forsaken them in favor of our love?§!\n\n§SinElka Utae§!\n§LI assure you, it was merely a courteous visit. Know that I am committed to you and no one else!§!\n\n§SakTamian Riwan§!\n§LThat is what your mouth says, but your mind! It tells me something else. Something different.§!\n\n§SinElka Utae§!\n§LNo, not you too!§!\n\n§SakTamian Riwan§!\n§LYes, I too! I discovered my psionic gifts just last night! And what I imagined would be a joyful exploration of our deepest feelings became one of despair! The treason in your thoughts!§!\n\n§SinElka Utae§!\n§LYou don't understand! My mind, I cannot help it. I cannot help but see this path as rational. Oh Riwan, if only your new psionic gifts allowed you to read my heart instead of my mind!§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1064`

## cstorms.235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]也翻译了几条新闻。他们认为这几条播报非常值得关注：\n\n§L从哈利俄斯星系发回的报告呈现出越来越可怕的画面。懦弱的索尔法尼当局在恩伊普萨尔地点伏击了我们勇敢的启蒙军！尽管如此，我们的最高领袖指出，这一进程将不会中断。他说：“我们的恩主并没有放弃我们。我们也不能丢下任何发展中文明。”\n\n也有传言称，邪恶的野蛮当局和宇联代表之间可能存在合作。然而，宇联的大使否认星际共同体与任何野蛮压迫者进行了沟通，并表示这是我们最高领袖的又一个“方便偏执”的例子。\n\n但无论有没有强大的盟友，索尔法尼启蒙进程都处于危急状态。要扭转这种局面并不容易，但是-§!\n\n§T不幸的是，广播的其余部分损坏太严重，无法破译。我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]已经创建了数据库的副本。希望随着时间的推移，我们能从这个被遗忘已久的文明中恢复出更多的信息。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] have also translated several news items. They believe this broadcast in particular is worth paying attention to:\n\n§LReports coming in from the Haliios system draw an increasingly dire picture. The cowardly Solphani authority ambushed our brave Enlightenment Forces at Location enYpsal! Nevertheless, our Exalted Leadership stated that the Process will not be interrupted. As quoted: 'Our Patrons didn't give up on us. And neither shall we leave any underdeveloped civilisation behind'.\n\nRumors have also surfaced of a possible collaboration between the wicked barbarian authorities and representatives of UniCom. UniCom's ambassador however denies the interstellar community communicated with any of the barbarian oppressors and states this is yet another instance of 'convenient paranoia' from our Exalted Leadership.\n\nBut with or without powerful allies, the Solphani Enlightenment Process is in a critical state. It will not be easy to turn this around, but-§!\n\n§TUnfortunately the rest of the broadcast is too corrupted to be deciphered. Our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] have already created a copy of the database. In time, we hope to recover more information from this long forgotten civilization.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1072`

## cstorms.245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[planet_windswept_fates.System.GetName]星系中席卷而过的宇宙风暴意外地在[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]让一处可能的考古遗址重见天日。\n\n对§Y[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]§!的初步扫描表明，它的大气层极其不稳定，在巨大的行星风暴主宰着一切。我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]从来没有预料到在这样一个星球上会有文明的遗迹，相信我们可以从调查中学到很多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:919`

### English

The passing of a cosmic storm in the [planet_windswept_fates.System.GetName] system has uncovered a site of possible archeological significance on [planet_windswept_fates.GetName].\n\nPreliminary scans of §Y[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]§! indicate a devastatingly unstable atmosphere where vast planetary storms dominate the landscape. Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] would never have expected remnants of a civilization on a world like this and believe we could learn much from investigating.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1077`
