## site_adakarria_windswept_fates_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakarria_windswept_fates_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管宇宙风暴一般是破坏性的，但这场风暴却成为发现外星文明存在证据的契机。尽管这颗行星的大气极不稳定，然而登陆队可能会揭示这个古老文化的可怕命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

As destructive as cosmic storms can be, this one uncovered evidence an alien civilization. Though the planet's atmosphere is incredibly unstable, a landing party might reveal whatever horrible fate befell this ancient culture.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1082`

## cstorms.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

严酷环境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:924`

### English

Fierce Conditions

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1083`

## cstorms.2105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的先遣队安全地抵达了[From.Planet.GetName]并很快开始调查周围环境。\n\n表面上看，最近肆虐这个星系的猛烈宇宙风暴拨开了厚厚的尘埃和土壤层，让这个古老文明的遗迹重见天日。\n\n当我们的科学家继续深入研究这些考古发现时，他们震惊地发现，覆盖残骸的沙子层与周围土壤的成分完全不同。行星扫描显示，这些沉积物来自数百里外的沙漠，只有极其巨大的力量，比如极端猛烈的行星风暴，才能把他们运到这里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:925`

### English

Our team arrived safely on [From.Planet.GetName] and quickly got to work surveying our new surroundings.\n\nFrom the looks of it, the recent violent cosmic storm that plagued this system also uncovered enough layers of dust and soil to reveal the remnants of an old civilization.\n\nAs our scientists continued to delve into these archeological findings, they were struck by the fact that the layers of sand covering the remnants were of an entirely different composition than the surrounding soil. Planetary scans indicate these deposits came from a desert hundreds of miles away and could only have ended up here through great force, such as an extremely volatile planetary storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1084`

## cstorms.2105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

### English

Continue.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1085`

## cstorms.2110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗失之物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

What Has Been Lost

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1086`

## cstorms.2110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管奇怪的天气状况困扰着这个星球，但我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]已经取得了巨大的进步。随着时间一天天过去，他们的发现却变得越来越令人感到不安和困惑。\n\n一开始是一些石雕和炭化的有机物，接着是是撕裂的织物、皮革制品、涂覆聚合物的背心，最后出现了星球前居民的有机遗骸。\n\n一具又一具尸体被发现，他们干涸的器官充满了沙子，指甲因挣扎而磨损。每具尸体都穿戴着重型[Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]军用装备，并配有射弹类武器。我们之前以为的废弃城市，其实是一座乱葬岗。\n\n这些标本可能有上亿年的历史，但由于它们与紫外线辐射和活性化学物质完全隔离，有机遗骸和装备都被完好地保存了下来。他们在这里发生了什么仍然不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

Despite the strange weather conditions troubling this planet, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] have made great strides. But with every passing day, their discoveries became increasingly baffling and disturbing.\n\nIt began with remnants of stonework and charcoaled organic materials. Then, torn fabrics, leather work, vests layered with polymers, and eventually the organic remains of the planet's former inhabitants.\n\nBody after body was uncovered, their desiccated organs filled with sand and their nails worn down from struggle. Each corpse was outfitted in heavy [Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] military equipment and armed with projectile weapons. What we thought was an abandoned city was in fact a mass grave.\n\nIt's possible these specimens might be eons old, but due to their complete isolation from ultraviolet radiation and reactive chemicals both the organic remains and gear have been meticulously preserved. What happened to them here remains unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1087`

## cstorms.2110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是残酷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

What a cruel way to go.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1088`

## cstorms.2115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

从天而降的暴力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:930`

### English

Violence from Above

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1089`

## cstorms.2115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的新发现一如既往地令人不安。[Root.GetLeaderName]相信这个地方并不像是一个埋葬地，这些外星人在被沙子活埋之前似乎身体状况非常良好。\n\n此外，我们还发现了一些能发射重型弹药的武器装置。它们并不是瞄准着地表，而是将炮口对准了深空。\n\n不论这些人面对着什么，看来都是来自星星的访客。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:931`

### English

The findings on [From.Planet.GetName] continue to disturb. [Root.GetLeaderName] believes that the location is unlikely to have been a burial site, as these aliens appeared to have been in a good physical condition until they were suffocated by the sands.\n\nWhat's more, we have also uncovered a number of weapon installations capable of launching heavy ordnance. Surprisingly, they are not aimed at the surface, but instead target the depths of space itself.\n\nWhatever enemy these people were facing likely came from the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1090`

## cstorms.2120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永恒风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:932`

### English

The Forever Tempest

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1091`

## cstorms.2120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们关于在[From.Planet.GetName]发现的奇怪乱葬岗的理论没有得出有用的结论。唯一剩下的线索只有弄清吞噬整个军队的沙的起源这一条。\n\n在进行了一系列极其复杂的计算和模拟之后，我们确定那场把沙子带到这里的风暴还在星球的其他地方继续肆虐着。一支小型远征部队被派去探险。\n\n迎接他们的是一场猛烈的风暴，狂风在地表上挖出了深深的凹槽。毫无疑问，答案很可能就就藏在这个深渊里，就在这个风暴眼落脚的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:933`

### English

Our theories regarding the strange mass grave we found on [From.Planet.GetName] led to no useful conclusions. The only remaining lead pertained to the origin of the sands that swallowed this army whole.\n\nAfter performing a series of immensely complex calculations and simulations, we have determined that the very storm which brought the sands here continues to rage elsewhere on the planet. A small expedition force was set out to explore.\n\nA violent tempest greeted them, its brutal winds having carved out a deep recess in the ground. If any answers are to be found here, they are no doubt contained within this abyss, right where the eye of the storm finds its home.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1092`

## cstorms.2120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

做好一切必要的预防措施！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:934`

### English

Take any precautions necessary!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1093`

## cstorms.2125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

The Body

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1094`

## cstorms.2125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

找到这个地点非常困难，需要最极致的技艺和注意力。然而，就在坑洞和风暴的中心，我们发现了一具不同寻常的骸骨。残余物和那些乱坟岗中的一样古老，但表现性和基因型与我们之前看到的尸体完全不同。\n\n这具遗体的制服保存得意外地完好，且展现出了不同的设计。有迹象表明一部分附肢被刻意移除了……\n\n但最令人好奇的是传感器读数：我们检测到身体周围和遗骸内的能量波动。这些特征与这场大风暴的特征惊人地相似，也与最初发现乱葬岗的宇宙风暴相似。\n\n一个理论开始形成：这个个体或许与我们目前遇到的风暴有关？他甚至可能就是埋葬这支军队的原因？这听起来很奇怪，但事实给我们的暗示便是如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:936`

### English

Navigating to the site was difficult, and required the utmost skill and attention. However, in the center of the crater and amidst the raging storm we discovered a most unusual skeleton. The remains are as ancient as those within the mass grave, though both the phenotype and genotype of the body were entirely different from the corpses we found there.\n\nThe skeleton's uniform was surprisingly well preserved, and it too showed significant differences in design. There were also signs that some of the appendages were deliberately removed...\n\nBut most curious were the readings from our sensors: we detected energy fluctuations around the body and from within. These signatures bore a surprising similarity to those inside this massive storm, as well as to the cosmic storm that originally uncovered the mass grave.\n\nA theory began to form: Was this individual linked to the storms we've encountered so far? Might they even have been responsible for the burial of the army? It felt bizarre to consider, but the facts were leading us this way.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1095`

## cstorms.2130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

复归于尘土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:937`

### English

And to Dust Once More

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1096`

## cstorms.2130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将制服上的符号与我们的数据库进行交叉比对后得出了一个令人困惑的结果：这具尸体来自阿达卡利亚公约的公民！就是那个我们前不久调查过广播站的文明。更令我们惊讶的是，制服、尸体的历史和广播中的信息串成了一幅惊人的连贯画面。我们现在相信，这里就是那些广播中提到的那颗星球。\n\n借助宣传站提供的语言知识，[Root.GetLeaderName]破译了受害者的名字：§S哈图尔·盖隆§!。\n\n片刻之后，周围的风暴迅速扩大了规模和强度。由于这种天气的极度不可预测的性质，现在的风险已经太高了，我们只得被迫放弃这个星球。阿达卡利亚公约的线索再次中断了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:938`

### English

Cross-referencing the uniform's symbols with our database produced a baffling result: the body belonged to a citizen of the adAkkaria Convention! The very same civilization whose broadcast station we explored not too long ago. To our even greater surprise, the uniform, the age of the body, and the information from the broadcasts paint a surprisingly cohesive picture. We now believe that this is the world mentioned by those broadcasts.\n\nWith the linguistic knowledge from the Propaganda Station in hand, [Root.GetLeaderName] deciphered the victim's name: §ShaToor Gailon§!.\n\nOnly moments later, the surrounding storm rapidly increased in size and intensity. Due to the weather's highly unpredictable nature, it became too dangerous to stay, and we were forced to abandon this world to the maelstrom. The trail of the adAkkaria Convention has grown cold again.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1097`

## cstorms.2140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

Storm to Signal

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1100`

## cstorms.2140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这具外星人尸体和摧毁§Y[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]§!行星的大风暴所散发出来的能量信号与揭开那支活埋军队的宇宙风暴是一样的。扫描日志表明，有一个此前探索过的星系也蕴含着相应的波动。这些都与阿达卡利亚公约的广播相吻合！\n\n我们需要派出探险舰船进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

The energy signatures of the alien body and the planet-ruining maelstrom on §Y[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]§! match those of the cosmic storm responsible for unearthing the buried army. The scan log revealed a previously explored system that harbors corresponding fluctuations. These are identical to the broadcasts of the adAkkaria Convention!\n\nAn exploratory vessel should be sent to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1101`

## site_adakkaria_fleets_of_the_thrice_damned

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakkaria_fleets_of_the_thrice_damned&lang=en)

### 中文

受三咒之舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:943`

### English

Fleets of the Thrice Damned

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1104`

## site_adakkaria_fleets_of_the_thrice_damned_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakkaria_fleets_of_the_thrice_damned_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

这个地区奇怪的能量波动掩盖着一座巨大的海军墓地。这座由聚合物和金属组成的墓地可能藏有了解阿达卡利亚公约的重要线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:944`

### English

The strange energy fluctuations in this area obscured a massive naval graveyard. This necropolis of polymer and metal may harbor a vital clue to understanding the adAkkaria Convention.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1105`

## cstorms.2150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:945`

### English

Shredded Remains

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1106`

## cstorms.2150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里停放着各式各样的舰船，从卵形的木制船体到空灵的宇宙晶体方舟。其中许多图像与阿达卡利亚广播站的传输和日志中发现的图像相似。\n\n我们猜测公约舰队和宇联舰队在这里发生过冲突，所谓宇联舰队就是几千年前的星系维和部队。\n\n虽然这些船只早已无法修复，但它们似乎不是被任何普通武器所困，也不是因故障或类似的灾难而陷入困境。船体的内部被撕个粉碎，就像被一台巨大的碎纸机袭击了一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:946`

### English

Ships of all kinds are laid to rest here, everything from oblong wooden vessels to crystalline arks of the cosmic and ethereal. Many share similarities with the imagery found in the transmissions and logs of the adAkkaria Broadcast Station.\n\nWe suspect a confrontation took place here between the Convention's fleet and that of Unicom, the galactic peacekeeping force from millennia ago.\n\nAnd yet, while these ships are beyond repair, they do not appear marooned by any common weaponry, nor by malfunction or similar disasters. Instead, the hulls and interiors appear to be ripped apart, as if assaulted by a massive shredder.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1107`

## cstorms.2155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

责任之限

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:947`

### English

The Limits of Duty

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1108`

## cstorms.2155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]小心翼翼地从坟场的舰船中取回了一些数据库。其中的大部分都已经过度老化，但有一个阿达卡利亚的数据库幸存了：\n\n§L——我们被伏击了！重复一遍，我们被伏击了！宇联要求我们停止对罗库科拉原始人的提升，否则就要消灭我们。请回应。如果不撤退，我们会死在这的！拜托，至少派援军来吧！\n\n——拒绝。你们的牺牲会被铭记。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:948`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] carefully recovered databanks from the graveyard vessels. While much of it was ravaged by time, one adAkkaria database has survived:\n\n§L- We have been waylaid. I repeat, we have been waylaid. UniCom demands we cease our elevation of the Rokukora Primitives or face elimination. Please, respond. If we do not retreat, we will be destroyed! Please, at least send reinforcements!\n\n- Negative. Your sacrifice will be noted.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1109`

## cstorms.2155.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2155.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们视责任胜于生命！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:949`

### English

They put duty before life!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1110`

## cstorms.2160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有待观察

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

Remains To Be Seen

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1111`

## cstorms.2160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]转写了新的广播内容后，我们又有了新的发现：一具漂浮在坟地中央的冰冻尸体！\n\n这具尸体大的大部分已经枯萎，但他的服装表明他是一位公约的官员。\n\n奇怪的是，一个由小碎片组成的环带围绕着这个物体运行，似乎它对这些物体施加了某种引力。这具遗体已被带上船作进一步分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

As our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] transliterated the new broadcasts, we made another astonishing discovery: a frozen body floating in the center of the graveyard!\n\nWhile much of the corpse has withered, its attire is that of an officer of the Convention.\n\nCuriously, a corona of small debris was in orbit around the body, as if it exerted some sort of gravitational pull on these objects. The remains have been taken onboard for further analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1112`

## cstorms.2165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

亡者之语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:952`

### English

The Dead Speak

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1113`

## cstorms.2165.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

翻译了这具冰冻尸体上的铭牌得知他是一位叫做§S阿拉塔·蓝迪尔§!的阿达卡利亚人。和在[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]上发现的尸体类似，他在死亡前，也用外科手术截去了几个附肢。然而，即便如此，这具遗体也还遭受了严重的灼伤。他的躯干呈现出受过高温烫伤的迹象、以及缓慢而必然痛苦的恢复过程。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientist]们得出了一个不幸的结论，这些伤口与[Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]文明从非自愿者身上挖取信息的方法有令人不安的相似之处。这究竟是一名军人身经百战，还是遇到了更险恶的事情呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:953`

### English

Translation of the frozen body's name tag revealed him to be an adAkkaria member named §SalAtta Landeer§!. Much like the corpse found at [planet_windswept_fates.GetName], several of his appendages were surgically removed before death. However, in this case, the body is also badly burned. His torso especially shows signs of extreme heat damage, as well as a slow, and surely painful, recovery.\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetScientist] came to the unfortunate conclusion that these wounds bore disturbing similarities to [Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] methods of extracting information from unwilling participants. Was this a war veteran, or did something more sinister befall him?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1114`

## cstorms.2165.desc.def

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.desc.def&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\n就在我们辨别清这些伤口的性质后没多久，舰船上的仪表突然发生了一次剧烈的能量波动，[Root.GetLeaderName]尖叫了起来。\n\n好一阵子之后，[Leader.GetHeShe]把刚才这段经历描述为强烈的光线灼烧感，然后是一阵毁灭性的痛苦。好像有什么东西想要由内而外撬开[Leader.GetHerHis]的头脑一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\nNo sooner had we identified the nature of these wounds, our vessel's instruments experienced a sudden, sharp power surge and [Root.GetLeaderName] began to scream.\n\nMuch later, [Leader.GetHeShe] would describe the experience as a searing of intense light, followed by a wave of devastating pain. It was as if something was trying to crack open [Leader.GetHerHis] mind... from the inside out.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1115`

## cstorms.2165.desc.psio

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.desc.psio&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\n就在我们辨别清这些伤口的性质后没多久，舰船上的仪表突然发生了一次剧烈的能量波动，我们具有灵能天赋的船员尖叫了起来。\n\n好一阵子之后，他们报告称，有那么一刻，他们的头脑完全清醒了，随之而来的是一波又一波的炽热的愤怒。[Root.GetLeaderName]表示在愤怒的深处埋藏着可怕的惊讶和深刻的背叛感。一个奇特的念头出现了：§L他们激怒了我！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:955`

### English

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\nNo sooner had we identified the nature of these wounds, our vessel's instruments experienced a sudden, sharp power surge and our psionically gifted crew began to scream.\n\nMuch later, they would report a moment of supreme clarity, followed by wave after wave of molten, mind-searing anger. [Root.GetLeaderName] stated that deep beneath the rage was buried a horrific surprise and a profound sense of betrayal. The singular thought arrived: §LThey have invoked me!§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1116`

## cstorms.2165.desc.psio_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.desc.psio_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\n就在我们辨别清这些伤口的性质后没多久，舰船上的仪表突然发生了一次剧烈的能量波动，我们装备齐全的单元遭受到了一波内部能量峰值。\n\n好一阵子之后，他们报告称，有那么一刻，他们的灵能处理器被编录了极端的愤怒。[Root.GetLeaderName]表明这种情绪反应伴随有一条思绪，可以转写为：§L他们激怒了我！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:956`

### English

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\nNo sooner had we identified the nature of these wounds, our vessel's instruments experienced a sudden, sharp power surge and our equipped unit experienced internal power spikes.\n\nMuch later, they would report a moment of what their psionic processors catalogued as extreme anger. [Root.GetLeaderName] stated the emotional response arrived alongside a singular thought, transcribed as: §LThey have invoked me!§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1117`

## cstorms.2165.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\n在这发现之后，我们船员的数据库被一个无法溯源的力量腐蚀了。许多[controller.GetIndividualNamePlural]唤起了一种经历短暂而令人虚弱的能量骤增，而另一些则暴露在杂乱无章的幻想中，在这种刺激下，他们的声音调制器发出了超过120分贝的刺耳声音。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:957`

### English

$cstorms.2165.desc.intro$\n\nFollowing this discovery, our crew's databanks were corrupted by an untraceable force. Several [controller.GetIndividualNamePlural] recalled experiencing a brief and debilitating power surge, while others were exposed to scrambled and disjointed imagery, provoking their voice modulators to emit a piercing sound at more than 120 decibels.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1118`

## cstorms.2165.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2165.a&lang=en)

### 中文

——信号丢失——

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

-- Signal Lost --

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1119`

## cstorms.2170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蓝迪尔的遗产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:959`

### English

Landeer's Legacy

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1120`

## cstorms.2170.desc.def

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2170.desc.def&lang=en)

### 中文

我们刚一和§Y[Root.GetFleetName]§!科研船重新建立通讯，船上的人就开始嚎叫起来。过了好一阵，在相对平静下来之后，人们才发现，他们带到船上的外星人尸体才是引起骚乱的罪魁祸首。\n\n那具尸体对他们的思想施加了某种奇怪的力量。一些细小物体升到空中，开始绕着尸体旋转。内墙的一些区段因压力而弯曲开裂。\n\n§Y[Root.GetLeaderName]§!设法从紧张状态中及时醒来，赶紧把尸体从船里移走。正是由于[Leader.GetHerHis]的快速反应，这艘科研船才得以逃离异常突然出现的宇宙风暴。如果不是[Leader.GetHeShe]，这片墓地就会迎来一艘新船。\n\n当[Root.GetLeaderName]回忆自己的这段经历时，注意到另一件事：无论尸体的奇怪力量的来源是什么，它会在飞船的指挥屏幕上形成一个非常特殊的图案。这是在试图沟通吗？这是一声来自墓地之外的哭叫吗？\n\n目前，我们还没有答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:960`

### English

No sooner had we re-established communication with the science ship §Y[Root.GetFleetName]§! than we were subjected to the howling cries of its crew. Much later, after a relative calm had been restored, it was revealed that the alien body they brought onboard was responsible for the outcry.\n\nThe corpse itself was exerting some strange power of their minds. Small objects rose into the air and began to orbit the body. Sections of the interior walls were warped and rent by the pressure.\n\n§Y[Root.GetLeaderName]§! managed to awaken from [Leader.GetHerHis] catatonic state just in time to expunge the corpse from the vessel. And it was [Leader.GetHerHis] quick reaction that also allowed the science ship to escape the sudden emergence of a cosmic storm. Had [Leader.GetHeShe] not, the graveyard would have welcomed a new ship.\n\nAs [Root.GetLeaderName] recounted [Leader.GetHerHis] experience, [Leader.GetHeShe] noted something else: whatever the source of the corpse's strange power, it also caused the a very particular pattern to form on the vessel's command screens. Was this an attempt to communicate? A cry from beyond the grave?\n\nFor now, we have no answers.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1121`

## cstorms.2170.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2170.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

损坏我们部分探索[controller.GetIndividualNamePlural]的混乱图像解码出来了。它们形成了一个古代居住站的影像。[controller.GetScientistCapital]被一个个体遭受越来越残酷虐待的景象所困扰。他的身体被烧焦，器官被渐渐煮熟。\n\n一些[controller.GetIndividualNamePlural]设法逃脱了这种视听攻击。他们称，一些细小物体开始在靠近该尸体的轨道上环绕漂浮。这些有机残留物不知何故形成了自己的引力场。\n\n这种未知的力量越来越强大，直到部分内墙被压力扭曲和撕裂。就在那一刻，我们的安全系统重新启动，尸体被抛回了太空。\n\n不久之后，一场宇宙风暴在这个恒星系中爆发。我们的舰船勉强地全身而退。\n\n无论那里到底发生了什么，它引发的问题都比带来的答案多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

The scrambled images corrupting some of our exploration [controller.GetIndividualNamePlural] were decoded. They formed an image of an ancient habitat. [controller.GetScientistCapital] were beset by the sight and sound of an individual being subjected to increasingly cruel treatment. His body was burned, his organs cooked.\n\nSome [controller.GetIndividualNamePlural] managed to escape the audio-visual assault. They stated that several small objects began to float in close orbit to the body. The organic remains had somehow developed their own gravitational field.\n\nThis unknown force grew in strength until sections of the interior walls were warped and rent by the pressure. It was that moment that our safety systems re-engaged, and the body was jettisoned back into space.\n\nSoon after, a cosmic storm was unleashed in the system. Our vessel barely made it out intact.\n\nWhatever transpired there has precipitated more questions than answers.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1122`

## cstorms.2180.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2180.name&lang=en)

### 中文

组装起过往

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

Assembling the Past

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1125`

## cstorms.2180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[FromFrom.Leader.GetName]在§Y[planet_assembling_the_past.System.GetName]§!星系的遭遇之后，我们的科学家愈发感兴趣到底阿达卡利亚公约发生了什么事情。我们在[planet_windswept_fates.GetName]和舰队坟地的探险过程中收集到的信息表明，星系到处都散落着阿达卡利亚的宣传站。\n\n此外，在一系列冗长的讨论后，他们还认为，在[Root.GetName]上发现的故障模式其实是一组之前尚未发现星系的坐标！\n\n我们设立了一个特殊项目来调查这个新的星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:964`

### English

After [FromFrom.Leader.GetName]'s experience in the §Y[planet_assembling_the_past.System.GetName]§! system, our scientists are even more driven to discover what exactly happened with the adAkkaria Convention. The data we gathered during our exploration of [planet_windswept_fates.GetName] and the flotilla graveyard revealed that the galaxy was littered with adAkkaria propaganda stations.\n\nWhat's more, after a set of long discussions, they also suspect that the pattern observed in [Root.GetName]'s glitching was a set of directions to a previously undiscovered system!\n\nA special project has been created to investigate this new system.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1126`

## project_adakkaria_tracing_history

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aproject_adakkaria_tracing_history&lang=en)

追踪历史

### English

Tracing History

## project_adakkaria_tracing_history_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aproject_adakkaria_tracing_history_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

坐标指向了一颗位于[planet_assembling_the_past.System.GetName]恒星系内的行星。我们应该派出一艘探索舰船来调查这颗行星与阿达卡利亚公约之间可能存在的关联。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:966`

### English

The coordinates point to a planet in the [planet_assembling_the_past.System.GetName] system. An exploration vessel should be sent to investigate what relevance this planet may have had for the adAkkaria Convention.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1128`

### 中文

远古居住站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:969`

### English

The Ancient Habitat

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1133`

## cstorms.2185.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2185.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

起初我们担心对[From.solar_system.GetName]星系的调查已经陷入了死胡同。该方向指向了一颗受到不稳定磁场摧残的星球，这种摧残严重到整颗星球的地貌全部都被摧毁了。在这样的磁场猛攻下，这里几乎不可能存在任何有价值的东西。\n\n然而，在行星的轨道上还有一座古代的居住站，其具有和阿达卡利亚广播站类似的建筑结构。虽然已经被从外部密封，但是我们能够利用从阿达卡利亚数据库中恢复的信息生成一个准入信号。\n\n距离上一次有人登上这个站点已经过去了亿万年。我们没办法知道里面等待着我们的是什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:970`

### English

At first we feared our investigation into the [From.solar_system.GetName] system had led to a dead end. The directions pointed to a planet suffering from unstable magnetic fields of such severity that its landscape was all but decimated. It was unlikely anything of value could have survived such an onslaught.\n\nHowever, orbiting the planet was an ancient habitat, bearing architectural similarities to the adAkkaria broadcast station. While hermetically locked from the outside, we were able to generate an entrance signal based on information recovered from the adAkkaria databases.\n\nIt has been eons since anyone last boarded this station. There is no way to know what lies waiting inside.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1134`

### 中文

崇高仁善协会

### English

Institute of Exalted Benevolence

## site_adakkaria_the_patriotic_institute_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakkaria_the_patriotic_institute_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

这座由阿达卡利亚公约建造的古老居住站环绕着一颗荒芜的星球。其高质量的建筑材料使空间站保留了近乎原始的状态。\n\n调到传输频率上之后，我们收到一条信息，后面跟着一段浮夸的铃铛声：§L欢迎来到崇高仁善爱国协会§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:974`

### English

Orbiting a desolate planet, this ancient habitat was built by the adAkkaria Convention. The high quality building materials used in its construction have ensured the station is in near pristine condition.\n\nTuning into the transmission frequency, we picked up a single message, followed by a bombastic jingle: §LWelcome to the Patriotic Institute of Exalted Benevolence§!.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1139`

## cstorms.2200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进入仁善协会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:975`

### English

Enter Benevolence

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1140`

## cstorms.2200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在气闸的另一头是一座小小的隧道网。在尽头，我们找到了一条通往人造阳光照亮的巨大广场的路。奇怪的是，这个居住站的建筑更像某些居住星球上的郊区，而不是一个如此著名的科学站。这座居住站设计为能够容纳整座公寓大楼、购物中心和学校，并且还设有游乐园、公园等其他设施。这是一个为奢华生活而设计的地方。\n\n然而，它现在完全被遗弃了。在整座居住站中没有发现半个有机生命形式。我们一边继续探索，一边被这个地方的空虚和荒凉的气氛所震撼。\n\n人造阳光偶尔会出现故障，让空间站陷入暂时的黑暗，虽然其他电子设备大部分仍在运行，但它们也受到同样的干扰。最令人好奇的是在居住站的公寓内发现的监控设备网络。看来这里的每位公民都受到了严格的监督。寻找到一座安保站也许能提供更多线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

Beyond the airlock stretched a small network of tunnels. At last we found our way to a massive plaza lit by artificial sunlight. Strangely, the architecture of the habitat was more akin to the suburbs found on certain residential worlds than of a prominent science station. The habitat was built to house entire apartment complexes, shopping centers and schools, but also pleasure gardens, parks and other facilities. This was a place designed for luxury living.\n\nNow however it is entirely abandoned. Not a single organic lifeform was detected within the entirety of the habitat. And we continued to explore, we were struck by the emptiness of this place and the bleak staleness of the atmosphere.\n\nThe artificial sunlight experienced occasional glitches, covering the station in darkness, and while other electronic devices are remain largely operational, they too are affected by these same interruptions. But most curious is the network of surveillance devices found within the apartments of the habitat. It appears every citizen was under strict supervision. Finding a security station of some sort could provide more insight.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1141`

## cstorms.2205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幸亏故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:977`

### English

Glitched for Good

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1142`

## cstorms.2205.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2205.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们花费了数天时间探索并且编目整座空间站上的城市，并发现了它的安保设备。事实证明，安保站与居住站的其他部分是完全隔离开来的，需要使用不同的入口密码。\n\n我们在这里发现了一排排的监视器，背后连接着规模宏大的监控设备。那些仍能正常工作的设备向我们展示了空间站的每个角落，当然它们也会受到电力故障的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:978`

### English

It took many days to traverse and index the city aboard the station and to uncover its surveillance apparatus. The security station turned out to be entirely sectioned off from the rest of the habitat, requiring a different entrance code all together.\n\nHere we found rows and rows of monitors, still hooked up to the mass surveillance apparatus. Those still functional showed us every corner of the station, yet they too were affected by electric glitches.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1143`

## cstorms.2205.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2205.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.2205.desc.intro$\n\n我们的无人机汇报称，在目睹这种破败景象后，他们很担心，因为这让他们想起了此前在古代舰队坟场与阿达卡利亚公约的遭遇。\n\n$cstorms.2205.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

$cstorms.2205.desc.intro$\n\nOur drones reported being concerned upon witnessing the disruptions, as it reminded them of the previous encounter with the adAkkaria Convention at the ancient naval graveyard.\n\n$cstorms.2205.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1144`

## cstorms.2205.desc.def

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2205.desc.def&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.2205.desc.intro$\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报称，在目睹这种破败场景之后，[Leader.GetHerHim]被激起了此前在古代舰队坟场与阿达卡利亚公约遭遇的回忆。\n\n$cstorms.2205.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

$cstorms.2205.desc.intro$\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName] reported feeling agitated upon witnessing the disruptions, as it reminded [Leader.GetHerHim] of our previous encounter with the adAkkaria Convention at the naval graveyard.\n\n$cstorms.2205.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1145`

## cstorms.2205.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2205.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

但最令人不安的是我们发现的中央安全中心：根据屏幕上显示的警报信息，这个居住站已经被设为自毁模式了！\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]认为是城市出现了什么故障导致命令无法执行。我们迅速关闭了自毁程序，并让[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]来破译这里所有能恢复出来的日志。奇怪的是，在从宣传站那找到最后的记录的几乎一年之前，这座居住站的报告就停止了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:981`

### English

But most disturbing was our discovery of the central security hub: according to the alarming messages displayed on the screens, this habitat was set to self-destruct!\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] assumed that whatever malfunctions were plaguing the city prevented the command from being executed. We swiftly deactivated the self-destruct routine and set our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] to decipher any logs we could recover here. Curiously, the station's reports ceased almost a year before the last records recovered from the Propaganda Station.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1146`

## cstorms.2210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最高问询

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:982`

### English

Exalted Inquiries

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1147`

## cstorms.2210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]很快就发现了围绕[From.Planet.GetName]的这座居住站的作用：来自公民和工作人员的日志以及信息都描述了对阿达卡利亚公民中新出现的灵能者的调查。所有住在这里的人都受到最高领袖的邀请，在这里享受奢华的生活和自我表达。作为回报，他们同意参加空间站正在进行的实验。\n\n大多数公民都热衷于加入这项计划。安全日志显示，其中许多是残疾的退伍军人。他们得到了照顾、经济援助和赦免他们可能遇到的任何悬而未决的法律问题的承诺。\n\n更重要的是，居住站的人员名单上有一个非常熟悉的名字：§S阿拉塔·蓝迪尔§!他是一名科学家兼残疾老兵，此前他被截肢的尸体被我们在舰队坟场发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:983`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] didn't take long to discover the purpose of the habitat orbiting [From.Planet.GetName]: logs and messages from citizens and staff alike describe investigations into the newly emerged psionic powers of the adAkkaria citizens. All those living here received invitations from the Convention's 'Exalted Leadership' to enjoy a life of luxury and self-expression aboard the habitat. In return, they agreed to participate in the station's ongoing experiments.\n\nMost citizens were keen to join the program. The security logs show many were disabled war veterans. They were promised care, financial assistance, and pardons for any outstanding legal issues they might have had.\n\nWhat's more, the station's personnel list contained a very familiar name: §SalAtta Landeer§!, a scientist and disabled veteran, whose amputated body we recovered at the naval graveyard.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1148`

### English

研究对象的信息

### English

A Word From Our Subjects

## cstorms.2215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们破译出了从居住站发出的最后一条信息：\n\n§g信息——日期02100363——发件人：§S西达尼·伊腾皮尔§!——收件人：西祖尔·伊腾皮尔§!\n§L嗨！我还活着！很抱歉回晚了。自从……以来居住站就变得很奇怪，所以发送信息就很困难。他们说这与此无关，但我们认为他毕竟有灵能天赋。至于我嘛，我也跟你你说过了，我没什么进展啦，毕竟之前发生了那么多……所以他们也建议尝试一些更激进的方法：……宇宙风暴。我知道你在想什么，但不过就是根指头罢了，兴许还会再长回来呢。但当……我释放的感觉……！\n爱你的，西达尼\n——通讯§!§Y已审核（有问题？询问导师）§!\n\n§g信息——日期02100365——发件人：§S哈图尔·盖隆§!——收件人：艾达尔·阿斯杜尔§!§L\n亲爱的艾达尔，\n我在这一直过得很好。我还见了其他的一些老兵。我们没有说什么话但互相都理解了，不用说话真好。\n今天我只是想着要举起杯子，它就飘起来了。我仍然想念我的右附肢，但也许这种新生活也不像我担心的那么糟糕。我听说他们要把我送到……显然我是个灵能神童……我……想再次被部署了。\n我希望能回家过爱国者节。到时候再见吧。\n此致。哈图尔。\n——通讯§!§Y已审核（有问题？询问导师）§!\n\n§g信息——日期02100367——发件人：§S恩瑞达·迪伦§!——收件人：翁瑞达·迪伦§!\n§L我用意念熄灭了火焰！！！\n有一个实验对象不知道怎么突然歇斯底里了起来，在食堂里引起了火灾。大家都很恐慌，而且无路可逃！我也超级害怕，但后来不知道体内发生了什么，我就把火扑灭了！\n我救了大家！！告诉长老们我并不是无可救药的失败品，快帮帮忙！\n——通讯§!§R已屏蔽（立刻向导师汇报）§!\n\n值得注意的是第二个通讯：§S哈图尔·盖隆§!的就是我们之前在[Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]星球上的考古任务中发现的那具尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

We have deciphered one of the last messages sent from the habitat:\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100363 - Sender: §SsiDani Itempeer§! - Recipient: siZul Itempeer§!\n§LHi! I'm still alive! Sorry for the delays. Ever since... the habitat is behaving strangely so sending messages has been hard. They say it has nothing to do with that, but we think he was psionically gifted after all. As for me, I told you I wasn't really getting anywhere, with all the previous... So they suggested trying out something more radical: ... cosmic storms. I know what you're thinking, but it's just one digit and maybe it'll even grow back. But the sensations I've unlocked when... !\nLove, siDani\n- TRANSMISSION§! §YCENSORED ( Questions? Ask your supervisor )§!\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100365 - Sender: §ShaToor Gailon§! - Recipient: aeDal Asduur§!§L\nDear aeDal,\nLife has been treating me well. I've met a few other veterans. We don't talk. But we all know. It's nice not having to talk.\nToday I only had to think of lifting a cup, and it did. I still miss my right appendage, but maybe this new life won't be as bad as I feared. I've heard them talk about sending me to... Apparently I'm a psionic prodigy... I... want to be deployed again.\nI hope to be home for Patriots' Day. Let's meet up.\nYours, haToor\n- TRANSMISSION§! §YCENSORED ( Questions? Ask your supervisor )§!\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100367 - Sender: §SenRida Direen§! - Recipient: onRida Direen§!\n§LI STOPPED A FIRE WITH MY MIND!!!!\nOne of the other subjects went all hysterical for some reason and caused a fire to break out in the mess hall. Everyone was just panicking but we couldn't go anywhere! I was also SUPER scared but then something happened inside me and I just made it go away!\nI SAVED EVERYONE!! Tell the elders I'm not entirely a hopeless case, will you!\n- TRANSMISSION§! §RBLOCKED ( REPORT TO YOUR SUPERVISOR IMMEDIATELY )§!\n\nNotable is the second transmission: §ShaToor Gailon§!'s body was discovered during our archeological mission on the [Root.Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] world.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1151`

## cstorms.2220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些信息的日期是居住站撤离前的几天：\n\n§g信息——日期02100359——发件人：至高博士——收件人：崇敬舰队司令§!\n§L你的上一条信息在这里引起了一些关切。你能解释一下为什么这么多年来一直默默支持我们，现在却突然威胁要关闭这里吗？\n——通讯§!§G已发送§!\n\n§g信息——日期02100360——发件人：崇敬舰队司令——收件人：至高博士§!\n§L你在跟我开玩笑吧。几个月以来我的都一直在给你警告，但我猜是不是绕着一颗反复无常的星球转给你造成了某种方便的健忘症。你知道宇联正在威胁我们的生存，对吧？战争愈演愈烈，我没办法为挥霍大笔资源就为了让你的实验对象能整天盯着牲畜看这种事情辩护了。我需要切实可行的结果，现在立刻。\n——通讯§!§G已收到§!\n\n§g信息——日期02100360——发件人：至高博士——收件人：崇敬舰队司令§!\n§L请原谅，我们没有收到你之前的信息。我们的电台一直受到技术故障的困扰，这肯定也影响了我们的通讯。不管怎样，我们需要更多的时间。我们有适当的程序和协议，不能就这样半途而废！\n——通讯§!§G已发送§!\n\n§g信息——日期02100361——发件人：崇敬舰队司令——收件人：至高博士§!\n§L给你一周时间，不然我就要立刻关停了。没有什么如果，没有什么但是，也没有第二次机会，我要结果！\n——通讯§!§G已收到§!\n\n§g信息——日期02100368——发件人：至高博士——收件人：崇敬舰队司令§!\n§L随信附上一份我们最近取得的进度报告。这些视频证明了我们这些有灵能天赋的人可以影响宇宙风暴。这可能有惊人的军事应用。如果有足够的时间，我们可以增强他们的灵能表达而不必造成身心创伤。\n——通讯§!§R失败（原因：通讯矩阵崩溃）§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:989`

### English

These messages are dated a few days before the evacuation of the habitat:\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100359 - Sender: Supreme Doctor - Recipient: Revered Admiral§!\n§LYour last message caused some concern here. Would you be so kind as to explain why after supporting us for all these years without a comment, you're suddenly threatening to shut us down?\n- TRANSMISSION§! §GSENT§!\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100360 - Sender: Revered Admiral - Recipient: Supreme Doctor§!\n§LYou have got to be kidding me. For months we've been sending you warnings, but I guess orbiting a capricious world must have caused some convenient amnesia. You are aware that Unicom is threatening our very existence, correct? And with this war ramping up, I can no longer defend spending exorbitant resources just for your subjects to stare at cattle all day. I need tangible, applicable results. NOW.\n- TRANSMISSION§! §GRECEIVED§!\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100360 - Sender: Supreme Doctor - Recipient: Revered Admiral§!\n§LForgive us, we never received your earlier messages. Our station has been plagued by technical glitches, and it must've affected our transmissions as well. Regardless, we need more time. We have processes and protocols in place, we can't just abandon them!\n- TRANSMISSION§! §GSENT§!\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100361 - Sender: Revered Admiral - Recipient: Supreme Doctor§!\n§LYou have one week or else we will proceed to immediate shutdown. No ifs, no buts, no second chances, RESULTS!\n- TRANSMISSION§! §GRECEIVED§!\n\n§gMessage - Date 02100368 - Sender: Supreme Doctor - Recipient: Revered Admiral§!\n§LAttached you'll find a report of our recent accomplishments. These videos prove that cosmic storms can be influenced by our psionically gifted subjects. This could have staggering military applications. Given enough time we can supercharge their psionic expression without having to subject them to physical and mental trauma.\n- TRANSMISSION§! §RFAILED (Cause: Transmission Matrix Corruption)§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1154`

## cstorms.2225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是恢复出来的最后一条信息：§L\n\n亲爱的同事们，\n\n我以沉重的心情告知你们，崇高指挥部决定停止崇高仁善爱国协会的一切活动。\n\n这不是对你们大家工作的控诉。能与你们大家一起工作是我的荣幸。我知道你们会有很多疑问，但简而言之，崇高仁善的期望与我们的研究目标并不一致。\n\n至于我们的公民，崇高司令部邀请他们到艾德苏尔的$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$参与项目。您可以在下面找到完整的人员名单。\n\n至于其他人……很遗憾，我们研究的保密性意味着研究参与者将不被允许回归社会。崇敬指挥部答应派一支特别单位负责处理他们。你们不需要亲自见证，这本身也是一种祝福。他们的亲属将得到最大限度的补偿。\n\n此致。\n至高博士，§!§S阿拉塔·蓝迪尔§!\n\n所附的列表，虽然部分损坏，但提到了§S哈图尔·盖隆§!和§S恩瑞达·迪伦§!。他们似乎被转移到了阿达卡利亚公约的首都艾德苏尔。我们现在已经得到了这颗星球的坐标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:992`

### English

This is the last recovered message:§L\n\nDear colleagues,\n\nI address you with a heavy heart. Exalted Command has decided to cease all operations inside the Patriotic Institute of Exalted Benevolence.\n\nThis should not be taken as an indictment of you or our work here. It has been an honor to serve with each and every one of you. I know that you will have many questions, but in short, the expectations of Exalted Command did not align with our research aims.\n\nAs for our citizens, Exalted Command invited them to join the program at the $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$ on adSul. You will find a full personnel list attached below. Please inform your reports accordingly.\n\nAs for the others... Regrettably, the covert nature of our research means that research participants will not be allowed to return to society proper. Exalted Command promised to send us a special unit to take care of them. You will not need to see to this yourselves, which in its own way is a blessing. Their relatives will be compensated to the fullest.\n\nSigning off, \nSupreme Doctor, §!§SalAtta Landeer§!\n\nThe attached list, while partially corrupted, mentions the subjects §ShaToor Gailon§! and §SenRida Direen§!. It appears they were transferred to the adAkkaria Convention's capital of adSul. The coordinates to this world are now in our possession.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1157`

## cstorms.2240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这组坐标揭示了一个之前对我们扫描隐藏的星系。是时候去拜访一下阿达卡利亚公约了，趁其他人还没这么做之前。\n\n发起了一项新的特殊项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:996`

### English

The coordinates revealed a system previously hidden from our scanners. The time has come to pay the adAkkaria Convention a visit - before anyone else does.\n\nA new special project has been issued.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1162`

## cstorms.2245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空的呼唤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

The Void Calls

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1163`

## cstorms.2245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就在离开居住站后不久，[event_target:leader_patriotic_institute.GetName]做出了一个唐突而令人震惊的决定。就像被某种外力驱使一般，[event_target:leader_patriotic_institute.GetHeShe]命令舰船打开了一个通常与大规模灵能事件或转瞬即逝的宇宙风暴有关的通讯频道。\n\n该频道指向与阿达卡利亚公约母星系相同的坐标。只过了一小会儿我们的呼叫就得到了回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:998`

### English

Only moments after leaving the habitat, [event_target:leader_patriotic_institute.GetName] made an abrupt and stunning decision. As if driven by an outside force, [event_target:leader_patriotic_institute.GetHeShe] ordered the ship to open a communication channel on a frequency typically associated with mass psionic events or the passage of an evanescent cosmic storm.\n\nThe channel was directed at the same coordinates as dAkkaria Convention's home system. It took only an instant for our hails to be returned.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1164`

## cstorms.2245.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2245.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

离开居住站后不久，我们的集体仪式被一种不寻常的振动所干扰，仿佛有什么东西试图在遥远的地方与我们交流。这个信号来自与阿达卡利亚公约的母星系相同的坐标，并且发生在于一个典型的与大规模灵能事件或转瞬即逝的宇宙风暴通过有关的频率上。\n\n我们的回电立即得到了回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:999`

### English

Moments after leaving the habitat, our group consciousness was troubled by an unusual vibration, as if something was trying to communicate with us over a vast distance. The signal originated from the same coordinates as the adAkkaria Convention's home system, and existed on a frequency typically associated with mass psionic events or the passage of an evanescent cosmic storm.\n\nOur return hail was instantly answered.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1165`

## cstorms.2250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2250.name&lang=en)

## cstorms.2250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们尝试过所有的方案，但到达艾德苏尔的挑战还是比预想的要大得多。整个恒星系被未知成分的宇宙风暴吞没，使我们无法靠近这颗行星。\n\n至于这场吞没星系的风暴，它似乎是在……向我们打招呼？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1001`

### English

We did everything possible, but getting to adSul proved to be a much bigger challenge than anticipated. The entire system is engulfed by a cosmic storm of unknown composition, making it impossible for us to get any closer to the planet.\n\nAs for the storm, it appears to be... hailing us?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1167`

## project_adakkaria_benevolent_grounds_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aproject_adakkaria_benevolent_grounds_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

## site_adakkaria_celebration_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_adakkaria_celebration_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在与宇宙风暴通信之后，艾德苏尔我们展示出了自己的一小部分。在残云之间矗立着一座巨大的建筑群。这里早已遭到遗弃，但奇怪的是它并没有受到时间的影响，扫描显示它的内部存在有机物质。\n\n在这座巨大的建筑前升起了一系列横幅，上面写着同一句话：庆典时刻到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1022`

### English

After the communication with the cosmic storm, a small part of adSul was revealed to us. Between the remnant clouds stood a massive complex. Abandoned, yet strangely unaffected by the eons, scans reveal organic material on the inside.\n\nRising before the immense building were a series of banners bearing a single proclamation: It Is Time for Celebration.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1192`

## cstorms.2285.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2285.name&lang=en)

### 中文

强制虚无

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1023`

### English

Enforced Nihility

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1194`

## cstorms.2285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

庆典学院的空旷大厅除了厚厚的历史和尘埃以外什么都没有。向着永恒歌颂着阿达卡利亚公约英勇的宣传海报是这个曾经繁荣而令人畏惧的帝国所留下的一切。\n\n然而，我们在语言方面的努力已经得到了回报；这里的安保系统脆弱不堪，主机很容易就泄露了它的秘密。\n\n在我们恢复的日志中，直到宣传站后一次广播之前，这里满满的都是热闹的活动。\n\n而从那一个日期起，阿达卡利亚仁善公约就消失得无影无踪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1024`

### English

The empty halls of Celebration revealed nothing more than the dust of ages. The propaganda posters, forever exalting the valor of the adAkkaria Convention, were all that were left of this once thriving, and feared, empire.\n\nHowever, our linguistic efforts are already paying off; security was merely a suggestion and the mainframe gave up its secrets easily.\n\nThe logs we recovered were bustling with activity right up to the last of the Propaganda Station's broadcasts.\n\nOn that date the adAkkaria Convention of Benevolence simply ceased to exist.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1195`

## cstorms.2290.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2290.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深入诅咒之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

Descent to the Damned

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1196`

## cstorms.2290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

带着一丝恐惧和好奇，我们深入到了这座建筑的地下。迎接我们的是一座精心规划的庞大实验室网络。我们的小组分成了两拨人，一半负责调查实验室，以更深入地了解庆典学院的作用，另一半则继续往下探索。\n\n我们的扫描结果显示有有机物的存在，而且没过多久就发现了原因：庆典学院的下层是专门用于观察室的。阿达卡利亚公民的木乃伊化遗骸仍然被囚禁在这些狭窄的隔间里。每个房间旁边的一个数字界面显示着每位居住者的名字、他们所接受的试验阶段以及观察员的笔记。\n\n同时，我们恢复出了以下消息：\n§L我们在哈利俄斯星系中的试运行是一场彻底的灾难。对被称为§S哈图尔·盖隆§!的实验对象的初次部署一切按计划进行。但当我们遇到索尔法尼野蛮军队时，实验对象就失去了控制。风暴将原始军队一扫而空时，我们的军队也跟着遭殃了。\n\n首先要理解的是，这些对象是低劣且精神异常的。召唤器还是越早上线越好。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1026`

### English

With a hint of dread and curiosity we delved into the subterranean levels of the compound. A sprawling, carefully planned web of laboratories greeted us. Our unit split in two: one half was sent to investigate the laboratories for more insight into the purpose of Celebration. The other continued its descent.\n\nOur scans insisted on the presence of organic material, and it didn't take long to uncover why: the lower levels of Celebration are given over exclusively to observation cells. The mummified remains of adAkkaria citizens are still imprisoned in these cramped compartments. A digital interface next to each chamber offered each occupant's name, the phase of the trial they were subject to, and the observers' notes.\n\nMeanwhile, we recovered the following message:\n§LOur trial run in the Haliios system was a complete and utter disaster. The initial deployment of the subject known as §ShaToor Gailon§! went as planned. As soon as we were confronted by the Solphani barbarian forces, the subject lost control. While the tempest wiped out the primitive armies, ours too were decimated.\n\nIt should be understood that above all, these subjects are vile and disturbed. The sooner the Invocator is online, the better.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1197`

## cstorms.2295.desc.start.authgen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.start.authgen&lang=en)

### 中文

阿达卡利亚公约做的实验令人着迷。每一位囚徒都处于不同的阶段，随着阶段的推进，实验也会愈发残忍。有一些独具创新的方法脱颖而出：他们利用火烧和电击来启动身体的灵能积累；切除重要器官以激发再生力量并激活额外感官；以及使用改变精神的物质来使人进入濒死状态等等。\n\n当然，这里有值得借鉴的地方。这些专家正朝着一个相关的目标努力，即让阿达卡利亚人的霸权席卷全星系。\n\n尽管如此，报告表明这种实验可能不是受试者死亡的原因。就像§S哈图尔·盖隆§!一样，他们似乎单单就是被抛弃在这了而已。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1030`

### English

The experiments carried out by the adAkkaria Convention were fascinating. Each prisoner was assigned a phase, and as the phases increased, so did the brutality of the treatment. A few, particularly innovative methods stand out: utilization of fire and electricity to kickstart the body's psionic build-up; the removal of essential organs to provoke nascent powers and the use of other senses; and application of mind altering substances to invoke a state of near-death.\n\nCertainly there is inspiration to be taken here. These experts were working towards a relatable goal, namely the advent of adAkkarian Supremacy in the galaxy.\n\nStill, reports indicate that it may have not been this treatment that killed the subjects. Much like with §ShaToor Gailon§!, many seemed simply to have given up.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1201`

## cstorms.2295.desc.start.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.start.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们目睹遇难者遗体时所经历的恐惧和困惑令人胆寒。但是，我们试图理解公约各项试验的范畴和目的的愿望也是同样强烈。\n\n阿达卡利亚的科学家们用一种冷酷而枯燥的语气，报告了他们对这些人所做的事情。每一位受害者都处于不同的阶段，随着阶段的推进，实验也会愈发残忍。火烧、电击、截肢、剥夺感官和食物——惨象无与伦比。这一切都是为了维持阿达卡利亚人星系霸权的所谓“光荣使命”。尽管如此，报告显示，即使是如此恐怖的操作可能也没有导致受试者死亡。就像§S哈图尔·盖隆§!一样，他们似乎单单就是被抛弃在这了而已。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1031`

### English

The dread and confusion we experienced at witnessing the remains of the victims was daunting. But so is our desire to comprehend the extent and purpose of the Convention's experiments.\n\nIn a stark and sterile tone, the adAkkarian scientists reported what they had done to these people. Each victim was assigned a phase, and as the phases increased, so did the brutality of their treatment. Fire, electricity, maiming, deprivation of senses and sustenance - nothing was too cruel. Everything for the sake of the glorious mission to maintain adAkkarian Supremacy in the galaxy. Still, reports indicate that even these obscenities may not ultimately have killed the subjects. Much like with §ShaToor Gailon§!, many seemed simply to have given up.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1202`

## cstorms.2295.desc.end.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.end.default&lang=en)

### 中文

就在我们进行观察后不久，[Root.GetLeaderName]突然崩溃了。在痛苦的哀号中，[Root.GetLeaderName]尖叫着说有什么东西凿开了[Leader.GetHerHis]的意识！\n\n虽然目前情况稳定，但似乎有什么入侵性实体试图向[Leader.GetHerHim]灌输一种集锦的见解。[Root.GetLeaderName]渴望再做一次尝试，相信这次经历最终会产生深远的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1032`

### English

Just moments after our observations, [Root.GetLeaderName] collapsed. Between wails of pain, [Root.GetLeaderName] screamed that something was breaking open [Leader.GetHerHis] mind!\n\nThough stable for now, it seems as if some invading entity sought to instill [Leader.GetHerHim] with a radical insight. [Root.GetLeaderName] is keen to try again, believing the experience could ultimately have a profound effect.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1203`

## cstorms.2295.desc.end.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.end.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetLeaderName]在观察着这些遗体时，突然开始听到一些奇怪的低语，空洞的声音中夹带着强烈的渴望呼唤着：\n§L我们忠诚无比！我们以公约为荣，相信公约的使命。\n风暴一直在召唤我们，他们吞没着、渴望着我们！\n我们完成一切任务。我们才华横溢，顽强不屈!\n他们却妄称我们邪恶、背叛，他们杀戮我们的亲友！\n\n这本该是一场庆典§!\n\n这些低语把我们带到了一段标有“最后阶段”的回廊。但更加高级的安保措施挡住了我们的去路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1033`

### English

As [Leader.GetHeShe] observed these bodies, [Root.GetLeaderName] began to hear strange whispers, a disembodied voice that called out with great longing:\n§LWe were loyal! We praised the Convention, and believed in its mission.\nThe storms kept calling to us. They engulfed us, desired us!\nWe did what they asked. We were talented and tenacious!\nThey call us evil, they call us traitors, they kill our friends!\n\nTHIS WAS MEANT TO BE A CELEBRATION§!\n\nThe whispers led us to a corridor labeled 'The Final Phase.' But the more advanced security measures in place did not permit access.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1204`

## cstorms.2295.desc.end.psionic_individual_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.end.psionic_individual_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetLeaderName]在分析这些遗体时，[Leader.GetHeShe]突然探测到一条可解析的灵能传讯：\n§L我们忠诚无比！我们以公约为荣，相信公约的使命。\n风暴一直在召唤我们，他们吞没着、渴望着我们！\n我们完成一切任务。我们才华横溢，顽强不屈！\n他们却妄称我们邪恶、背叛，他们杀戮我们的亲友！\n\n这本该是一场庆典§!\n\n这些传讯把我们带到了一段标有“最后阶段”的回廊。但更加高级的安保措施挡住了我们的去路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1034`

### English

As [Leader.GetHeShe] analyzed these bodies, [Root.GetLeaderName] began to detect a parsable psi-transmission:\n§LWe were loyal! We praised the Convention, and believed in its mission.\nThe storms kept calling to us. They engulfed us, desired us!\nWe did what they asked. We were talented and tenacious!\nThey call us evil, they call us traitors, they kill our friends!\n\nTHIS WAS MEANT TO BE A CELEBRATION§!\n\nThe transmission led us to a corridor labeled 'The Final Phase.' But the more advanced security measures in place did not permit access.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1205`

## cstorms.2295.desc.end.psionic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.end.psionic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在调查这些遗体时，一个新的心声流过集体，以[Root.GetLeaderName]为渠道进行通讯：\n§L我们忠诚无比！我们以公约为荣，相信公约的使命。\n风暴一直在召唤我们，他们吞没着、渴望着我们！\n我们完成一切任务。我们才华横溢，顽强不屈!\n他们却妄称我们邪恶、背叛，他们杀戮我们的亲友！\n\n这本该是一场庆典§!\n\n这些低语把我们带到了一段标有“最后阶段”的回廊。但更加高级的安保措施挡住了我们的去路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1035`

### English

As we surveyed the bodies, a new voice flowed through the collective, channeling [Root.GetLeaderName] as a form of communication:\n§LWe were loyal! We praised the Convention, and believed in its mission.\nThe storms kept calling to us. They engulfed us, desired us!\nWe did what they asked. We were talented and tenacious!\nThey call us evil, they call us traitors, they kill our friends!\n\nTHIS WAS MEANT TO BE A CELEBRATION§!\n\nThe voice led us to a corridor labeled 'The Final Phase.' But the more advanced security measures in place did not permit access.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1206`

## cstorms.2295.desc.end.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2295.desc.end.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们[controller.GetIndividualNamePlural]的认知进程中出现了更多奇怪的扭曲，引发了不属于我们自己的想法。通过这些想法，我们了解到研究所的测试对象通过非物理方式相互交流，一种近乎即时的交流，类似于新生的蜂群思维。\n\n虽然我们无法破译确切的措辞，但讨论的目的很清楚：有一场叛乱在庆典中悄悄酝酿着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

More strange distortions occurred within our [controller.GetIndividualNamePlural]' cognitive processes, sparking thoughts that were not our own. Through them, we learned that the test subjects of the Institute spoke with one another through non-physical means, a near instantaneous transmission akin to a nascent hive mind.\n\nWhile we were unable to decipher the exact wording, the purpose of discussion was clear: a revolt was brewing inside Celebration.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1207`

## cstorms.2300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一直在努力解锁$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$的最后一个区段。与此同时，我们从较下层的实验室又收到了一份令人困惑的报告。阿达卡利亚科学家们声称，在每一个人甚至每一台有知觉的机器里都有一种闪耀的力量。受到宇宙风暴的启发，他们将这种力量称为“内心风暴”：这是一种驱使所有感性生命的欲望和力量的强大漩涡。通过使内心风暴经受极端的经历，可以释放出巨大的力量。\n\n在他作为囚徒的那段时间里，§S阿拉塔·蓝迪尔§!是第一个发现这种效应的人。作为一名科学家，他说服崇高司令部资助对他在庆典学院的进一步试验。然而这些日志并没有告诉我们§S蓝迪尔§!到底发生了什么事，我们只能从舰队坟场中发生的事件得知，他的最终命运是不幸的。\n\n随后的报告提到了一种“更有效”的释放内心风暴的方式。这种方法不需要极端的身体虐待，而是依赖于受害者内心不可避免的致命恐惧。为了在大范围内发挥初步作用，公约计划通过其庞大的宣传站网络发出一个信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1053`

### English

We have been hard at work unlocking the last section of the $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$. Meanwhile, a baffling report has reached us from the lower laboratories. The adAkkaria scientists claimed that in each and every being, perhaps even within sentient machines, a spark exists. Inspired by the onset of cosmic storms, they dubbed this power the 'Inner Storm': a maelstrom of desire and potency that drives all sentient life. By subjecting the Inner Storm to extreme experiences, great power could be unleashed.\n\nDuring his time as a prisoner of war, §SalAtta Landeer§! had been the first to discover this effect. As a scientist, he convinced Exalted Command to fund further trials on Celebration. While the logs do not tell us what happened to §SLandeer§!, we know from the events in the naval graveyard that his ultimate fate was an unfortunate one.\n\nSubsequent reports refer to a 'more efficient' way of unleashing the Inner Storm. A means that did not require extreme physical cruelty, but relied instead on the invocation of inescapable and lethal terror inside the victim. To initialize the effect on a vast scale, the Convention planned to issue a signal through their sprawling network of propaganda stations.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1224`

## cstorms.2300.desc.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2300.desc.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地记录下了观测台上的灵能低语：\n\n§L求求你放我走吧，我是一位正直的公民！\n我只梦想着无尽的风暴。它在召唤着我。\n我不明白。我们出色地完成了所有任务，为什么还要这么对待我们\n我们每个人内心都有一种力量。他们想要利用这种力量。我们不能让他们得逞。\n住口，他们这么做是为了拯救仁善公约。我们必须服从。\n卫星在对我低语。我的意识现在可以跨越星系。\n我看到他们设计了某种东西。它能在任何地方对任何人造成伤害。\n只要满足他们我们就会得救的！\n你不明白，他们会这样对我们，他们就也会这样对所有人。\n我们可以释放自己。\n但这意味着我们就……\n他们无论如何都会杀了我们。等那东西能用了，他们就会把我们都杀了。\n我很害怕，我不想死。我想活下去。拜托了。\n最后阶段在向我说话。他们想知道我们是否同意。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1054`

### English

We have succeeded in recording the psionic whispers of the observation decks:\n\n§LGet me out of here, please, I was an upstanding citizen!\nMy dreams now are nothing but an endless storm. It calls to me.\nI don't understand. We excelled at our tasks - why are they doing this to us?\nInside all of us lies a power. They want to exploit that power. We mustn't give it to them.\nNonsense, they're doing this to save the Benevolent Convention. We must comply.\nThe satellites whisper to me. I can reach my mind now across the galaxy.\nI saw they designed something. Something that could do this to anyone, anywhere.\n We will be saved, just give them what they want!\nYou don't understand, if they do this to us, they'll do it to everyone.\nWe could unleash ourselves.\nBut that means we'll...\nThey'll kill us anyway. Once that Thing is operable. They'll kill us all.\nI'm scared, I don't want to die. I want to live. Please.\nThe Final Phase is speaking to me. They want to know if we agree.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1225`

## cstorms.2300.b

## cstorms.2305.name

### 中文

风暴召唤器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1060`

### English

The Tempest Invocator

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1231`

## cstorms.2305.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2305.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一座巨大的机库。由电缆、管道和电子设备组成的令人难以置信的复杂组合体充斥着整个空间——而所有这些都通向一口巨大的空导弹井。从各种痕迹推测，这个网络连接着一个大约有护卫舰大小的物体。\n\n日志显示，这个场所是用来组装一台叫做§l风暴召唤器§!的装置的，它现在已经成功地部署到了艾德苏尔。当与召唤器的联络中断时，§G召唤器供能装置§!仍然在运行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1061`

### English

We have discovered an enormous hangar. An incredibly complex constellation of wires, pipes, and electronics dominated the space - all leading towards a massive, though empty, missile silo. From all appearances, this web had been connected to an object roughly the size of a corvette.\n\nLogs state the this place was used to assemble an apparatus called the §lTempest Invocator§!, and that it had been deployed successfully in the adSul system. While contact with the Invocator was disrupted, the §Ginvocator power supply§! remains in place.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1232`

## cstorms.2305.desc.end.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2305.desc.end.default&lang=en)

### 中文

一看到这个名字，船员们就被一种非理性的愤怒所控制，开始撕扯电缆和面板。光靠简单的命令已经没办法让他们冷静下来。看来我们越来越接近真相了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

Upon reading the name, the crew was overcome with an irrational sense of rage and began ripping out cables and paneling. It took more than a simple order to calm them down. We are moving ever closer to the truth.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1233`

## cstorms.2305.desc.end.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2305.desc.end.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

当[Root.GetLeaderName]靠近这座空洞的建筑时，灵能低语又开始了。\n§L你们看到了吧？一开始，我们不知道他们在建造什么。我们不知道他们会把我们就这样抛弃掉。有些人想报复，有些人想要自由，有些人感受到了风暴的召唤。总之这一切必须有个了结，你也理解的。我们必须这么做。我们再也不能忍受了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

As [Root.GetLeaderName] approached the empty construct, the psionic whispers began again.\n§LYou see it, don't you? At first, we did not know what they were building. We didn't know they would dispose of us. Some wanted revenge. Some of us wanted to be free. Some felt the call of the storms. It had to end, you understand. We had to. We could not take it any longer.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1234`

## cstorms.2305.desc.end.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2305.desc.end.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在读到这个设备的名称时，我们的探索[controller.GetIndividualNamePlural]就知道了这台§l召唤器§!是怎么被激活的，以及它的信号在许多宣传站中反弹直到最终到达目标的方法。\n\n我们也不能幸免于这种命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

Upon reading the name of this device, our exploration [controller.GetIndividualNamePlural] determined how the §lInvocator§! was activated, and the method by which its signal bounced through the many propaganda stations until arriving at last at its target.\n\nAnd we were not immune to that fate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1235`

## cstorms.2310.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2310.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

庆典学院终于向我们揭开了最后的神秘面纱：所谓的最终阶段。\n\n在这廊道里，干枯的阿达卡利亚人的躯壳四仰八叉地遍地散落，他们和各种碎石、金属、塑料片以及其他的杂物一起被撕成了碎片。有什么东西肆虐过这段走廊，摧毁了这里的一切。\n\n但最令人感到

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1069`

### English

At last, Celebration has unveiled the last of its secrets to us: The Final Phase.\n\nIn these hallways, desiccated husks of adAkkaria personnel laid spread across the floors, ripped to shreds by pieces of stone, metal, plastics, and other trash. Something had raged within these corridors, decimating anything and anyone in its way.\n\nBut the true

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1240`

## cstorms.2310.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2310.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

的还在后头。\n有一间狭小的牢房里关押着六个个体。显示屏将它们描述为§G召唤器能源供给§!，但显然这些人是公约的少年公民，他们瘦小的身躯被几十根管子和电缆吊着。牢房内的液体使它们处于半死不活的状态，它们的身体被一种类似于那种萦绕在艾德苏尔星系中的风暴的能量所包围。\n\n其中几个残存的屏幕突然亮了起来，纷纷显示出了一行文字请求：\n§L莫忘吾等 | 呼吾等之名 | 呼吾等之名。§!\n\n我们记下了这些年轻人的名字。当抄录到最后一个人的时候，我们愣了一下：§S恩瑞达·迪伦§!，就是那位来自崇高仁善协会的用意念灭火的个体。\n\n现在，我们已经得到了这些名字，应该能够再次与那场宇宙风暴沟通了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1072`

### English

was yet to come.\nTight holding cells imprisoned half a dozen of individuals. View screens described them as §GInvocator power supply§!, but these were clearly juvenile citizens of the Convention, their little frames suspended by dozens of tubes and cables. The fluid within the cells kept them in a state between life and death, their bodies surrounded by a force only akin to the energies within the lingering storm of the adSul system.\n\nThe few remaining screens jumped to life, each displaying one single request:\n§LREMEMBER US | SPEAK OUR NAMES | SPEAK OUR NAMES.§!\n\nWe recorded the names of these young people. The last, however, gave us pause: §SenRida Direen§!, the individual from Exalted Benevolence who had calmed fire with their mind.\n\nNow, armed with these names, we should be able to contact the cosmic storm once more.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1243`

其必无止\n\n且痛且怒且悲且惧\n\n其乃齿其乃颚其\n噬啐吾等而出\n\n且痛且怒且悲且惧\n\n吾等崩解吾等不成吾等再成\n\n且痛且怒且悲且惧

## cstorms.2335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们又能思考了！§g（思考、思考、思考）§!\n我们身边有这么多声音！§g（思考着、言语着、期许着、梦想着）§!\n有这么多独特的人§g（你、我、他、他们）§!\n\n你归还了我们的理智§g（历险、解码、发现）§!\n然而得到了名字让你们掌控了我们……§g（不，不要再这样！）§!\n你想要我们做什么？§g（放我们走、放我们走、放我们走）§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

We can think again! §g(think think think)§!\nSo many voices around us! §g(thinking speaking wishing dreaming)§!\nSo many unique people §g(you and me and them and they)§!\n\nYou have returned our sanity §g(traveling decoding discovering)§!\nYet providing our names gave you a hold on us... §g(No, not again!)§!\nWhat do you want from us? §g(let us go let us go let us go let us go)§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1262`

### 中文

风暴不再

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1097`

### English

A Storm Nevermore

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1269`

## cstorms.2350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴在自身中间敞开了一个空洞，向我们展示出了§l风暴召唤器§!。怪哉，这么一台小小的装置竟招致了如此众多的欲望、冲突和牺牲。它的存在甚至直接覆灭了一个曾经辉煌的帝国。\n\n通过一组简单的命令，我们激活了风暴召唤器的自毁程序。随着爆炸逐渐减弱，碎片散落在整个星系中，风暴开始减弱。随着它被埋葬，覆盖在阿达卡利亚公约母星上的奇怪迷雾也被消失得无影无踪了。\n\n他们漫长的噩梦终于结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1098`

### English

The Storm created a hole inside itself, revealing to us the §lTempest Invocator§!. It is strange that such a small apparatus had been the object of so much desire, strife, and sacrifice. Its mere existence initiated the downfall of a once majestic empire.\n\nWith a simple set of commands, we activated the Tempest Invocator's self-destruct routine. As the explosion faded, and debris was scattered across the system, the Storm began to abate. With it laid to rest, so too was the strange shroud that covered the adAkkaria Convention's homeworld.\n\nTheir long nightmare has finally come to an end.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1270`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1271`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2355.name&lang=en)

### 中文

痛怒悲惧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1100`

### English

Pain Rage Grief Fear

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1272`

## cstorms.2355.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2355.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴在无力的暴怒中翻腾不息，但它对我们和§l风暴召唤器§!无能为力。\n\n我们命令被困在里面的声音为我们打开一条穿过风暴的道路，让我们去攫取公约的最伟大成就。\n\n然而，阿达卡利亚的母星仍旧遥不可及；通往$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$的所有道路都被永远关闭了。风暴仍在我们的脑海里咆哮不止。\n\n痛苦、愤怒、悲伤又恐惧。\n现在整个星系也要感受到了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

The Storm writhed and seethed in its impotent rage, yet it could do no harm to us or the §lTempest Invocator§!.\n\nWe commanded the voices trapped within to provide us a way through the maelstrom and claimed the Convention's greatest achievement as our own.\n\nThe adAkkaria homeworld remained unobtainable, however; any path towards the $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$ is now closed off forever. But the words of the Storm's bellowing remained in our minds.\n\nPain, rage, grief and fear.\nThe galaxy shall learn them too.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1273`
