## cstorms.2365.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2365.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

深入研究阿达卡利亚公约的研究我们发现，他们的科学家似乎不受任何公认标准的约束；不仅研究方法粗制滥造，结论也都相当平庸。他们完全不做任何仔细或勤奋的研究，几乎只专注于最堕落的形式。而且，他们的测试对象遭受的残酷程度越高，崇高指挥部的表扬就越高。\n\n然而，他们早期的研究却讲述了一个截然不同的故事：实验对象在唤醒“内心风暴”的过程中是受到了善待和尊重的。事实上，比较起早期和晚期研究的成功率，不可避免地会得出一个可怕的结论：酷刑的程度和觉醒者的数量之间从来没有任何关联。无数的受试对象在遭受痛苦之后毫无意义地死去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1113`

### English

Delving into the adAkkaria Convention's research, it seems that their scientists were not beholden to any recognized standard; the methodology was shoddy, the conclusions almost trite. Careful and diligent research was abandoned; depravity embraced. Yet, the more cruelty their tests subjects were subjected to, the higher the praise from Exalted Command.\n\nEarly research however, told a very different story: subjects were well treated, and respected on their journey towards awakening the "Inner Storm". In fact, comparing the success rates between early and late research, there is no escaping the horrifying conclusion: there was never any correlation between the degree of torture and and the number of awakened beings. Countless subjects suffered and died for nothing.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1288`

## cstorms.2365.desc.end.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2365.desc.end.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在了解了其中的原理。从阿达卡利亚公约的错误中吸取教训，我们应该能够鼓励自己的人民培养出内心的小风暴，让他们充满暴风般的活力。危险的是，其中一些§G$Name_Storm_Fused$§!个体可能会响应风暴的召唤，最终自己引发风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1114`

### English

We now understand the principles at play. Learning from the mistakes of the adAkkaria Convention, we should be able to encourage our own people to cultivate their Inner Storms and become infused with tempestuous vigor. The danger is that some of these §G$Name_Storm_Fused$§! individuals may answer the call of the storm, eventually precipitating a tempest themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1289`

## cstorms.2365.desc.end.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2365.desc.end.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的集体现在学会了其中的原理。从阿达卡利亚公约的错误中吸取教训，我们的一部分可以培养出内心风暴，时期充满暴风般的活力。危险的是，其中一些§G$Name_Storm_Fused$§!节点可能会响应风暴的召唤，最终自己引发风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1115`

### English

Our collective now understands the principles at play. Learning from the mistakes of the adAkkaria Convention, some parts of us can cultivate their Inner Storms and become infused with tempestuous vigor. The danger is that these §G$Name_Storm_Fused$§! nodes may feel the call of the storm too strongly, eventually precipitating a tempest themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1290`

## origin_tooltip_storm_chasers_nomadic_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_storm_chasers_nomadic_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—$LEADERS_TRAITS$ ['concept_storm_rider']\n—§Y风暴降临§!锚定在方舟舰的初始星系。\n—一旦研究了所需技术，首都建筑将自动提供§Y风暴避难所§!的修正效果。\n—§Y宇宙风暴技术§!研究选项出现概率：§G+175%§!\n—允许使用['concept_weather_forecasting_mode']\n—$ADDITIONAL_TECHNOLOGIES_STRING$$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—£physics£ ['technology:tech_storm_prediction_1']

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1128`

### English

- $LEADERS_TRAITS$ ['concept_storm_rider']\n- The §YStormfall§! anchors to the Arkship's starting system.\n- Capital Buildings automatically provide the modifiers of §YStorm Shelters§! once the required technologies are researched.\n- §YCosmic Storm Technology§! Research Option Chance: §G+175%§!\n- Access to the ['concept_weather_forecasting_mode']\n- $ADDITIONAL_TECHNOLOGIES_STRING$$TABBED_NEW_LINE$- £physics£ ['technology:tech_storm_prediction_1']

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1306`

## origin_storm_chasers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_storm_chasers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

没有他们不敢面对的挑战，没有他们不愿追逐的线索，也没有他们会视若罔闻的风暴。\n\n从本质上来说，这是一个追求刺激的文明。即有好处也有坏处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1129`

### English

There is no challenge they will not face, no lead they'll fail to chase - and no storm they will ignore.\n\nAt its core, this is a civilization seeking thrills. To its benefit, and to its detriment.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1307`

## origin_tooltip_storm_chasers_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_storm_chasers_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—$LEADERS_TRAITS$['concept_storm_rider']\n—每座§Y风暴建筑§!产生的行星$mod_planet_jobs_unity_produces_mult$：§G+$@storm_chasers_storm_building_unity_produces_mult|%0$§!\n—一旦所需科技研究完毕，首府建筑将自动提供§Y风暴庇护所§!修正。\n—§Y$default_storm$§!出现在国境内的几率：§G+100%§!\n—获得§Y宇宙风暴科技§!研究选项的几率：§G+175%§!\n—§Y风暴吸引建筑§!稀有资源花费和维护费：§G-100%§!\n—可以使用['concept_weather_forecasting_mode']\n—$ADDITIONAL_TECHNOLOGIES_STRING$$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—£physics£ ['technology:tech_storm_prediction_1']

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1130`

### English

- $LEADERS_TRAITS$ ['concept_storm_rider']\n- Planet $mod_planet_jobs_unity_produces_mult$ per §YStorm Building§!: §G+$@storm_chasers_storm_building_unity_produces_mult|%0$§!\n- Capital Buildings automatically provide the modifiers of §YStorm Shelters§! once the required technologies are researched. \n- §Y$default_storm$§! Appearance Chance Within Borders: §G+100%§! \n- §YCosmic Storm Technology§! Research Option Chance: §G+175%§!\n- §YStorm Attraction Buildings§! Rare resource cost and upkeep: §G-100%§!\n- Access to the ['concept_weather_forecasting_mode']\n- $ADDITIONAL_TECHNOLOGIES_STRING$$TABBED_NEW_LINE$- £physics£ ['technology:tech_storm_prediction_1']

## START_SCREEN_STORM_CHASERS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_STORM_CHASERS&lang=en)

### 中文

无奔波不生活。生于灿烂，死于热烈——这一直以来就是我们的信条。当每条航迹都被燃尽，每座山峰都被攀登，然后呢？\n\n我们的行星常年风起云涌，各种风暴和洪水肆意挥洒。如果能找到利用这些独特条件的方法，我们就有机会召唤一场狂暴的宇宙风暴。\n\n我们早就已经征服了母星上的一切挑战，是时候尖啸着飞向深空了。这当然不会是一条坦途，但我们有一句老话：“有志者，惊事成！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1131`

### English

What's life without the rush? Burn fast and die radically - that's always been our way. But when every trail is blazed and even the tallest peak is scaled, what next?\n\nOur planet has always been a stormy one, prone to tempests and deluges of all kinds. If we find ways to use these unique conditions, we have a chance to summon a raging cosmic storm.\n\nOur legendary forebears long since mastered the challenges of our homeworld. The time has come to blast off screaming into the void. It will not be easy, but as the old saying goes, "Only the bold can reach the horizon."

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1310`

## leader_trait_storm_rider_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_storm_rider_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴骑士们陶醉在肾上腺素的冲击中，在宇宙风暴的洪流中释放着真正的潜力。内阁职位会将他们的领域修正替换为内阁修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1133`

### English

Storm Riders revel in the rush of adrenaline, unlocking their true potential in the torrent of a cosmic storm. Council positions replace their field bonuses with council bonuses.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1313`

## progress.2.desc.storm.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.2.desc.storm.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

一位[Root.Owner.GetScientist]说，“快来看看这个小家伙！”\n\n$progress.2.desc.storm.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1143`

### English

In the words of one [Root.Owner.GetScientist], "Get a load of this little guy!"\n\n$progress.2.desc.storm.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1325`

## progress.2.desc.storm.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.2.desc.storm.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们兴奋得发抖。\n\n$progress.2.desc.storm.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1144`

### English

We vibrate with excitement.\n\n$progress.2.desc.storm.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1326`

## progress.2.desc.storm.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.2.desc.storm.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

有外星生命，就在[From.GetName]！这些野东西可能不会那么快能和我们说话，但至少我们知道在宇宙的航行中不是孤单的了！\n\n可能我们最后会发现这个星系到处都是各种不同的生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1145`

### English

Alien life, right here on [From.GetName]! These wild things will not be talking to us any time soon, but at least we know we are not alone on the cosmic journey.\n\nThe galaxy just may turn out to be rife with lifeforms after all.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1327`

## progress.3.desc.storm.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.3.desc.storm.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

“惊奇!”[ruler.GetRegnalName]喊道。“这是个新世界，充满了新机遇！”\n\n我们刚一把殖民船改造成大本营，第一批探险者就已经准备好面对这个星球可能给他们带来的一切挑战了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1146`

### English

"Incredible," [ruler.GetRegnalName] exclaimed. "A new world ripe with opportunity!"\n\nAs we convert our colony ship into a base camp, the first explorers are already gearing up to experience whatever this world has to offer.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1328`

## progress.3.desc.storm.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.3.desc.storm.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

令人颤栗的发现！一颗遍布机遇的新星球！！\n\n我们刚一把殖民船改造成大本营，探险节点就已经准备好面对这个星球可能给我们带来的一切挑战了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

A thrilling discovery! A new world ripe with opportunity!!\n\nAs we convert our colony ship into a base camp, exploratory nodes are already gearing up to experience whatever this world has to offer.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1329`

## progress.3.a.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.3.a.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1148`

### English

Onward.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1330`

## progress.4.desc.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.4.desc.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

寂静的太空对我们的呼唤做出了回应！这个与我们科技水平相当的外星文明，他们一定和我们在差不多的时候踏上了星海的征途。\n\n他们的出现引领着一个全新的体验前言。他们会成为志趣相投的盟友，或是磨炼勇气的试金石？无论如何，未知的刺激正在召唤着我们前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1149`

### English

Out of the silence of space, our calls have been answered! An alien civilization with equivalent technology. They must have entered the galactic fray right around the time we did.\n\nTheir appearance heralds a new frontier of experience. Do they come as like minded allies, or rivals to test our mettle? Either way, the thrill of the unknown beckons us forward.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1331`

## progress.4.h.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.4.h.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

真是有趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1150`

### English

Most intriguing.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1332`

## progress.5.desc.storm.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.5.desc.storm.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]发现了未开智生命形式。我们的一位[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]表示，“这些东西虽然看起来平平无奇，但蕴含着巨大的潜力！”\n\n当然现在为止他们还是未开智的。观察这些物种繁荣昌盛是一件再有趣不过的事情了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1151`

### English

We have discovered pre-sapient lifeforms on [From.Planet.GetName]. As one of our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] put it, "These things might not seem like much yet, but their potential is massive!"\n\nFor now however, they remain pre-sapient. It should be more than interesting to observe these creatures thrive.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1333`

## progress.5.desc.storm.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.5.desc.storm.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]发现了未开智生命形式。\n\n我们兴奋与观察它们的潜力究竟到何等地步，但就目前为止它们仍然尚未开智。观察这些物种繁荣昌盛是一件再有趣不过的事情了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1152`

### English

We have discovered pre-sapient lifeforms on [From.Planet.GetName].\n\nWe thrill to see what comes of their potential, but for now, they remain pre-sapient. It should be more than interesting to observe these creatures thrive.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1334`

## progress.5.h.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.5.h.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

真野.

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1153`

### English

Wild.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1335`

## progress.6.desc.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.6.desc.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

从寂静的太空中，我们的呼唤得到了回应！但第一印象不太对劲，这些人和我们想法不同，他们更喜欢保守自己的秘密。我们目前最好保持距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1154`

### English

From out of the silence of space, our calls have been answered! But the initial vibes are totally off; these people don't share our ways, and prefer to keep to themselves. We'd best back off for now.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1336`

## progress.6.h.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogress.6.h.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

他们慢慢会明白的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1155`

### English

They should get with the times.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1337`

## pirate.1.desc.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.1.desc.storm&lang=en)

### 中文

太空海盗！即使他们这种想法也令人兴奋，这个自称为§S[pirate_band.GetName]§!的团体把目光转向了我们。\n\n虽然我们可以尊重他们选择的生活方式，但这对我们贸易网络产生的影响可不太理想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1156`

### English

Space pirates! Even the thought is a thrill, and a group calling themselves §S[pirate_band.GetName]§! have turned their sights on us.\n\nWhile we can respect their choice of lifestyle, the effect it's having on our trade networks is less ideal.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1338`

## pirate.2.j.storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apirate.2.j.storm&lang=en)

太不幸了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1157`

### English

Most unfortunate.

## situation_stormfall_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_stormfall_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

极端天气揭示了风暴中令人振奋的力量。如果我们掌握了控制它们的技巧，就能在宇宙尺度上利用这项知识，召唤出属于我们自己的风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1161`

### English

Intense weather has revealed the exhilarating power of storms. If we master their control, we could harness that knowledge on a cosmic scale and call forth a storm of our very own.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1344`

## situation_stormfall_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_stormfall_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将学会控制这些风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1162`

### English

We will learn to master these storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1345`

## situation_stormfall_outcome

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_stormfall_outcome&lang=en)

This planet's relentless storms have driven its lifeforms into two distinct paths: those who seek shelter from the chaos, and those who revel in the thrill of the tempest.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1372`

## PROJECT_TEMPEST_BREWING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APROJECT_TEMPEST_BREWING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这场千载难逢的风暴具有一种非常适合调制的独特性质。但首先我们必须加深对其构成的理解。谁知道我们会给生活带来什么奇妙的东西呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1200`

### English

This once-in-a-lifetime storm system has special properties that make it ripe for modulation. But first, we'll need to better understand its underlying composition. Who knows what brilliant oddity we may bring to life?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1388`

## cstorms.3100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴将临

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1202`

### English

Stormfall

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1391`

## cstorms.3100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[owner.capital_scope.GetName]§!上自然发生的风暴向我们展现了大自然的洪荒之力——既有暴风雨的惊心动魄，也有毁灭之路通向的新生。\n\n最近，全球各地的气象站都发现了一个不寻常的现象：一组超级风暴网络即将席卷全球。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]称之为“正义的机缘巧合”。这些罕见的风暴创造了完美的条件来让我们见证一些最激进的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1203`

### English

Naturally occurring storms on §Y[owner.capital_scope.GetName]§! have shown us the awe-inspiring power of nature - both the thrilling intensity of the tempest and the renewal that follows its path of destruction.\n\nRecently, weather stations across the planet have detected an extraordinary phenomenon: a network of superstorms poised to sweep across the world.\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] call it 'righteous serendipity.' These rare tempests create the perfect conditions to witness something truly radical.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1392`

## cstorms.3100.desc.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3100.desc.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

自然发生的风暴向我们展示了大自然令人敬畏的力量——既有暴风雨那令人胆战心惊的剧烈，也有随之而来的万物新生。\n\n最近，方舟舰的传感器在周围的虚空中检测到一种非同寻常的现象：一个超级风暴网络即将在该星系中爆发。\n\n我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]将其称为“正义的机缘”。这些罕见的暴风雨创造了完美的条件，让我们得以见证某种真正彻底的变革。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1204`

### English

Naturally occurring storms have shown us the awe-inspiring power of nature - both the thrilling intensity of the tempest and the renewal that follows in its wake.\n\nRecently, the arkship's sensors detected an extraordinary phenomenon in the surrounding void: a network of superstorms poised to break across this very system.\n\nOur [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] call it 'righteous serendipity.' These rare tempests create the perfect conditions to witness something truly radical.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1393`

## cstorms.3100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

带上勘察设备！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1205`

### English

Grab the field gear!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1394`

## cstorms.3105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

暴风欲来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1206`

### English

Tempest Brewing

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1396`

## cstorms.3105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]发现了风暴操纵的秘密。风暴能量预测已经突破了有史以来的一切记录。我们即将经受考验，并证明我们是最值得的。\n\n特殊的建筑可以作为提高风暴的协调中心。§Y[owner.capital_scope.GetName]§!上提升风暴的吸引力的建筑将加速我们向最终风暴的发展进程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1207`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] have discovered the secrets of storm manipulation. The storm power projections are off the charts and we're all here for it. We will be tested and found most worthy.\n\nSpecial buildings can serve as coordination centers for raising storms. A building on §Y[owner.capital_scope.GetName]§! that increases storm attraction will accelerate our progress towards the ultimate storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1397`

## cstorms.3105.desc.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3105.desc.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]已经揭开了操控风暴的奥秘。预测显示，风暴的力量将突破极限，而我们正是为此齐聚于此。我们将接受考验，并被证明是最为称职之人。\n\n只要让方舟舰稳定驻留在这个星系中，并悉心培育正在汇聚的风暴，我们就能加速迈向终极风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1208`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] have discovered the secrets of storm manipulation. The storm power projections are off the charts and we're all here for it. We will be tested and found most worthy.\n\nBy holding the arkship steady in this system and tending the gathering tempest, we will accelerate our progress towards the ultimate storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1398`

### English

## cstorms.3105.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3105.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

首都行星上有§Y风暴吸引建筑§!：\n$situation_speed_tt$：§G+10%§!\n\n首都恒星系空间站上有§Y$sm_storm_attraction_computer$§!：\n$situation_speed_tt$：§G+10%§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1210`

### English

A §Ystorm attraction building§! on the capital planet:\n$situation_speed_tt$: §G+10%§!\n\nA §Y$sm_storm_attraction_computer$§! in the capital system station:\n$situation_speed_tt$: §G+10%§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1400`

将方舟舰留在§Y[stormfall_system.GetName]§!恒星系中，以应对正在聚集的风暴：\n$situation_speed_tt$：§G+10%§!\n\n

### English

Keep the arkship in the §Y[stormfall_system.GetName]§! system to tend the gathering storm:\n$situation_speed_tt$: §G+10%§!\n\n

## cstorms.3110.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3110.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

通过向自然风暴中注入能量，我们可以为电力风暴创造出条件。\n\n操纵行星核心可以改变[owner.capital_scope.GetName]的磁场方向，从而引起磁力风暴。\n\n最后，通过在行星的特定位置逆转快子的极性，场的变化将可以用于产生引力风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1213`

### English

By injecting energy into the natural storms, we can generate the conditions for electric storms.\n\nPlanetary core manipulation could reorient the magnetic field of [owner.capital_scope.GetName] to give rise to magnetic storms.\n\nFinally, by reversing the tachyon polarity at specific planetary sites, field changes can produce gravity storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1404`

## cstorms.3110.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3110.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经学会了如何启动[owner.capital_scope.GetName]的狂暴振动，以及将它们塑造成一种终极体验。但是我们必须决定哪一种风暴是最为壮观的。\n\n$cstorms.3110.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1214`

### English

We have learned how to kickstart the tempestuous vibrations of [owner.capital_scope.GetName], shaping them into the ultimate experience. But we must decide which storm type is the most glorious.\n\n$cstorms.3110.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1405`

## cstorms.3110.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3110.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经学会了如何启动[owner.capital_scope.GetName]的狂暴振动，以及将它们塑造成一种终极体验。但是我们尚未决定哪一种风暴是最为壮观的。\n\n$cstorms.3110.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1215`

### English

We have learned how to kickstart the tempestuous vibrations of [owner.capital_scope.GetName], shaping them into the ultimate experience. But we have yet to decide which storm type is the most glorious.\n\n$cstorms.3110.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1406`

## cstorms.3110.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3110.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经学会了如何启动[owner.capital_scope.GetName]的狂暴振动，以及将它们塑造成一种终极体验。但是在试图确定哪种风暴类型是最为壮观时，我们的价值判断进程陷入了递归循环。\n\n$cstorms.3110.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1216`

### English

We have learned how to kickstart the tempestuous vibrations of [owner.capital_scope.GetName], shaping them into the ultimate experience. But our value judgment processes are stuck in recursive loops trying to determine which storm type is the most glorious.\n\n$cstorms.3110.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1407`

## cstorms.3110.desc.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3110.desc.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经学会如何启动方舟舰周围狂暴的振荡，并将其塑造成终极体验。但我们必须决定哪种风暴类型最为壮观。\n\n通过将原始能量引入周围的风暴，我们可以创造出形成电暴的条件。\n\n重新调整作用于方舟船体的磁场方向，或许能引发磁暴。\n\n最后，通过逆转我们周围空间中的超光速粒子极性，场的变化可以产生重力风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1217`

### English

We have learned how to kickstart the tempestuous vibrations surrounding the arkship, shaping them into the ultimate experience. But we must decide which storm type is the most glorious.\n\nBy channeling raw power into the surrounding storms, we can generate the conditions for electric storms.\n\nReorienting the magnetic fields that play across the arkship's hull could give rise to magnetic storms.\n\nFinally, by reversing the tachyon polarity in the space around us, field changes can produce gravity storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1408`

### 中文

出发吧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1224`

### English

Get Out There

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1416`

## cstorms.3130.desc.electric

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3130.desc.electric&lang=en)

### 中文

天雷滚滚，电闪嘶鸣！\n\n这些强大的放电过程不仅使行星表面带上电荷，而且也让我们感受到了真正的兴奋！虽然农业将遭受电力风暴的影响，但发电的潜力是不可否认的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1225`

### English

The sky rumbles. Lightning sizzles all around us!\n\nNot only have these powerful discharges electrified the planet's surface, but they've left us truly amped! While farming will suffer from Electrical Storms, the potential for energy generation is undeniable.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1417`

## cstorms.3130.desc.magnetic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3130.desc.magnetic&lang=en)

### 中文

[owner.capital_scope.GetName]的磁场剧烈扭曲着，罗盘也随之狂乱起舞。\n\n通过钻穿我们母星的地壳，我们将磁化物质直接注入地核，自然风暴放大了由此产生的磁场波动。这将妨碍能源生产，但会改善对新矿藏的探测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1226`

### English

Compasses spin wildly as the magnetic field of [owner.capital_scope.GetName] contorts itself.\n\nBy boring through the crust of our homeworld, we were able to inject magnetized matter directly into the core, and the natural storms amplified the resulting field fluctuations. This will hamper energy generation, but improve the detection of new mineral deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1418`

## cstorms.3130.desc.gravity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3130.desc.gravity&lang=en)

### 中文

简单的运动在如今也是如此令人感到前所未有地兴奋！重力在难以察觉的瞬间增大减小。\n\n分散在[owner.capital_scope.GetName]各地的粒子操作站逆转了快子的极性，将它们聚焦成噼啪作响的能量球。当光球球迅速消失时——[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]相信它们被送回了过去——引力波在各个方向荡漾。\n\n由此产生的波动将使行星表面作业变得困难，但可以改善促进在轨空间站的岗位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1227`

### English

Never before has simple movement around been so exhilarating! Gravity increases and decreases without a moment's notice.\n\nParticle manipulation sites scattered across [owner.capital_scope.GetName] reversed the polarity of tachyons, which focused them into crackling balls of energy. As the orbs quickly vanished - [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] believe they were sent backward in time - gravity waves rippled in all directions.\n\nThe resulting fluctuations will make working on the planet's surface harder, but could improve jobs on orbiting stations.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1419`

## cstorms.3130.desc.electric.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3130.desc.electric.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空噼啪作响，闪电划过方舟舰的船体！\n\n这些强大的放电让我们的船员真正振奋起来。虽然我们的水培系统将遭受电磁风暴的破坏，但其发电潜力不容置疑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1228`

### English

The void crackles. Lightning sizzles across the arkship's hull!\n\nThese powerful discharges have left our crew truly amped. While our hydroponics will suffer from the electrical storms, the potential for energy generation is undeniable.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1420`

## cstorms.3130.desc.magnetic.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3130.desc.magnetic.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

方舟舰周围的磁场剧烈扭曲，舰上每一个罗盘都在疯狂旋转。\n\n通过向周围的风暴中排放磁化物质，我们放大了其磁场波动。这会妨碍能源生产，但也能提高发现新矿物的几率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1229`

### English

Every compass aboard spins wildly as the magnetic fields around the arkship contort themselves.\n\nBy venting magnetized matter into the surrounding storms, we amplified their field fluctuations. This will hamper energy generation, but improve the detection of new mineral deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1421`

## cstorms.3130.desc.gravity.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3130.desc.gravity.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

从未有过，仅仅穿行于各层甲板之间就如此惊心动魄！重力在毫无预兆的情况下时强时弱。\n\n方舟舰各处的场发射器逆转了快子的极性，将其聚焦成噼啪作响的能量球。随着这些能量球迅速消失——[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] 认为它们被送回了过去——引力波向四面八方荡漾开来。\n\n由此产生的波动会让舰上的工作更加困难，但也可能提高轨道空间站的工作效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1230`

### English

Never before has simple movement through the decks been so exhilarating! Gravity swells and fades without a moment's notice.\n\nField emitters across the arkship reversed the polarity of tachyons, focusing them into crackling balls of energy. As the orbs quickly vanished - [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] believe they were sent backward in time - gravity waves rippled in all directions.\n\nThe resulting fluctuations will make work aboard harder, but could improve jobs on orbiting stations.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1422`

## cstorms.3140.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3140.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]相信，我们用来刺激行星风暴的想法可以应用到更大的范围中去，现在整个恒星系都在狂暴的能量中旋转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1235`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] are convinced that the same ideas that we devoted to prodding the planetary storms could apply on a much larger scale, now that the whole system swirls with tempestuous energy.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1428`

## cstorms.3140.desc.electric

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3140.desc.electric&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3140.desc.intro$\n\n我们可以利用现成的知识和基础设施将其转化为一场$electric_storm$，也可以创造一些刺激的新东西。\n\n引入特定的矿物将会增强风暴的磁场共振，而利用合金则会将其变为一个重力井。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1236`

### English

$cstorms.3140.desc.intro$\n\nWe can use existing knowledge and infrastructure to transform it into an $electric_storm$, or we create something new and exciting.\n\nIntroducing certain minerals would amplify the storm's magnetic resonance, while utilizing alloys would instead transform the storm into a gravity well.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1429`

## cstorms.3140.desc.magnetic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3140.desc.magnetic&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3140.desc.intro$\n\n我们可以利用现成的知识和基础设施将其转化为一场$magnetic_storm$，也可以创造一些刺激的新东西。\n\n充能恒星将会增强风暴的电力潜能，而利用合金则会将其变为一个重力井。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1237`

### English

$cstorms.3140.desc.intro$\n\nWe can use existing knowledge and infrastructure to transform it into an $magnetic_storm$, or we create something new and exciting.\n\nCharging our sun would amplify the storm's electric potential, while utilizing alloys would instead transform the storm into a gravity well.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1430`

## cstorms.3140.desc.gravity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3140.desc.gravity&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3140.desc.intro$\n\n我们可以利用现成的知识和基础设施将其转化为一场$gravity_storm$，也可以创造一些刺激的新东西。\n\n引入特定的矿物将会增强风暴的磁场共振，而充能恒星将会增强风暴的电力潜能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1238`

### English

$cstorms.3140.desc.intro$\n\nWe can use existing knowledge and infrastructure to transform it into an $gravity_storm$, or we create something new and exciting.\n\nIntroducing certain minerals would amplify the storm's magnetic resonance, while charging our sun would instead amplify the storm's electric potential.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1431`

## cstorms.3140.a

给恒星充能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1239`

### English

Charge up the sun.

播种一场宇宙§Y$electric_storm$§!。\n§Y2§!年内：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_country_energy_produces_mult$：§R-50%§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$mod_country_unity_produces_mult$：§G+20%§!

## cstorms.3160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴之后

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1249`

### English

After the Storm

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1443`

## cstorms.3160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着行星风暴的消散，[owner.capital_scope.GetName]上又出现了一个新的长期特征。风暴又一次带来了新生的机遇。\n\n虽然这场局部风暴令人振奋，但是我们也只能畅想着一场真正的宇宙风暴的可能性。为什么它要花这么长时间？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1250`

### English

When the planetary storm dispersed, a new lasting feature appeared on [owner.capital_scope.GetName]. Once again a storm has brought about change and rebirth.\n\nAs exhilarating as this local storm was, we can only imagine the possibilities of a true cosmic storm. Why does it take so long?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1444`

## cstorms.3160.desc.nomad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3160.desc.nomad&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴消散后，在方舟舰本身留下了永久的印记。风暴再次带来了改变与新生。\n\n尽管这一切令人振奋，我们也只能想象真正的宇宙风暴会带来怎样的可能性。为什么它迟迟不来？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1251`

### English

When the storm dispersed, it left a lasting mark upon the arkship itself. Once again a storm has brought about change and rebirth.\n\nAs exhilarating as this was, we can only imagine the possibilities of a true cosmic storm. Why does it take so long?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1445`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3160.a&lang=en)

## cstorms.3170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙塑形

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1259`

### English

Cosmic Shapes

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1454`

## cstorms.3170.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3170.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以加速巨大的宇宙风暴的涌现，但我们也必须扪心自问——我们真的准备好为此命悬一线了吗？\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1260`

### English

We can hasten the appearance of the immense cosmic storm now taking shape, but must ask ourselves - are we truly ready for the ride of our lives?\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1455`

## cstorms.3170.desc.scientist.snippet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3170.desc.scientist.snippet&lang=en)

### 中文

一支由§Y[event_target:storm_scientist.GetName]§!带领的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]队伍可以研究这些早期的宇宙风暴模式，以发现操作它们生成的理想途径。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1261`

### English

A team of [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] led by §Y[event_target:storm_scientist.GetName]§! could research these early cosmic storm patterns to find optimal ways to manipulate their formation.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1456`

## cstorms.3170.desc.commander.snippet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3170.desc.commander.snippet&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们让§Y[event_target:storm_commander.GetName]§!带领一支军队过去，他们能够引爆萌发中的风暴，让能量倾泻而下。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1262`

### English

If we send the military led by §Y[event_target:storm_commander.GetName]§!, they can blast the burgeoning tempest and let the energy cascade.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1457`

## cstorms.3170.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3170.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

这支由领袖带领的队伍未必能安全回来。然而，他们也有成就一番伟大事业的可能！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1263`

### English

This leader-led team risks an uncertain return. Then again, they may achieve something awesome!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1458`

## cstorms.3170.desc.none

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3170.desc.none&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在能做的只有坐等宇宙风暴成型。\n\n这种等待让人心急如焚，但也会让回报来得更猛烈一些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1267`

### English

All we can do now is wait for the cosmic storm to take shape.\n\nThe anticipation is almost unbearable, but will make the payoff even greater.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1462`

## cstorms.3190.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3190.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前无古人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1273`

### English

The First of Many

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1470`

## cstorms.3190.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3190.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

起初只是宇宙织构中的一点波纹。能量在这里舞动、融合、碰撞。加一点奇异粒子、再一点质量，最后，附近一颗恒星的引力使这一切都倾泻到我们面前的这只野兽身上。\n\n这个按照我们的意愿雕刻而来的自然和智慧的奇迹主宰了天空，并激起了狂热的兴奋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1274`

### English

It began with a wrinkle in the cosmic fabric. Energies danced, merged, and collided. A hint of exotic particles, a dash of mass, and finally, the nascent storm that circles our star precipitated the beast before us.\n\nSculpted according to our wishes, this blessing of nature and ingenuity dominates the skies.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1471`

## cstorms.3190.desc.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3190.desc.success&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3190.desc.intro$我们的领袖穿越风暴，激起了一阵狂热的兴奋。\n\n我们做到了。只有我们做到了。让我们乘风破浪，享受一生难得的快感吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1275`

### English

$cstorms.3190.desc.intro$ Our leader riding out through the storm has whipped up a frenzy of excitement.\n\nWe did this. We alone did this. Let us ride this storm and embrace the thrill of a lifetime!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1472`

## cstorms.3190.desc.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3190.desc.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3190.desc.intro$我们的领袖迷失在风暴中，但这是多么好的出路！\n\n我们做到了。只有我们做到了。让我们乘风破浪，享受一生难得的快感吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1276`

### English

$cstorms.3190.desc.intro$ Our leader was lost in the storm, but what a way to go out!\n\nWe did this. We alone did this. Let us ride this storm and embrace the thrill of a lifetime!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1473`

## cstorms.3190.desc.wait

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3190.desc.wait&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3190.desc.intro$我们已经等待了太久，但这一天终于到了。\n\n我们做到了。只有我们做到了。让我们乘风破浪，享受一生难得的快感吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1277`

### English

$cstorms.3190.desc.intro$ We have waited long, but the day is finally here.\n\nWe did this. We alone did this. Let us ride this storm and embrace the thrill of a lifetime!

### English

## cstorms.3205.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3205.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙的振动消失了，一如既往的壮举却没有像往常一般唤起人们的兴奋。我们毫无疑问完成了难以置信的事情，但人们都感到缺少了一些东西。我们祖先的成就，从水平和野心上都是传奇，向身后的我们投下了长长的阴影。\n\n“这些斗争是最存在主义的！”我们的一位顶尖人士最近写道。“我们是在穿越了虚空之后，让自己陷入了空虚之中？”\n\n焦虑是真实存在的，现在是采取行动的时候了。为了重新开始，我们回到起点：制定一系列挑战，以最大限度激发肾上腺素！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1287`

### English

The cosmic vibrations are off, and the usual feats just don't evoke the same excitement they used to. There's no doubt we've accomplished incredible things, but there is a pervasive sense that something is missing. The achievements of our ancestors, legendary in their scope and ambition, cast a long shadow.\n\n"These struggles are most existential!" one of our top minds recently wrote. "By traversing the void, have we opened a void in ourselves?"\n\nThe angst is real, and the time to act is now. To start fresh, we return to the beginning: a series of challenges custom designed to invoke maximum adrenaline!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1488`

## cstorms.3205.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3205.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙的振动消失了，一如既往的壮举却没有像往常一般唤起人们的兴奋。我们毫无疑问完成了难以置信的事情，但人们都感到缺少了一些东西。\n\n我们被这样的问题所困扰着：我们真的知道自己是谁吗？我们的光辉岁月已经过去了吗？作为一个集体，我们有责任成为最好的自己。\n\n焦虑是真实存在的，现在是采取行动的时候了。为了重新开始，我们回到起点：制定一系列挑战，以最大限度激发肾上腺素！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1288`

### English

The cosmic vibrations are off, and the usual feats just don't evoke the same excitement they used to. There's no doubt we've accomplished incredible things, but there is a pervasive sense that something is missing.\n\nWe are plagued by questions: do we truly know who we are? Are our glory days behind us? As a collective, we have a responsibility to become the best version of ourselves.\n\nThe angst is real, and the time to act is now. To start fresh, we return to the beginning: a series of challenges custom designed to invoke maximum adrenaline!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1489`

## cstorms.3215.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3215.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做到了！\n\n我们的目标是追求纯粹的刺激，我们也已经迎接了每一个挑战。见证我们在往昔岁月里所取得的成就吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1292`

### English

We've done it!\n\nSetting our sights on the pure pursuit of thrills, we've met every challenge. Witness what we've accomplished in the past years!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1493`

## cstorms.3215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.3215.intro$\n\n就像我们的杰出先烈们一样，我们超越了自己的极限。\n\n甚至，由于与自然的狂怒抗衡，我们还获得了对自身存在价值的难以置信的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1293`

### English

$cstorms.3215.intro$\n\nLike the most excellent pioneers of old, we have pushed ourselves beyond our limits.\n\nEven better, due to our engagement with nature's fury, we have gained incredible insight into the meaning of our existence.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1494`

### 中文

我们一直在挑战极限。在前人事迹的鼓舞下，我们现在有机会塑造自己梦想的风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1323`

### English

We have always pushed the limits. Inspired by the deeds of our predecessors, we now have the chance to sculpt the storm of our dreams.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1527`

## cstorms.3220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

追风者信条

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1324`

### English

A Chaser's Creed

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1529`

## cstorms.3220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人民永无止境地追求刺激。每天我们都会发现不可思议的情景，面临更大的挑战，但[ruler.GetRegnalName]觉得我们还能更进一步：\n\n“宇宙风暴的核心唯有潜能；那是真正的、最尖锐的前沿。它尖锐到足以斩断现状，释放出一生难得一次的快感。”\n\n挑起一场真正史诗级风暴的时机已到，这将会是一场混乱的狂欢：浩宇风暴。现在只剩下两个小细节：一是要进行最后的研究，以敲定如何触发完美风暴，二是星系的其他人对我们是否应该这么做有什么看法。\n\n实现这样的东西肯定会激起一些怨恨的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1325`

### English

Our people will never stop looking for a thrill. Every day we discover incredible situations and face greater challenges, but [ruler.GetRegnalName] thinks we can go even further:\n\n"The core of a cosmic storm is pure potential; the true, bleeding edge. An edge sharp enough to sever the status-quo and unleash the rush of a lifetime."\n\nThe time is ripe to provoke a storm of truly epic proportions, a nexus of joyous pandemonium: The Big One. Only two minor details remain: a final study to determine exactly how to invoke the perfect storm, and the opinion of the rest of the galaxy should we choose to do so.\n\nBringing something like this into being is sure to stir up some resentment.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1530`

## PROJECT_SOW_THE_BIG_ONE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APROJECT_SOW_THE_BIG_ONE&lang=en)

### 中文

引动浩宇风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1326`

### English

Entice The Big One

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1532`

## PROJECT_SOW_THE_BIG_ONE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APROJECT_SOW_THE_BIG_ONE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有培植风暴的技术，附近却没有初生的风暴。我们需要研究如何播种出一个可以培植的风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1327`

### English

We have the technology, but without a nascent storm in the vicinity, we will need to investigate how to seed a storm we can sculpt.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1533`

## cstorms.3222.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3222.name&lang=en)

### 中文

暗物质培植

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1328`

### English

Dark Matter Cultivation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1535`

## cstorms.3222.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3222.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

暗物质独一无二的性质使其成为浩宇风暴的完美导火索。我们将向环绕风暴中心的太空织构注入这种难以捉摸的物质，等待风暴的第一波涟漪出现。\n\n然而，这件事已经引起了多余的关注：邻国们开始抗议了。“不是谁都跟我们一样兴高采烈地随风逐浪，”我们一位[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]埋怨道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1329`

### English

The unique properties of Dark Matter make it the perfect thing to stir up The Big One. We will inject this elusive material into the spatial fabric surrounding the storm's heart and wait for the first ripples of the storm to appear.\n\nHowever, this has already attracted some unwanted attention: our neighbors have begun to protest. "Not everyone is as stoked as us to ride this particular wave," complained one of our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural].

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1536`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1333`

### English

## cstorms.3224.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3224.name&lang=en)

### 中文

浩宇风暴的萌动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1334`

### English

Onset of The Big One

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1543`

## cstorms.3224.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3224.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

难以置信！即将来临的风暴的萌动已经能感觉到了。我们[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]的还在努力着，但使我们离引发浩宇风暴又近了一步。\n\n然而，我们的邻国并不太满意。他们声称我们在胡搞我们不可能理解的力量，这已经影响了我们间的关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1335`

### English

Incredible! The nascent vibrations of the coming storm are already being felt. The work of our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] is ongoing, but we are now one step closer to summoning The Big One.\n\nHowever, our neighbors are less pleased. They claim we're meddling with powers we cannot possibly comprehend, and this has already affected our relations.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1544`

## cstorms.3224.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3224.a&lang=en)

### English

It is what it is.

## cstorms.3225.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3225.name&lang=en)

### 中文

饲育浩宇风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1337`

### English

Feeding the Big One

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1547`

## cstorms.3225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数据已然明晰：这场风暴可谓种瓜得瓜，种豆得豆。既是如此，若是我们给风暴饲喂合适类型的资源，它就能拥有任何一种风暴的特性，并达到史诗般的规模。\n\n星系的住民们再次表达了担忧，但世纪之旅已指日可待。该穷尽国力饲育风暴了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1338`

### English

The data is clear: whatever we put into this storm, something similar will come out of it. Ergo, if we feed it the right type of resources, then it will take on properties - and epic proportions- of every type of storm.\n\nThe people of the galaxy continue to express concern, but the ride of the century is just waiting to be born. Let us feed it what we have!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1548`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3225.a&lang=en)

饲育风暴！

## cstorms.3226.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3226.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

若是我们想要创造一场史诗般的风暴，就必须为这场初生的风暴提供种种养分。这项惊人的壮举需要我们手头上一组特定的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1342`

### English

We must provide the nascent storm a wide variety of nourishment if we wish to create a storm of epic proportions. This amazing feat requires a specific sampling of resources at our disposal.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1553`

## cstorms.3227.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3227.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后推手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1347`

### English

The Final Push

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1561`

## cstorms.3227.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3227.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

兴奋之情溢于言表。前方的道路越发清晰：前进，前进，向着目标前进。让我们与宇宙的狂热融为一体！万事俱备，只需稍加一把推手，浩宇风暴就要来临。\n\n我们的邻国已然怒不可遏，声称我们所谓的“$nexus_storm$”将对星系造成巨大的破坏。但我们真要让这些扫兴的家伙毁掉这一史诗般的项目吗？抉择的时刻到了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1348`

### English

The excitement is palpable. The path ahead grows clear: push further, push harder, push through. Let us become one with cosmic frenzy itself! Everything is now ready for the Big One. We just need to give one small push.\n\nOur neighbors are already beyond livid, claiming that our so-called '$nexus_storm$' will wreak havoc on the galaxy. But are we really going to let these killjoys derail a project of such epic proportions? The time to choose is now.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1562`

### English

## cstorms.3230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们站在新体验的悬崖边。终极灾难的时刻到了。我们的风暴骑士无所畏惧，从没有他们不肯接受的挑战。让星系听见我们的名字吧！\n\n让整个星系开开眼界！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1354`

### English

Here we are - at the precipice of a new experience. The time of ultimate calamity has arrived. There is nothing our Storm Riders won't face. No challenge we will ever deny.\n\nLet the galaxy witness!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1569`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴尾声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1357`

### English

Storm Coda

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1573`

## cstorms.3235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们学到了很多。终于知道星系不是玩物，而是一场有待体验的冒险。\n\n[ruler.GetRegnalName]说得好：“人生从不缺刺激。我们永远不会停止追求下一个挑战，但我们必须怀着敬意。与星系及其居民和谐共处是我们的准则。我们选择了这么做，这才是我们真正的意义所在。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1358`

### English

We have learned much. Now we understand that the galaxy is not a toy to be played with, but an adventure to be experienced.\n\n[ruler.GetRegnalName] said it best: "In this life, there are no shortages of thrills. We'll never stop seeking the next challenge, but we must do so with respect. This is what we have chosen. This is who we are truly meant to be.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1574`

## cstorms.3235.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3235.a&lang=en)

### 中文

放轻松！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1359`

### English

Hang loose!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1575`

## cstorms.3235.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.3235.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

$every_empire_opinion_hit$§G+$@the_code_total_change$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1360`

### English

为了再创新高，感受欢愉以及挑战我们的极限，我们现在开始一系列试炼。欢乐时间到！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1363`

To reach new heights, to know joy and to test our limits, we now embark on a series of trials. Let the fun begin!

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_1&lang=en)

### 中文

在风暴内战胜一支舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1365`

### English

Defeat a Fleet Inside a Storm

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1584`

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

激烈的战斗一直算是试金石。现在，想象一下在宇宙风暴的狂暴核心内体会战斗！让我们向星系展示我们的狂野。\n\n当前挑战是在风暴中击败一支敌方舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1366`

### English

The heat of battle has always been a proving ground. Now imagine experiencing it from within the raging heart of a cosmic storm! Let's show the galaxy just how wild we really are.\n\nThe challenge now is to defeat an enemy fleet in a storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1585`

## fleets_defeated_in_storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afleets_defeated_in_storm&lang=en)

在风暴内战胜舰队

### English

Fleets Defeated Inside a Storm

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_2&lang=en)

### 中文

探索星系的边缘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1368`

### English

Explore the Edge of the Galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1588`

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

超越我们的边界，徘徊在群星边缘，探索太空最遥远的深处——还有什么比这更令人振奋吗？\n\n我们需要派遣一艘科研船前往指定的恒星系——不择手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1369`

### English

To go beyond our own borders, to balance on the edge of the stars, to seek the furthest reaches of space - is there anything more invigorating?\n\nWe'll need to send a Science Ship to the designated system - any way we can.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1589`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_3&lang=en)

### 中文

驾驭风暴

### English

Ride the Storms

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_3_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_3_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

最优秀的人才渴望风暴的刺激。让他们在风暴中驰骋、绽放吧！\n\n我们的下一项挑战是帮助一位风暴骑士在宇宙风暴中坚持超过180天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1372`

### English

The best of the best yearn for the thrill of the storm. Let them cruise and thrive!\n\nOur next challenge is to help a Storm Rider weather a cosmic storm for more than 180 days.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1593`

## days_storm_rider_spend_in_storm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adays_storm_rider_spend_in_storm&lang=en)

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_4&lang=en)

### 中文

出土调查

### English

Investigate the Unearthed

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_4_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_4_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

星系中充满埋藏的宝藏与古老的秘密——还有一股邪恶的风将星系搅动！让我们出发吧，去看看能找到什么。\n\n挑战自己，去调查一处被风暴揭示的异常现象或考古遗址吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1375`

### English

A galaxy full of buried treasure and ancient secrets - and a wicked wind to stir them up! Let's get out there and see what we can find.\n\nLet's challenge ourselves to investigate an anomaly or archaeology site revealed by a storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1597`

## MISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_5_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSION_CS_STORM_FEVER_CHALLENGE_5_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么要独享乐趣呢？就冒险而言，分享多多益善。\n\n为了让我们的邻国也感受到刺激，当前挑战是让一场宇宙风暴偏转到他们的领土。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1378`

### English

Why should we have all the fun? When it comes to adventure, it's always better to share.\n\nTo help our neighbors feel included, the challenge now is to cause a cosmic storm to veer into their space.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1601`

### 中文

老去然后淡出，这不是我们的风格。耀眼地燃烧殆尽远胜于此。\n\n下一个挑战是帮助风暴骑士在宇宙风暴体验最终时刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1381`

It is not in our style to grow old and fade away. Far better to burn out in a blaze of glory!\n\nThe next challenge is to help a Storm Rider experience their final moments inside a cosmic storm.

### 中文

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\n钻研远古的秘密、发掘埋藏的宝藏总是这么令人兴奋。谁知道外面的世界还有什么等着我们去发现呢?

## cstorms.325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\n这场风暴径直穿过了他们的空间，在同等程度上传播了兴奋和混沌！为邻居们提供了如此令人难以置信的体验，这下我们可以放心了。\n\n他们现在可能不感激，但最终会感谢我们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1405`

### English

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\nThat storm tore right through their space, spreading thrills and disarray in equal measure! We can rest easy knowing we provided our neighbors with such an incredible experience\n\nThey may not appreciate it now, but they'll thank us eventually.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1631`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.330.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\n强大的风暴呼唤他的名字，他也欣喜地回应。致敬风暴骑士，你是绝对的传奇！愿你通往彼岸的旅程如同你的退场一样壮丽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1409`

### English

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\nThe great storm called their name and they answered with jubilation. Hail Storm Rider, you absolute legend! May your journey to the great beyond be as epic as your exit.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1635`

## cstorms.330.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.330.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\n强大的风暴呼唤我们的一部分加入它的欢腾肆虐。从未有过对失去节点感觉如此有意义和充满活力！这一史诗般的经历将在未来许多年里定义我们是谁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1410`

### English

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\nThe great storm has called a part of ourself to join it in jubilant carnage. Losing a node has never felt so meaningful and invigorating! This epic experience will define our species for many years to come.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1636`

## cstorms.330.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.330.a&lang=en)

### 中文

乘风破浪吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1411`

### English

Ride on!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1637`

## cstorms.331.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.331.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\n大风暴呼唤着他，他也欢欣鼓舞地回应着。向风暴骑士致敬，您绝对是个传奇!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1412`

### English

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\nThe great storm called their name and they answered with jubilation. Hail Storm Rider, you absolute legend!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1638`

## cstorms.331.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.331.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\n大风暴召唤了我们的一部分加入它的欢腾屠杀。从来没有一次近乎失去一个节点的感觉是如此地有意义，令我们充满活力！这一史诗般的经历将在未来的日子里告诉我们自己是谁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1413`

### English

$challenge_completed_intro$\n\nThe great storm has called a part of ourself to join it in jubilant carnage. Nearly losing a node has never felt so meaningful and invigorating! This epic experience will define our species for many years to come.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1639`

## electric_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

电磁场的碰撞使这场宇宙风暴充满了接近危险水平的电荷。它对护盾有害，但可以提升行星产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1414`

### English

The collision of electromagnetic fields imbues this cosmic storm with near a dangerous level of electric charge. Hazardous for shields, but beneficial for planetary production.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1641`

## particle_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparticle_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种风暴看起来就像活物一般，通过星系织构的内部移动，它们既不猎杀也不躲避太空舰船。它们既危险又不稳定，这种存在足以跨越各种要素凝聚起一个文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1415`

### English

These storms almost seem alive, moving through and within the galactic fabric, neither hunting nor avoiding spaceborne vessels. Dangerous and unstable, their mere presence is enough to unite a civilization in defiance of the elements.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1642`

## gravity_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agravity_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每当发生大型天体碰撞，造成的影响将向整个星系散播引力涟漪。在涟漪相撞的地方，引力风暴就会诞生。对舰船的火控系统会造成负面影响，而且波动的重力场迫使大多数人口闭门不出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1416`

### English

When massive stellar bodies collide, the resulting impact sends gravitational ripples throughout the galaxy. When these in turn collapse, a gravity storm is born. Negative effects on ship targeting systems, and the fluctuating gravity makes almost everyone forced to stay at home.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1643`

## magnetic_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagnetic_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当游荡的金属小行星带与强大的引力相互作用时，其内部就会形成磁场。这种类型的风暴会撕裂船体、导致工业建造中断，但它们也有助于从地壳中带出有价值的矿物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1417`

### English

When roving metallic asteroid belts interact with strong gravitational forces, magnetic fields are formed within. Storms of this type rip hulls apart and cause interruptions to industrial construction - yet they also assist in bringing up valuable minerals from a planet's crust.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1644`

## solar_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asolar_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$solar_storm$会在空间织构中创造出一种耀眼的波动。它的存在导致恒星产生前所未有的耀斑。由此产生的辐射极具破坏性，但可以超载空间站产出并让舰船难以隐形。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1418`

### English

A $solar_storm$ creates a glistening fluctuation within the spatial fabric. Its presence causes stars to produce unprecedented flares. The resultant radiation is extremely damaging, but supercharges space station output and makes it hard for ships to stay hidden.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1645`

## celestial_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acelestial_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每时每刻，来自数百万行星的尘埃都在星系中漫无目的地飘荡。有时，这些颗粒聚集起极大的数量，以至于形成了沙尘暴。虽然这种沙尘暴会干扰整个行星的通信，但其带来的化学物质也对资源产出有益。但这些潜藏在风暴中的物体对我们的扫描仪而言却往往难以捕捉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1419`

### English

At any given moment, dust from millions of planets is drifting aimlessly through the galaxy. Sometimes these granules gather in such significant quantities that a dust storm results. Disturbs planet-wide communication, but the chemical elements it brings are useful for resource production. Objects inside these tempests may as well not exist as far as our scanners are concerned.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1646`

## shroud_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroud_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在极罕见的情况下，虚境会向现实世界低语。这种稀有的风暴会让人们看到崇高而又怪诞的景象。那些与虚境交流的人甚至可以享受到变幻莫测的协同作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1420`

### English

On rare occasions, the Shroud may whisper through the physical world. These rare storms expose sentients to visions both sublime and grotesque. Those who have communed with the Shroud can even enjoy a fickle synergy.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1647`

## nexus_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anexus_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当数颗恒星直列时，即使是很小的宇宙风暴也会成长得超出常规限制。这种罕见的事件将引发世界末日般的恐怖威胁。在这可憎之物里面没有人能够侥幸获益；光是幸存下去就已经谢天谢地了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1421`

### English

When the stars align, even a minor cosmic storm can grow beyond conventional limits. This rare event will unleash terror of apocalyptic proportions. There is no thriving inside this abomination; the only option is to survive.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1648`

## FROM_STORM

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFROM_STORM&lang=en)

### 中文

自风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1422`

### English

From Storm

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1650`

## hyper_ecosystem

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyper_ecosystem&lang=en)

### 中文

超级生态系统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1424`

### English

Hyper Ecosystem

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1653`

## hyper_ecosystem_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyper_ecosystem_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的土地含有从地壳自然形成的矿物中重吸收的高浓度氮、磷元素。这让植物以极夸张的速度生长，对农业有帮助但会妨碍建筑工程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1425`

### English

This planet's soil contains uniquely high concentrations of nitrogen and phosphorus, reabsorbed from naturally formed minerals in the crust. This results in extraordinarily rapid plant growth; great for farming, but hampering any building projects.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1654`

## docile_fauna

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adocile_fauna&lang=en)

### 中文

温顺生物群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1426`

### English

Docile Fauna

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1655`

## docile_fauna_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adocile_fauna_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上的野生动物和植物生长缺乏许多天敌，生得异常温顺，因此与它们一起生活很愉快。种群数量主要受到风暴破坏的制约，而不是适者生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1427`

### English

This planet's wildlife and plant growth lacks many natural predators, making them unusually docile, and thus pleasant to live with. The population is checked by storm damage rather than survival of the fittest.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1656`

## obsidian_lakes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_lakes&lang=en)

### 中文

黑曜石湖泊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1428`

### English

Obsidian Lakes

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1657`

## obsidian_lakes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_lakes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

构造活动使岩浆湖冷却成巨大的黑曜岩，将宝贵的资源带到离地表更近的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1429`

### English

Tectonic activity has caused magma lakes to cool into enormous sheets of obsidian, bringing valuable resources much closer to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1658`

## obsidian_lakes_volcanic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_lakes_volcanic&lang=en)

### English

## crimson_crawlers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers&lang=en)

### 中文

猩红爬虫
