### English

Crimson Crawlers

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1661`

## crimson_crawlers_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§R$COLONIZATION_IMPOSSIBLE_CRIMSON_CRAWLERS$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1433`

### English

§R$COLONIZATION_IMPOSSIBLE_CRIMSON_CRAWLERS$§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1662`

## crimson_crawlers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的表面是一片由万亿的红色小昆虫组成的深红色的地毯。尽管它们的数量惊人，但它们似乎是无害的，而且它们成功地在行星的生态系统内建立了一种脆弱的平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1434`

### English

The planet's surface is a carpet of crimson due to the presence of trillions of tiny red insects. Despite their incredible numbers, they appear harmless, and have managed to establish a tenuous balance within the planet's ecosystem.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1663`

## metal_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ametal_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

金属行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1435`

### English

Metal Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1664`

## metal_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ametal_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星完全被天然的金属所覆盖。它缺乏基本的生活必需品，如水或可呼吸的大气，但确实含有大量可用的资源！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1436`

### English

This planet is entirely covered in crude, naturally occurring metals. It lacks basic life necessities such as water or a breathable atmosphere, but does contain a lot of usable resources!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1665`

## terraform_fail_is_metal_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraform_fail_is_metal_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星存在金属外壳，因此不能被改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1437`

### English

This planet cannot be terraformed due to its metallic crust.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1666`

## previously_terraformed_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreviously_terraformed_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

以前环境改造过的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1438`

### English

Previously Terraformed Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1667`

## previously_terraformed_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreviously_terraformed_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一颗有氮氧大气和大量海洋的岩质星球。有证据表明，更古老的帝国曾经到达并殖民过这个行星，但它们后来消失了。这意味着这里有很多尚待发现与未知的东西，但也让这个行星成为一个预先准备好的殖民地候选者!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1439`

### English

A rocky world with a nitrogen-oxygen atmosphere and plenty of oceans. There is evidence of older empires having reached and colonized this planet before, but they have since disappeared. This means there is a lot to discover and uncover, but also leaves the planet a pre-made candidate for a colony!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1668`

## previously_terraformed_planet_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreviously_terraformed_planet_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

之前的环境改造尝试使得这颗行星更容易改造成为盖亚星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1440`

### English

Previous terraforming attempts make turning this planet into a Gaia world much easier.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1669`

## has_patriotic_institute

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahas_patriotic_institute&lang=en)

### 中文

$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1441`

### English

The $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1670`

## has_patriotic_institut_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahas_patriotic_institut_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球曾是阿达卡利亚公约的基础。这座$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$就是这一度强盛帝国的唯一残迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1442`

### English

This planet was once the foundation of the adAkkaria Convention. The $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$ is all that is left of this once mighty civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1671`

## has_patriotic_institute_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahas_patriotic_institute_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

拥有建筑：$building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$。殖民该行星以获取控制权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1443`

### English

Has building: $building_adakkaria_patriotic_institute$. Colonize this planet to gain ownership.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1672`

## adakkaria_patriotic_institute_desc.supercharged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadakkaria_patriotic_institute_desc.supercharged&lang=en)

### 中文

每年在该星系中以§Y100§!£pop£§H人口§!为代价触发风暴的概率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1444`

### English

Chance every year a Storm is triggered in the system at the cost of §Y100§! £pop£ §HPops§!.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1673`

## crimson_crawlers_sashimi_style

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_sashimi_style&lang=en)

### 中文

猩红爬虫：直接生吃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1446`

### English

Crimson Crawlers: Raw Consumption

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1676`

## crimson_crawlers_sashimi_style_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_sashimi_style_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的表面是由数万亿的小红色昆虫组成的深红色的阴影。即使直接生吃，它们也是一种美味的食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1447`

### English

The planet's surface is a deep shade of crimson due to the presence of trillions of tiny red insects. Even when consumed raw, they are a delectable source of food.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1677`

## crimson_crawlers_bbq_them

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_bbq_them&lang=en)

### 中文

猩红爬虫：煮熟再吃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1448`

### English

Crimson Crawlers: Cooked Consumption

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1678`

## crimson_crawlers_bbq_them_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_crawlers_bbq_them_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的表面是由数万亿的小红色昆虫组成的深红色的阴影。它们是一种人们喜闻乐见的烧烤食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1449`

### English

The planet's surface is a deep shade of crimson due to the presence of trillions of tiny red insects. They have become a joy for the population to grill and consume.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1679`

## COLONIZATION_IMPOSSIBLE_CRIMSON_CRAWLERS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACOLONIZATION_IMPOSSIBLE_CRIMSON_CRAWLERS&lang=en)

### 中文

§R这个行星不能被机器帝国或噬岩者帝国殖民。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1450`

### English

§RThis planet cannot be colonized by Machine or Terravore empires.§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1681`

## collided_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollided_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

被撞的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1452`

### English

Collided Planet

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1684`

## collided_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acollided_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

两颗行星撞到了一起，它们没有互相毁灭，反倒是纠缠在了一起。在增加可用面积的同时，这颗星球也带来了独特的居住和建筑挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1453`

### English

Two planets crashed into one, and rather than destroying each other, they have become entangled. While boasting increased usable surface area, the world poses unique habitation and building challenges.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1685`

## wooden_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awooden_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

树栖星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1454`

### English

Arboreal World

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1686`

## wooden_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awooden_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几千年来，这颗行星的地壳已经变成了一层厚厚的树根，厚达几公里，使它成为一个巨大的生命有机体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1455`

### English

Over thousands of years, this planet's crust has become a dense layer of roots several kilometers thick, making it one titanic living organism.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1687`

## wooden_planet_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awooden_planet_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

建造和维护住房需要花费$w_food$。\n建造农场需要花费$w_food$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1456`

### English

Housing costs $w_food$ to build and upkeep.\nFarms cost $w_food$ to build.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1688`

## recent_eruption

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_eruption&lang=en)

近期火山喷发

### English

Recent Eruption Occurred

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1691`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeruption_report_tooltip&lang=en)

\n汇报将在30天内到达。

### English

\nThe reports will arrive within 30 days.

## colony_mod.31.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.31.name&lang=en)

### 中文

火山喷发！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1460`

### English

Eruption!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1693`

## colony_mod.31.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.31.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的构造变动导致一个巨大板块上的黑曜岩薄层破碎。熔岩倾泻而出遍布地表，造成了毁灭性的影响。从殖民地发回的报告相当可怕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1461`

### English

Tectonic events on [Root.GetName] have caused the Obsidian sheets on one of the giant plates to shatter. Lava pours across the surface to ruinous effect. Reports from the colony are dire.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1694`

## colony_mod.31.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.31.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们听听。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1462`

### English

Let us hear them.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1695`

## colony_mod.32.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.32.name&lang=en)

### 中文

喷发伤害汇报

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1463`

### English

Eruption Damage Report

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1696`

## colony_mod.32.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.32.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近那场震动了我们在[Root.GetName]上殖民地的黑曜岩原野爆发已经演化成了一座活火山！附近的一切都已经被埋葬在熔岩之下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1464`

### English

The recent eruption on the Obsidian Fields that shook our colony on [Root.GetName] has turned into an active volcano! Anything that once existed there is now buried under molten rock.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1697`

## colony_mod.33.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.33.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近那场震动了我们在[Root.GetName]上殖民地的黑曜岩原野爆发奇迹般地没有造成损伤。更幸运的是，它击碎了大量的黑曜石，方便了我们采集。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1465`

### English

The recent eruption on the Obsidian Fields that shook our colony on [Root.GetName] miraculously caused no damage. In a fortunate twist, it has also shattered the Obsidian for easy gathering!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1698`

## colony_mod.34.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_mod.34.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近那场震动了我们在[Root.GetName]上殖民地的黑曜岩原野爆发奇迹般地没有造成损伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1466`

### English

The recent eruption on the Obsidian Fields that shook our colony on [Root.GetName] miraculously caused no damage!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1699`

## electric_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$electric_storm$在大气层中残留下涌动的电力，这会让机械产出变得不稳定。然而，能量产出已经爆表了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1472`

### English

The $electric_storm$ left surging electricity in the atmosphere, causing unreliable output from machinery. However, energy production is off the charts!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1707`

## particle_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparticle_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$particle_storm$可以将一切磨为齑粉，无论是金属还是有机生命，这会造成严重的损伤并影响食物产出。然而，残余物包含着极具价值的物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1481`

### English

$particle_storm$ grind down everything from metal to organic life, causing great damage to our mineral output. However, the residue contains valuable substances.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1717`

## gravity_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agravity_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$gravity_storm$引发的重力水平的波动使建筑工程变得困难，但它们确实为引力研究提供了独特的科学洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1490`

### English

While fluctuating gravity levels from the $gravity_storm$ have made building difficult, they do provide unique insights into gravity and space-time.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1727`

## magnetic_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagnetic_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$magnetic_storm$残留的磁场波动导致了许多大气光学现象。尽管景观美轮美奂，但这对导航设备而言是一场灾难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1499`

### English

The magnetic disturbances left by the $magnetic_storm$ cause a brilliant display of atmospheric lights. Remnant magnetic fields also impact the speed of planetary construction projects.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1737`

## solar_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asolar_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$solar_storm$造成的高水平辐射使得我们不得不进行几乎无限期的封控。由于所有人的生活空间都受到了限制，犯罪反倒是受到了打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1508`

### English

High levels of radiation caused by the $solar_storm$ necessitate near permanent lockdown. Given the heavily controlled living spaces, armed control of the planet has become significantly easier at a cost of a despondent population.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1747`

## celestial_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acelestial_storm_aftermath_modifier_severity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然由稀有矿物组成，但$celestial_storm$带来星际尘埃的无情冲击一直渗透到了矿山的最深处，阻碍了生产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1517`

### English

Although composed of rare materials, the relentless onslaught of interstellar dust from the $celestial_storm$ has seeped all the way down into the soil, impeding production.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1757`

### 中文

最近的$shroud_storm$引发了关于奇怪的目击和事件的报道。在余波之中，有些人得到了启示，另一些人则陷入了疯狂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1526`

### English

The recent $shroud_storm$ has precipitated reports of strange sightings and occurrences. In the aftermath, some have found enlightenment, others madness.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1767`

## TEMPEST_INVOCATOR_VIEW_BODY

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATEMPEST_INVOCATOR_VIEW_BODY&lang=en)

### 中文

该装置即使处于关闭状态，其中蕴含的能量也是显而易见的。如果我们真有勇气使用它，就可以获得驾驭风暴的力量。\n\n选择一个位置产生一场$nexus_storm_color_tooltip$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1542`

### English

The waiting potential of this device is tangible even in a powered-down state. Should we have the courage to use it, we would harness the power of the storms themselves.\n\nSelect a location to spawn a $nexus_storm_color_tooltip$.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1786`

### English

Tutorial: Storm Manipulation

## cstorms_tutorial.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms_tutorial.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以根据自身利益来§S操控§!某个§Y$default_storm$§!的§S移动路线§!。可以将风暴§S驱斥§!远离我方恒星系，或是§S吸引§!风暴进而利用其正面效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1546`

### English

We can §Smanipulate§! the §Spath§! of a §Y$default_storm$§! for our own benefit. Either to §Sdeflect§! them away from our systems, or §Sattract§! them to utilize their positive effects.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1792`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1547`

### English

Tutorial: Weather Forecast

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1794`

## TUTORIAL_STORM_FORECAST_MAP_MODE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_STORM_FORECAST_MAP_MODE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

该地图可视化地展现了星系中所有的§S宇宙风暴§!，以及关于其产生的效果的信息。\n\n£storm_forecast_map£\n\n单击右下角的地图图标即可进入天气预报地图模式。\n\n随着科技的提升，该模式将不仅能展示§S$default_storm$§!的位置和方向，还能以越来越高的准确度进行预测，此外还能显示预期的风暴类型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1548`

### English

This map mode visualizes any §SCosmic Storms§! in the galaxy, with information on their effects.\n\n£storm_forecast_map£\n\nTo access the weather forecast map mode, click the map icon in the bottom right.\n\nWith advanced technology, it can be upgraded to give increasingly accurate predictions not only of the location and direction of a §S$default_storm$§!, but also what type of storm to expect.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1795`

### 中文

### English

## TUTORIAL_STORM_PROTECTION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_STORM_PROTECTION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S宇宙风暴§!是一股强大的自然力量，能够对我方一些舰船，经济，甚至是人口带来毁灭性影响。然而，宇宙风暴也会带来一些不错的机遇。\n\n研究§S风暴科技§!能够解锁一些显著§S降低§!风暴负面§S影响§!的建筑与技术。§S进一步的研究§!甚至能够让我们§S开发利用§!风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1552`

### English

§SCosmic Storms§! are a formidable force of nature. They can have devastating effects on our fleets, economy, and even our population. However, they can also provide great opportunities.\n\nResearching §SStorm Technology§! provides us with buildings and tech to greatly §Sreduce§! the negative §Seffects§! of a storm. With §Sadditional research§! should even be able to take §Sadvantage§! of the storms.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1800`

## MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

## MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_STORM_INTRO&lang=en)

### 中文

这类风暴会对所有殖民地造成破坏，并妨碍我们的舰队进行紧急超光速。强烈建议采取预防性措施。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1578`

### English

This storm will cause devastating effects to colonies and hamper our fleets' access to emergency FTL. Taking preemptive measures is strongly advised.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1834`

## MESSAGE_ELECTRIC_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_ELECTRIC_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$风暴所携带的极端电荷会对我们舰队的护盾造成伤害。但由于风暴中所蕴含的丰沛能量，我们预期殖民地的产出和舰船速度会有显著提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1579`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The extreme electrical charges carried by the storm will present a danger to our fleets' shield systems. However, we are expecting a significant increase in colony production and ship speed due to the abundant energy present in the storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1836`

## MESSAGE_SOLAR_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_SOLAR_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$这场风暴中的辐射将会使陷入其中的舰队无法对能扫描风暴者隐蔽身形。尽管这会带来一些安全问题，但我们的空间站在这种条件下工作效率大幅提高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1580`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The radiation from this storm will make fleets caught inside visible to anyone who has the storm on their scanners. While this presents some security risks, our stations will operate much more efficiently under these conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1838`

## MESSAGE_CELESTIAL_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_CELESTIAL_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$这类风暴会阻碍我们的传感器，让我们无法探测这些星系内的其他舰队，并且拖慢太空旅行。与这些风险相对，我们也应该能积极利用这些条件，在风暴过境的过程中提升特定岗位的产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1581`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$This type of storm will jam our sensors, making us unable to detect other fleets within these systems and slow down space travel. Despite these risks, we should be able to use these conditions to our advantage and increase the output of certain jobs throughout the duration of the storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1840`

## MESSAGE_NEXUS_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_NEXUS_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$此类风暴对我们的殖民地、舰队和空间站有极高的危险。当风暴过境时，它们会受到严重的损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1582`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$This type of storm poses an extreme risk to our colonies, fleets, and stations. They will be taking heavy damage until it passes.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1842`

## MESSAGE_SHROUD_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_SHROUD_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$该风暴中高浓度的灵能能量将会显著降低我们殖民地的稳定度，但会有助于舰队闪避的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1583`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The heavy concentration of psionic energy in this storm will significantly reduce stability in our colonies, but aid in the effectiveness of fleet evasion.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1844`

## MESSAGE_SHROUD_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_SHROUD_BREACHED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_SHROUD_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_SHROUD_BREACHED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$该风暴中高浓度的灵能能量将会显著降低我们殖民地的稳定度，但我们关于在虚境中导航的知识会提高我们舰队和心灵感应的效率。\n\n虚境正在搅动着……我们必须做好一切准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1584`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The heavy concentration of psionic energy in this storm will significantly reduce stability in our colonies, but our knowledge in navigating the Shroud will aid us in the effectiveness of our fleets and Telepaths.\n\nThe Shroud stirs... We must be prepared for anything.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1846`

## MESSAGE_GRAVITY_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_GRAVITY_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$该风暴强大的重力影响会对我们的舰队行动造成越来越大的困难，舰船尺寸越大面临的风险也越高。\n\n尽管存在这些问题，我们的殖民地则可以积极利用该极端环境，提升我们特定任务的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1585`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The heavy gravitational influences of this storm will inflict progressive difficulty to fleet operations, with our larger ships at the highest risk.\n\nDespite these issues, our colonies will be able to use this extreme environment to our advantage, enhancing the effectiveness of certain tasks.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1848`

## MESSAGE_MAGNETIC_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_MAGNETIC_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$该风暴极端的磁场会让我们的舰队和建筑的维护系统疲于奔命。\n\n风暴的磁力与我们先进的冶金技术产生了意想不到的协同作用，这应该会在整场星系事件的过程中降低与我们冶金师有关的花费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1586`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The extreme magnetic forces of this storm will place our fleet and structural maintenance systems in disarray.\n\nThe storm's magnetic forces have created an unexpected synergy with our advanced metallurgical techniques. This interaction should reduce the costs associated with our Metallurgists throughout the duration of this galactic event.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1850`

## MESSAGE_PARTICLE_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_PARTICLE_STORM_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$这场风暴中高密度的微粒碎片的将对舰船和空间站造成毁灭性的影响，使它们的护盾完全失效。敢于在风暴内逗留的舰队应该做好应对高危环境的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1587`

### English

$MESSAGE_COSMIC_STORM_SPOTTED_DESC$$MESSAGE_STORM_INTRO$The density of the particulate debris in this storm will inflict devastating effects on ships and stations, negating their shield regeneration entirely. Those fleets brave enough to remain inside should prepare for a highly dangerous environment.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1852`

## pm_magnetized_planetary_core_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apm_magnetized_planetary_core_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

地幔深处的电磁活动使得磁性采矿成为可能，但同时也破坏了技术设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1634`

### English

Electromagnetic activity in the deep mantle makes magnetic mining viable while also disrupting technical infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1910`

## cstorms.1705.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1705.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$pm_magnetized_planetary_core$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1635`

### English

$pm_magnetized_planetary_core$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1911`

## cstorms.1705.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1705.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据报道，在最近的§Y$magnetic_storm$§!之后，[this.GetName]出现了不寻常的后遗症。风暴的强大能量场增加了行星熔融核心的磁场波动，导致科技基础设施普遍失灵。\n\n然而，这种情况也提供了一个绝无仅有的资源开采机会。地幔下富含金属的熔岩流被带上了电荷，可以在采矿磁铁的帮助下收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1636`

### English

Unusual after effects are being reported on [this.GetName] in the wake of the recent §Y$magnetic_storm$§!. The storm's potent energy fields increased magnetic volatility in the planet's molten core, resulting in the widespread failure of technological infrastructure.\n\nHowever, the situation also presents a unique resource extraction opportunity. Metal-rich lava flows underneath the planet's mantle are now electromagnetically charged and can be harvested with the aid of mining magnets.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1912`

## cstorms.1715.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1715.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在最近的§Y$electric_storm$§!后，[this.GetName]上观察到了一种异常现象。该行星的[GetOceansState]现已充斥着永久性的电流，电流在广阔的水体中循环，对水生生物构成致命威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1643`

### English

An unusual phenomenon has been observed on [this.GetName] in the wake of the recent §Y$electric_storm$§!. The planet's [GetOceansState] have been left charged with a perpetual electrical current now circulating in this vast body of water, posing lethal risks for aquatic life.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1920`

## cstorms.1715.desc.aquatic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1715.desc.aquatic&lang=en)

### 中文

这对我们殖民地的生存前景构成严重威胁，必须立即加以解决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1644`

### English

This represents a serious threat for our colony's survival prospects and must be addressed immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1921`

## cstorms.1715.desc.non_aquatic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1715.desc.non_aquatic&lang=en)

### 中文

这种现象对我们没有造成直接的伤害，但对我们殖民地的未来发展产生了挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1645`

### English

This phenomenon is causing us no direct harm but poses challenges to our colony's future development.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1922`

## d_ghostly_canyon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_ghostly_canyon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

## cstorms.1725.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1725.name&lang=en)

### 中文

鬼影重重

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1651`

### English

Ghostly Apparitions

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1929`

## cstorms.1725.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1725.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在最近[this.solar_system.GetName]的§Y$shroud_storm$§!事件后，[owner.GetColonistPlural]报告称在某个孤立的峡谷中发现了难以解释的神秘现象。幽灵般的蓝色身影短暂出现，似乎无法或不愿与我们的[owner.GetColonistPlural]互动，只是漫无目的地徘徊，随后消失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1652`

### English

Following the recent §Y$shroud_storm$§! in [this.solar_system.GetName], [owner.GetColonistPlural] report inexplicable sightings of ethereal apparitions in an isolated canyon. These ghostly blue figures appear briefly, unable or unwilling to interact with our [owner.GetColonistPlural], wandering aimlessly before dissipating.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1930`

## cstorms.1725.desc.psionic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1725.desc.psionic&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然这些幽灵在本质上似乎是灵能体，但我们仍无法与这些实体建立任何数量的精神交流。然而，我们发现他们的存在是对我们心灵感应能力的被动强化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1653`

### English

While the apparitions appear to be psionic in nature, we have not been able to establish any amount of mental communication with these entities. However, we have found their presence to be a passive reinforcement to our own telepathic abilities.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1931`

## cstorms.1725.desc.psionic_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1725.desc.psionic_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然这些幽灵在本质上似乎是灵能体，但我们仍无法与他们建立任何灵能交流。然而，系统检查倒是能确认他们的出现给我们的心灵感应能力带来了积极的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1654`

### English

While the apparitions appear to be psionic in nature, we have not been able to establish any psionic communication with these entities. However, system checks have confirmed that their presence provides passive reinforcement to our own telepathic abilities.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1932`

## cstorms.1725.desc.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1725.desc.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

这一非凡现象提醒我们，我们的生存中有着无形的奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1655`

### English

This remarkable phenomenon is providing a welcome reminder of the intangible wonders of our existence.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1933`

## cstorms.1735.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1735.name&lang=en)

### 中文

死火山

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1660`

### English

Volcanic Inactivity

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1939`

## cstorms.1735.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1735.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的§H[this.GetAffectedStormType]§!残留的辐射导致§Y[this.GetName]§!上的地质活动变得异常。\n\n尤其值得注意的是，风暴的影响使得一座活火山不再活跃，将其与地质学上的热点隔离，切断了岩浆供应。\n\n由于冷却的岩浆开拓了新的土地，进一步开发这颗行星的新机遇因这些变化而呈现于我们面前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1661`

### English

The residual radiation from the recent §Y[this.GetAffectedStormType]§! has led to erratic geological activity across §H[this.GetName]§!.\n\nMost notably, the impact of the storm has rendered an active volcano extinct, cutting it off from its geological hotspot and severing the supply of magma.\n\nThese changes present us with new opportunities to develop the planet further, as the cooling magma opens up new lands.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1940`

## d_sky_mountain

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_sky_mountain&lang=en)

### English

Mountains in the Sky

## d_sky_mountain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_sky_mountain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座巨大的山脉悬挂在行星上层大气的稀薄空气中，暴露出崭新的矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1664`

### English

A colossal mountain range hangs suspended in the thin air of the planet's upper atmosphere, exposing fresh ore deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1944`

## cstorms.1745.name

$d_sky_mountain$

### English

## cstorms.1745.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1745.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场严重的§Y$gravity_storm$§!导致了§H[this.GetName]§!的地景发生了剧变。这场风暴强劲的重力波动将行星最高的山脉从构造架上剥离，径直提到了星球的上方。\n\n它现在仍然悬浮在上层大气中，由风暴的残余引力作用保持在高空。这一剧变使得地下基岩中以前无法开采的矿物贮藏暴露了出来，也为[owner.GetResearchers]提供了很多可以分析的资料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1667`

### English

A severe §Y$gravity_storm$§! caused an extraordinary transformation of §H[this.GetName]§!'s landscape. The storm's powerful gravitational fluctuations severed the planet's largest mountain range from its tectonic shelf, lifting it vertically above the planet.\n\nIt remains suspended in the upper atmosphere, held aloft by a residual gravitational effect of the storm. This remarkable upheaval has exposed previously inaccessible ore reserves in the bedrock underneath, as well as providing [owner.GetResearchers] with much to analyze.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1947`

## cstorms.1755.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.name&lang=en)

### 中文

融化冰川

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1669`

### English

Melting Glaciers

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1950`

## cstorms.1755.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

近期的§Y[this.GetAffectedStormType]§!对[this.GetName]的气候产生了意想不到的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1670`

### English

The recent §Y[this.GetAffectedStormType]§! has had an unexpected impact on the climate of [this.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1951`

## cstorms.1755.desc.electric

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.electric&lang=en)

### 中文

大气中积聚的电荷导致了两极地区的剧烈温度变化。$cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1671`

### English

The increased electrical charge in the atmosphere has led to a drastic temperature shift in the polar region, $cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1952`

## cstorms.1755.desc.particle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.particle&lang=en)

### 中文

大气中积聚的电荷导致了两极地区的剧烈温度变化。$cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1672`

### English

The corrosive effect of the storm's particles has heavily impacted the polar regions, $cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1953`

## cstorms.1755.desc.gravity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.gravity&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴带来的重力变化将许多水下冰层撕裂，$cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1673`

### English

The gravitational shifts brought on by the storm have shattered multiple layers of subaquatic ice, $cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1954`

## cstorms.1755.desc.magnetic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.magnetic&lang=en)

### 中文

产生的行星磁场扰动导致了剧烈的地震运动，$cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1674`

### English

The resulting disturbances in the planets magnetic fields have led to drastic seismic shifts, $cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1955`

## cstorms.1755.desc.solar

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.solar&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴的极端热量在其持续期间内提升了整颗行星的温度，$cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1675`

### English

The extreme heat of the storm raised the temperatures across the planet throughout its duration, $cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1956`

## cstorms.1755.desc.celestial

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.celestial&lang=en)

### 中文

高浓度超热星尘严重地影响了两极地区，$cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1676`

### English

Large concentrations of superheated celestial dust have heavily impacted the polar regions, $cstorms.1755.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1957`

## cstorms.1755.desc.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1755.desc.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

让以前无法到达的冻土带发生了解冻。\n\n现在我们可以开发这片新发现的土地了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1677`

### English

causing previously inaccessible frozen tundra to thaw.\n\nWe can now develop this newly-uncovered land.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1958`

## cstorms.1761.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1761.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙阻塞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1679`

### English

Cosmic Clogging

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1961`

## cstorms.1761.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1761.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[this.GetName]§!的[Owner.GetEngineerPlural]汇报§Y$celestial_storm$§!它对水过滤系统有不利影响。随着颗粒在星球地下水中沉淀，形成的矿浆堵塞了关键的基础设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1680`

### English

Our [Owner.GetEngineerPlural] on §H[this.GetName]§! report that the §Y$celestial_storm$§! is having adverse effects on water filtration systems. As the particles settle in the planet's groundwater, the resulting mineral slurry is clogging key infrastructure points.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1962`

## cstorms.1761.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1761.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

由此导致的水培故障严重影响了星球上的农业，也导致了安全饮用水的短缺。\n\n这个问题如果不加以处理，可能会对我们的供水造成长期的影响。幸运的是，我们的[owner.GetEngineerPlural]有信心他们可以创造更强大的过滤系统来解决这个问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1681`

### English

The resulting hydroponic failures are heavily impacting the planet's agriculture, as well as leading to shortages in safe drinkable water.\n\nThis problem could have long-lasting consequences on our water supply if not dealt with. Fortunately, our [owner.GetEngineerPlural] are confident they can create more robust filtration systems to address the issue.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1963`

## cstorms.1761.desc.lithoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1761.desc.lithoid&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究水培堵塞的过程中，我们的[Owner.GetColonistPlural]发现，矿化的浆液本身对石质生理学人口实际上既美味又有营养。在对殖民地的过滤系统进行了一些小调整之后，它们大量生产了一种新的季节性饮料，可以供应到风暴结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1682`

### English

While investigating the hydroponic blockages, our [Owner.GetColonistPlural] discovered that the mineralized slurry itself is actually both delicious and nutritious when consumed by those with lithoid physiology. After making a few minor adjustments to the colony's filtration systems they have mass-produced a new seasonal drink with which to wait out the storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1964`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1761.a&lang=en)

进行研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1683`

### English

Proceed.

## CLEAR_DUSTY_PIPES_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACLEAR_DUSTY_PIPES_PROJECT&lang=en)

清理管道

### English

Clear the Pipes

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1972`

## CLEAR_DUSTY_PIPES_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACLEAR_DUSTY_PIPES_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$celestial_storm$§!对§H[this.GetName]§!的影响导致了水过滤系统被许多不可溶的矿物泥浆阻塞了。我们必须改建出更强劲的过滤系统来解决这个问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1689`

### English

The impact of a §Y$celestial_storm$§! on §H[this.GetName]§! has left water filtration systems clogged with an insoluble mineral slurry. We must re-engineer a more robust filtration system to address the issue.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1973`

## cstorms.1765.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1765.name&lang=en)

### 中文

过滤器疏通

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1690`

### English

Filters Unclogged

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1975`

## cstorms.1765.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1765.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Owner.GetEngineerPlural]解决了§H[this.GetName]§!上的水培阻塞和用水短缺的问题。殖民地的水供应网络得到了升级，现在可以滤去$celestial_storm$所带来的复杂杂质。\n\n这些对用水基础设施的改进还可以用来提升[Owner.GetName]上的灌溉作业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1691`

### English

The [Owner.GetEngineerPlural] have dealt with the hydroponic blockages and water shortages on §H[this.GetName]§!. The colony's water supply network has been improved and can now filter out complex impurities such as those introduced by the $celestial_storm$.\n\nThese improvements to our water infrastructure can be repurposed to improve irrigation efforts across the [Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1976`

## cstorms.1771.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1771.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不飞之羽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1693`

### English

Flightless Fauna

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1979`

## cstorms.1771.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1771.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.GetName]上的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]们观察到§Y$gravity_storm$§!对当地野生鸟类带来了严重的影响。\n\n风暴带来的重力波动导致了异常的大气条件，让许多鸟类物种无法飞行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1694`

### English

The [Owner.GetScientistPlural] on [this.GetName] have observed harsh repercussions of the §Y$gravity_storm$§! on the local avian wildlife.\n\nThe gravitational fluctuations brought on by the storm are leading to erratic atmospheric conditions, rendering several species of avian fauna incapable of flight.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1980`

## cstorms.1771.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1771.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

这不仅使它们无法正常捕猎，也更容易受到捕食者攻击。\n\n有人担心，如果我们不加以干预，这些受影响的物种可能会被赶尽杀绝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1695`

### English

Not only is this preventing the creatures unable to hunt as normal, it is also leaving them vulnerable to predators.\n\nThere is concern that the afflicted species may be hunted to extinction if we do not intervene.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1981`

## cstorms.1771.desc.swarm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1771.desc.swarm&lang=en)

### 中文

真正的盛宴等待着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1696`

### English

A veritable feast awaits us.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1982`

## cstorms.1771.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1771.a&lang=en)

### 中文

区区猎物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1697`

### English

Easy Prey.

## d_avian_reserve_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_avian_reserve_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座隐蔽的自然保护区，为保护脆弱的鸟类而建。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1702`

### English

A secluded nature reserve built to house vulnerable species of avian fauna.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1989`

## cstorms.1776.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1776.name&lang=en)

### 中文

再次飞翔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1703`

### English

Airborne Again

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1990`

## cstorms.1776.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1776.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

紧接着§Y$gravity_storm$§!过境之后，[this.GetName]上暂时无法飞行的在我们的鸟类保护区内避难，又一次翱翔天空。\n\n虽然许多动物现在已经回到了它们以前的栖息地，但仍有相当数量的动物留在保护区，似乎在享受保护区提供的舒适和保护。\n\n这为近距离研究这颗星球上的野生鸟类提供了一个独特的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1704`

### English

Following the passing of the §Y$gravity_storm$§!, the temporarily flightless fauna of [this.GetName] have taken to the skies again having taken shelter inside our avian reserve.\n\nWhile many of the creatures have now returned to their former habitats, significant numbers remain in the reserve, seemingly enjoying the comforts and protections it provides.\n\nThis presents a unique opportunity to study the planet's avian wildlife at close proximity.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1991`

## cstorms.1776.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1776.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1705`

### English

Excellent News.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1992`

## cstorms.1801.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1801.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴之眼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1710`

### English

Eye of the Storm

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:1999`

## cstorms.1801.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1801.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描发现，在[from.GetName]中的[fromfrom.GetName]的中心出现了不规则现象，表明该构造的中心携带着一个奇怪的物质。这些不寻常的读数值得在风暴消散或离开该恒星系之前进行更深入的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1711`

### English

Scans detect irregularities in the eye of [fromfrom.GetName] raging in [from.GetName], indicating that the formation is carrying a strange mass at its center. These unusual readings warrant closer investigation, before the storm dissipates or leaves the system.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2000`

## EYE_OF_THE_STORM_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEYE_OF_THE_STORM_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

$cstorms.1801.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1712`

### English

$cstorms.1801.name$

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2001`

## EYE_OF_THE_STORM_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEYE_OF_THE_STORM_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描位于[this.GetName]中的[fromfrom.GetName]，结果表明其内部漩涡中携带着大量物质。远程传感器无法对这一不寻常现象提供确凿的答案。为了增进我们的认识，需要对风暴中心进行一次实地考察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1713`

### English

Scans of [fromfrom.GetName] in [this.GetName] suggest it is carrying a large mass within its inner vortex. Long-range sensors have been unable to provide conclusive answers about this unusual phenomenon. A field expedition into the eye of the storm is required to advance our understanding.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2002`

## cstorms.1805.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1805.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进入漩涡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1714`

### English

Into the Vortex

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2003`

## cstorms.1805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过进入[from.GetName]的宇宙风暴眼，[leader.GetName]发现了我们扫描不规则的原因。这场不寻常的风暴是由巨大的中心[from.star.GetEarnedResources]聚集所推动的。\n\n我们的科研船在被猛烈地弹射出去之前收获了可以收集的资源。传感器的数据将帮助我们的工程师在极端风暴条件下提高船只的性能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1715`

### English

By navigating into the eye of the cosmic storm in [from.GetName], [leader.GetName] discovered the cause of our scan irregularities. This unusual storm is being fueled by a huge central concentration of [from.star.GetEarnedResources].\n\nOur science ship harvested what resources could be gathered before being violently ejected. The sensor data from their narrow withdrawal will aid our engineers in improving vessel performance under extreme storm conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2004`

## cstorms.1805.desc.traumatized

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1805.desc.traumatized&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，这种危险的折磨对[leader.GetName]产生了持久的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1716`

### English

Unfortunately, this dangerous ordeal has had a lasting impact on [leader.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2005`

## cstorms.1816.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1816.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂泊胶囊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1743`

### English

Drifting Capsule

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2035`

## cstorms.1816.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1816.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个神秘的太空舱飘在§H[from.GetName]§!中。它看起来没有任何攻击能力并且对我们的信号毫无反应。\n\n初步扫描显示它受损的外部是用先进的材料和陌生的工程科技建造的。需要进一步分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1744`

### English

A mysterious capsule is adrift in §H[from.GetName]§!. It has no obvious offensive capability and is unresponsive to our signals.\n\nInitial scans show that its damaged exterior is constructed using advanced materials and unfamiliar engineering techniques. Further analysis is needed.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2036`

## cstorms.1816.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1816.a&lang=en)

### 中文

调查它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1745`

### English

Investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2037`

## STORM_IN_A_BOX_INVESTIGATE_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORM_IN_A_BOX_INVESTIGATE_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

研究胶囊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1746`

### English

Study the Capsule

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2039`

## STORM_IN_A_BOX_INVESTIGATE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORM_IN_A_BOX_INVESTIGATE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一个明显受损的神秘胶囊正在飘过§H[this.GetName]§!星系。我们不知道它的起源，也对其功能一无所知。它看起来不具备明显的武器，我们相信对其先进的建造工艺进行分析是一个安全和值得投入的项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1747`

### English

A mysterious and visibly-damaged capsule is drifting through the §H[this.GetName]§! system. We know neither its origin nor function. It possesses no evident weaponry and we believe further analysis of its advanced construction would be a safe and worthwhile venture.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2040`

## cstorms.1820.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1820.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴陷阱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1748`

### English

Storm Trap

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2042`

## cstorms.1820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.leader.GetName]相信飘过[solar_system.GetName]星系的神秘太空舱是一种古老的“风暴陷阱”，可能曾是一个更大的防御系统的一部分。\n\n这个设备的进气口密封得很紧，但我们的扫描显示它里面藏有被俘获其中的高度压缩的宇宙风暴能量和物质。\n\n研究如何这个非凡的容器可能会教会我们很多关于抑制风暴的知识，但也有释放其蕴含的所有能量的风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1749`

### English

[this.leader.GetName] believes that the mysterious capsule drifting through the [solar_system.GetName] system is an ancient "storm trap", which may have once been part of a larger defense grid.\n\nThe device's intake port is sealed tightly, but our readings suggest it is holding the compressed energy and matter of a captive cosmic storm inside.\n\nFinding a way to open this remarkable container may teach us a great deal about storm mitigation, although at the risk of releasing whatever forces are currently contained within.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2043`

## cstorms.1820.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1820.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这里有很多值得学习的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1750`

### English

There is much to learn here.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2044`

### English

## STORM_IN_A_BOX_TRAP_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORM_IN_A_BOX_TRAP_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

打开风暴陷阱

### English

Open the Storm Trap

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2047`

## STORM_IN_A_BOX_TRAP_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTORM_IN_A_BOX_TRAP_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一个古老的“风暴陷阱”在太空中自由漂流，内部携带着压缩的宇宙风暴。打开它将为风暴管理和缓解提供宝贵的洞见，但代价是释放被困在里面的未知力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1753`

### English

An ancient "storm trap" is drifting freely through space, carrying a compressed cosmic storm within. Opening it would provide valuable insights into storm management and mitigation, at the risk of releasing whatever forces are trapped inside.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2048`

## cstorms.1826.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1826.name&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴释放

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1754`

### English

Storm Released

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2050`

## cstorms.1826.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1826.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[leader.GetName]完成了对古老风暴陷阱工作原理的分析，并解开了其输入装置。随着端口打开，§H[from.GetStormType]§!内部的风暴被释放到[solar_system.GetName]星系的虚空中。\n\n对陷阱复杂工程的研究已经对抗风暴材料和结构产生了惊人的洞见，尽管我们仍然不明白这个装置是如何能够完全控制风暴的。这些知识将极大地帮助我们抵御未来的宇宙扰动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1755`

### English

[leader.GetName] completed analysis of the ancient storm trap's workings and was able to unlock its intake mechanism. As the ports opened, the §H[from.GetStormType]§! held inside was released into the void of the [solar_system.GetName] system.\n\nStudy of the trap's complex engineering has produced fascinating insights into storm-resistant materials and structures, though we still do not understand how the device is capable of containing storms entirely. This knowledge will greatly aid us in weathering future cosmic disturbances.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2051`

## cstorms.1826.storm_tt

[solar_system.GetName]星系中诞生了一场小规模的低等级§Y$default_storm$§!

### English

A small, low-level §Y$default_storm$§! spawns in the [solar_system.GetName] system

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2052`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1758`

### English

## cstorms.1835.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.1835.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，我们深表同情！我们目睹了一场大风暴在[this.GetName]肆虐。\n\n听到那里遭到的破坏，我们当然感到非常难过。我们哭了，我们真的哭了！所以，请允许我们帮个忙……这是一笔特别的一次性超级交易！\n\n我们可以提供一些小工具，一流的小工具。是我们能给的最好的！有了这些，当下一次风暴来临时，你就能做好准备了。那么我们成交吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1759`

### English

Commiserations, [this.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! We chanced to see a big storm wreaking havoc in [this.GetName].\n\nOf course, we were very sad to hear of the damage done there. We cried, indeed we cried! So please, allow us to help... with a special one time super deal!\n\nWe can provide gadgets, first class gadgets. Only the best from us! With these, when the next storm comes, you will be ready. So do we have a deal?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2056`

## cstorms.2000.desc_common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2000.desc_common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在正在§H[target.solar_system.GetName]§!星系严阵以待，开始着手进行制造一场§Y$default_storm$§!的必要准备。\n\n通过暂时过载[target.GetName]，我们就可以为播种出所想要的星际气象学反应提供必要条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1818`

### English

We are now in position in the §H[target.solar_system.GetName]§! system and ready to begin the necessary preparations to generate a §Y$default_storm$§!.\n\nBy temporarily supercharging [target.GetName], we can create the ideal conditions to seed the desired astrometeorological reaction.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2122`

## cstorms.2000.desc_tech

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2000.desc_tech&lang=en)

### 中文

\n使用先进的能量操纵技术，我们可以用原材料使[target.GetName]过饱和来形成结构上更复杂的风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1819`

### English

\nUsing advanced energy manipulation methods, we could oversaturate [target.GetName] with raw materials to form a more structurally complex storm.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2123`

## cstorms.2000.desc_common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2000.desc_common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

\n这会是一笔时间成本不菲的投资，但其结果会是[owner.GetName]的巨大成就。在行动结束后，恒星本身将会恢复原状。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1820`

### English

\nThis will be a time-consuming endeavor, but the end result will be a great achievement for [owner.GetName]. The star itself will remain entirely intact after the operation is complete.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2124`

## cstorms.2010.desc_irradiate_only

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2010.desc_irradiate_only&lang=en)

### 中文

对§H[target.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中$default_storm$的准备已经到了关键阶段，下一步行动需要高度专注以及更多资源投入，将通过操纵§H[target.GetName]§!的恒星风来形成§Y$solar_storm$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1828`

### English

The preparations towards the $default_storm$ in the §H[target.solar_system.GetName]§! system have reached a pivotal stage. The next actions require intense focus and new resource commitments to forge a §Y$solar_storm$§! by manipulating the solar winds of §H[target.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2133`

## cstorms.2010.desc_others

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2010.desc_others&lang=en)

对§H[target.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中$default_storm$的准备已经到了关键时刻，下一步行动非常关键，需要对这一项目做出更集中的资源承诺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1829`

### English

The preparations towards the $default_storm$ in the §H[target.solar_system.GetName]§! system have reached a critical juncture. The next actions are crucial, and require more focused resource commitments to the cause.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2134`

## cstorms.2015.desc_one_tech_only

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2015.desc_one_tech_only&lang=en)

### 中文

对§H[target.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中$default_storm$的准备已经到了关键阶段，[storm_researcher.GetName]的下一步行动需要高度专注以及更多资源投入，并将从[target.GetName]的膨胀状态受益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1838`

### English

The preparations towards the $default_storm$ in the §H[target.solar_system.GetName]§! system have reached a pivotal stage. The next actions of [storm_researcher.GetName] require intense focus and new resource commitments to take advantage of the bloated state of [target.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2144`

## cstorms.2015.desc_others

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.2015.desc_others&lang=en)

### 中文

对§H[target.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中$default_storm$的准备已经到了关键时刻，[storm_researcher.GetName]的下一步行动非常关键，如果我们要从[target.GetName]的碰撞状态中获取优势，就需要对这一项目做出更集中的资源承诺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1839`

### English

The preparations towards the $default_storm$ in the §H[target.solar_system.GetName]§! system have reached a critical juncture. The next actions of [storm_researcher.GetName] are crucial, and require more focused resource commitments to the cause, if we are to properly take advantage of the bloated of state of [target.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2145`

强化的§H[target.GetName]§!恒星风终于到达了关键质量。\n\n一场§Y$solar_storm$§!正如弦上之箭，弹手可发。\n\n$cstorms.storm_gathered.final_word$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1850`

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2157`

## d_magnetic_storm_1_minerals

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_magnetic_storm_1_minerals&lang=en)

### 中文

磁化矿石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1862`

### English

Magnetized Ore

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2172`

## d_magnetic_storm_1_minerals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_magnetic_storm_1_minerals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴磁化了大量以前未开发的矿物矿藏，使我们的采矿作业非常容易获取这些新资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1863`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has magnetized an immense amount of previously unexploited mineral deposits, making it exceedingly easy for our mining operations to access these new resources.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2173`

## d_magnetic_storm_2_alloys

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_magnetic_storm_2_alloys&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴铸造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1864`

### English

Storm-Forged Foundry

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2174`

## d_magnetic_storm_2_alloys_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_magnetic_storm_2_alloys_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴蹂躏了这个行星，使得大量的铸造设备莫名其妙地带电。即使我们没有完全了解其机理，我们的合金产量也有了显著提高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1865`

### English

A passing cosmic storm that ravaged this planet has left a significant number of foundry equipment strangely charged. Even without fully understanding the mechanism, our alloy production has improved significantly.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2175`

## d_magnetic_storm_3_mix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_magnetic_storm_3_mix&lang=en)

### 中文

天翔高原

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1866`

### English

Skybound Plateaus

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2176`

## d_magnetic_storm_3_mix_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_magnetic_storm_3_mix_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经过的宇宙风暴的引力作用下，雄伟而漂浮的陆地可以直接移动到现有的工业园区上方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1867`

### English

Magnetized by the gravitational effects of a passing cosmic storm, the majestic and floating landmasses can be maneuvered directly over existing industrial complexes.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2177`

## cstorms.8000.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8000.title&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙调整

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1868`

### English

Cosmic Alteration

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2179`

## cstorms.8000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

过境的宇宙风暴留下的不仅仅是毁灭。在[Planet.GetName]上，它还创造了一个全新的地理特征。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1869`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has left more than mere devastation in its wake. On [Planet.GetName], it has created an entirely new geographical feature.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2180`

## cstorms.8000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8000.a&lang=en)

很迷人

### English

## d_electric_storm_1_engineering

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_electric_storm_1_engineering&lang=en)

### 中文

雷击废墟

### English

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2183`

## d_electric_storm_1_engineering_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_electric_storm_1_engineering_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场令人难以置信的狂暴宇宙风暴将一个曾经繁荣的定居点变成了雷击的废墟，将我们平凡的建筑材料变成了稀有而不寻常的化合物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1872`

### English

A passing cosmic storm of incredible fury has transformed a once-thriving settlement into lightning-struck ruins, transmuting our mundane building materials into rare and unusual compounds.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2184`

## d_electric_storm_3_physics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_electric_storm_3_physics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴对无数民用船只的导航系统造成了严重破坏，将受灾最严重的地区变成了闹鬼的墓地，到处是破碎的船体和被摧毁的机器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1876`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has wreaked havoc on the navigation systems of countless civilian ships, turning the hardest-hit areas into haunting graveyards of broken hulks and decimated machinery.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2188`

## d_particle_storm_1_society_desc

宇宙风暴把我们的一些郊区变成了废墟，仅存残骸用于研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1878`

## d_particle_storm_2_unity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_particle_storm_2_unity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴在这个星球上留下了一个巨大的撞击坑，残酷地提醒着宇宙对我们的存在漠不关心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1880`

### English

The Cosmic Storm has left a tremendous crater on this world, a brutal reminder of cosmic indifference to our existence.

## d_particle_storm_3_motes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_particle_storm_3_motes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴把几片巨大的平原变成了构造高活动区，巧合的是，危险的增加也伴随着易爆微粒的增产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1882`

### English

The Cosmic Storm has turned several vast planes into areas of high tectonic activity, coincidentally the increase in danger has also come with an increase in volatile motes.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2195`

退化的臭氧层

### English

Degraded Ozone Layer

## d_solar_storm_1_energy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_solar_storm_1_energy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴将这颗行星大气的臭氧层上撕开了一个洞。严重影响我们生活质量，但提高了生产力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1887`

### English

The Cosmic Storm has torn a hole into the ozone layer of this planets atmosphere. While severely infringing on our quality of life, the quality of production has gone up.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2202`

## d_solar_storm_2_districts

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_solar_storm_2_districts&lang=en)

### 中文

辐射高地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1888`

### English

Radiated Highlands

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2203`

## d_solar_storm_2_districts_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_solar_storm_2_districts_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴用辐射洗涤了这个星球的高地，为我们的能源生产创造了一片升华之地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1889`

### English

The Cosmic Storm has bathed the highlands of this world with radiation, creating a sublime ground for our energy production.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2204`

## d_solar_storm_3_output

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_solar_storm_3_output&lang=en)

### 中文

发光沙漠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1890`

### English

Glowing Desert

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2205`

## d_solar_storm_3_output_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_solar_storm_3_output_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙风暴使整个沙漠发光，这是一种致命的景象，但如果有足够的安全措施，它就可以变成一个经济上可行的旅游景点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1891`

### English

The Cosmic Storm has left entire desert aglow, a deadly view but with sufficient security measures it can turned into a financially viable tourist spot.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2206`

## d_celestial_storm_1_mines

羽毛状水晶矿
