### English

Crystalline Quills

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2208`

## d_celestial_storm_1_mines_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_celestial_storm_1_mines_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场经过的宇宙风暴的破坏性爆炸留下了锯齿状和尖刺晶体的景观，创造了一个危险但潜在利润丰厚的领域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1893`

### English

The ravaging blasts of a passing cosmic storm have left behind a landscape of jagged and spiked crystals, creating a perilous yet potentially profitable terrain.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2209`

## d_celestial_storm_2_society

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_celestial_storm_2_society&lang=en)

### 中文

史前沉淀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1894`

### English

Historic Sedimentation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2210`

## d_celestial_storm_2_society_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_celestial_storm_2_society_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴将整个沉积层炸飞，揭开了我们以前从未研究过的万年痕迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1895`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has blasted away entire layers of sediment, unveiling traces of eons we have never before been able to study.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2211`

## d_celestial_storm_3_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_celestial_storm_3_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

星尘沙丘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1896`

### English

Stardust Dunes

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2212`

## d_celestial_storm_3_crystal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_celestial_storm_3_crystal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场转瞬即逝的宇宙风暴像巨浪一样席卷了这个世界。在它的尾迹中留下了一个闪闪发光的星尘沙丘的空灵景观。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1897`

### English

A passing cosmic storm swept over this world like a great wave. In its wake, it left behind an ethereal landscape of glittering, stardust dunes.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2213`

## d_gravity_storm_1_districts

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_gravity_storm_1_districts&lang=en)

### 中文

压实的平原

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1901`

### English

Compacted Plains

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2219`

## d_gravity_storm_1_districts_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_gravity_storm_1_districts_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴的毁灭性引力效应已经压缩了几个以前被认为太不稳定而无法建设的地区，有效地给予我们免费的土地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1902`

### English

The crushing gravitational effects of a passing cosmic storm have compacted several areas previously deemed too unstable for construction, effectively granting us free real estate.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2220`

## d_gravity_storm_2_unity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_gravity_storm_2_unity&lang=en)

### 中文

粉碎纪念碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1903`

### English

Pulverized Monuments

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2221`

## d_gravity_storm_2_unity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_gravity_storm_2_unity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场经过的宇宙风暴对我们一些比较脆弱的纪念碑施加了巨大的压力，使它们化为碎片与尘埃——这是一个谦逊的提醒，提醒着有可怕的力量在等待着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1904`

### English

A passing cosmic storm unleashed tremendous pressure on some of our more fragile monuments, reducing them to dust and debris - a humble reminder of the terrifying forces that await us out there.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2222`

## d_gravity_storm_3_engineering

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_gravity_storm_3_engineering&lang=en)

### 中文

重力洞穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1905`

### English

Gravitational Caves

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2223`

## d_gravity_storm_3_engineering_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_gravity_storm_3_engineering_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴把我们的一些采矿设施变成了破碎的超致密材料竖井。虽然我们重新开采这些矿山的尝试带来了工程和钻探方面的新理论，但在回收这些矿山方面进展甚微。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1906`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has transformed some of our mining complexes into broken shafts of hyper-compact material. While our attempts to reopen them have sparked new theories in engineering and drilling, they have made little progress in reclaiming the mines.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2224`

## d_shroud_storm_1_society

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shroud_storm_1_society&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境寄生虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1907`

### English

Shroud Parasites

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2226`

## d_shroud_storm_1_society_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shroud_storm_1_society_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场宇宙风暴在我们的牲口中传播了寄生虫。它们在我们的现实世界和虚境之间相位变换，同时它们被证实是可食用的、健康的，而且意外地很美味。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1908`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has spread parasites among our livestock. Phasing between our reality and the Shroud, they have proven to be edible, healthy and surprisingly delicious.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2227`

## d_shroud_storm_2_zro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shroud_storm_2_zro&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1909`

### English

Fields of Zro

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2228`

## d_shroud_storm_2_zro_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shroud_storm_2_zro_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过一场短暂的宇宙风暴，这个行星上最翠绿的生物群落已经变成了摇摆不定的泽珞场。这些是我们所见过的最纯净的矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1910`

### English

Transformed by a passing cosmic storm, this planet's most verdant biomes have become wavering fields of Zro. These are some of the purest deposits we have ever encountered.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2229`

## d_shroud_storm_3_happy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shroud_storm_3_happy&lang=en)

### 中文

湖中实体

### English

Entity of the Lake

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2230`

## d_shroud_storm_3_happy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shroud_storm_3_happy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场过境的宇宙风暴给我们留下了一份令人惊奇的礼物。这颗星球上最大的水体泛起了涟漪，组织出了某种看起来很重要的图案和符号，并能够以惊人的智慧回答问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1912`

### English

A passing cosmic storm has left us with a strange and wonderful gift. The largest body of water on this planet forms ripples that create patterns and symbols of obvious importance, capable of answering inquiries with surprising wisdom.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2231`

## shroud_switched

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroud_switched&lang=en)

### English

## shroud_switched_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroud_switched_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境的帷幕覆盖在这个天体上，它们不自然的存在表明，这要么是一个深刻的破口，要么是一场灾难性虚境风暴的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1915`

### English

Veils of the Shroud linger upon this celestial body, their unnatural presence suggesting either a profound breach or the aftermath of a catastrophic Shroud Storm. 

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2235`

## cstorms.8510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一阵灵能风暴中，[from.GetName]被替成了来源不明的[from.GetClassName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1917`

### English

In a flurry of psionic activity, [from.GetName] was switched with a [from.GetClassName] from parts unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2238`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8510.a&lang=en)

### English

## cstorms.8515.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8515.title&lang=en)

### 中文

质量改造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1919`

### English

Mass Transfiguration

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2240`

## cstorms.8515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一股灵能浪潮席卷了[from.GetName]，使其部分人口发生了违背自然法则的改变。整个群体都被重塑了，它们的形态变得奇怪而陌生，仿佛有某种我们无法理解的东西改写了他们的本质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1920`

### English

A surge of psionic energy has swept through [from.GetName], leaving parts of its population altered in ways that defy natural law. Entire groups have been reshaped, their forms now alien and unfamiliar, as if something beyond our understanding has rewritten their very essence.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2241`

## cstorms.8515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8515.a&lang=en)

### 中文

未知的力量在扰动……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1921`

### English

Unknown forces stir...

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2242`

## cstorms.8520.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8520.title&lang=en)

### 中文

恒星演变

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1922`

### English

Stellar Transmutation

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2243`

## cstorms.8520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一股灵能浪潮重塑了[from.GetName]，时期变成了一颗具有截然不同性质的新恒星。如今，一颗[from.GetClassName]正向[from.solar_system.GetName]恒星系投射着它怪异的光，仿佛现实本身被扭曲了一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1923`

### English

A surge of psionic energy has remade [from.GetName], transforming it into a star of an entirely new nature. Now, a [from.GetClassName] casts its strange light upon the [from.solar_system.GetName] System, as if reality itself has been altered.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2244`

## cstorms.8520.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acstorms.8520.a&lang=en)

### 中文

即使是恒星也未能幸免……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1924`

### English

Even the stars are not immune...

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2245`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1939`

## job_sacred_noble_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_sacred_noble_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

予神性以血统方是正道。予血统以神性是手段。因此，我们的高贵是受到天佑的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1943`

### English

Divinity by Inheritance is the way. Inheritance by Divinity is the means. Thus we are blessed with nobility.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2270`

## job_augmentation_merchant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_augmentation_merchant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

能引导我们帝国的，除了这些以如此唾手可得实惠价格和最长的保修供应自我提升的人以外还有谁呢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1947`

### English

Who could possibly guide our empire but the ones who offer self-improvement at such an affordable cost and with an extended warranty on top.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2275`

## job_visionary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_visionary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不为先例所拘，也无谦卑之累，这些明日的梦想家将大胆的想象化为可售的现实。于真正的先驱而言，进步不仅是信条，更是商品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1951`

### English

Unfettered by precedent and unburdened by humility, these dreamers of tomorrow transmute bold speculation into marketable reality. To a true visionary, progress is not merely a principle; it is a product.

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2280`

## job_noble_merchant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_noble_merchant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

统治权并非由继承决定，而是由一个人的价值衡量；而衡量价值的最佳方式，难道不就是看资产负债表上的总额吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/cosmic_storms_l_simp_chinese.yml:1955`

### English

The right to rule is determined not by inheritance, but by the measure of one's worth; and how better to measure your worth than by the sum total of the balance sheet?

Source: `localisation/english/cosmic_storms_l_english.yml:2285`
